Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Cardiac magnetic resonance imaging (CMR) captures rich spatiotemporal information about ventricular structure and motion, but conventional risk models use only a few image-derived indices from selected cardiac phases. We present a latent dynamical model that encodes bi-ventricular anatomy and full-cycle cine motion as a continuous latent trajectory, using heart-rate-aware neural ordinary differential equation (ODE) dynamics and a graph-based mesh autoencoder to reconstruct anatomically consistent 3D+t ventricular motion. A covariate-conditioned prior defines the expected end-diastolic latent state, and a Cox proportional hazards model tests whether deviations from this prior predict incident heart failure. We studied 72,386 UK Biobank participants without baseline cardiovascular disease, including 367 incident heart failure events. In a held-out evaluation subset, adding the latent score to refitted pooled cohort equations improved the stratified C-index from 0.704 to 0.785, compared with 0.764 for seven established cardiac markers. Compared with non-graph and non-ODE approaches, the proposed model gave the best trade-off between reconstruction fidelity, generative realism, and downstream prognostic performance. These results suggest that continuous full-cycle modeling of ventricular motion provides informative cardiac phenotypes beyond conventional CMR summaries, while external validation in more representative patient cohorts is required before clinical risk-prediction use.

</details>


### [34] [Socratic agents for autonomous scientific discovery in high-dimensional physical systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26722)
*Xianrui Zeng,Pengfei Liu,Yirui Zang,Yang Shen,Fei Yu,Chunlei Yu,Minghao Liu,Yang Du*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AHOIS——一种嵌入苏格拉底式追问的多智能体AI科学家系统，在真实多模光纤光学平台上实现了自主科学发现。系统无需先验编码方案，自主提出并验证了随机干扰编码假设，发现了任务自适应稀疏测量策略，诊断了不同故障模式，并成功将已发表协议迁移到非原始配置上，实现了16×16测量、有效秩56.9、MNIST分类准确率76.97%和Fashion-MNIST 83.17%的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI科学发现系统本质上是程序化的——执行由人类设计者固定的工作流，缺乏真正的认知自主性。真正的自主科学需要"认知自主性"——即根据证据构建、质疑和修正物理解释的能力。本文旨在弥合流程自动化与真正自主发现之间的鸿沟。

Method: 提出AHOIS多智能体系统，核心创新在于将"苏格拉底式助产术"嵌入闭环实验循环中。系统包含一个"物理批评家"智能体，通过因果提问、约束检查、反例生成和证伪标准制定来质疑假设。在真实多模光纤平台上评估，该系统处理的是具有复杂波变换、间接检测、环境漂移和多模态采集的高维系统。

Result: 自主提出并验证了随机干扰编码假设；发现任务自适应稀疏测量策略；诊断出三种不同故障模式（编码不稳定、荧光污染、检测器噪声）；将已发表的成像协议迁移到非原始配置上。量化结果：16×16测量、有效秩56.9、MNIST 76.97%、Fashion-MNIST 83.17%。消融实验表明苏格拉底式追问改善了物理一致性、假设完备性、不确定性校准和实验计划有效性。

Conclusion: 从工作流自动化迈向基于证据、自我修正的自主科学发现是可行的。苏格拉底式追问机制是实现认知自主性的关键组件，为复杂物理环境中的自主科学奠定了路径基础。

Abstract: The automation of scientific discovery has reached an inflection point. While AI systems now operate instruments, optimize parameters and generate hypotheses, most remain procedural: they execute workflows fixed by human designers. True autonomous science demands epistemic autonomy--the capacity to construct, challenge and revise physical explanations in response to evidence. Here we introduce AHOIS, a multi-agent AI scientist that embeds Socratic midwifery into closed-loop experimentation. A physics-critic agent interrogates hypotheses through causal questioning, constraint checking, counterexample generation and falsification-criteria formulation. We evaluate AHOIS on a real multimode-fibre optical platform, a high-dimensional system with complex wave transformations, indirect detection, environmental drift and multi-modal acquisition. Without prior encoding schemes, classifiers or speckle models, the system autonomously proposed and validated a random-interference encoding hypothesis, discovered task-adaptive sparse-measurement strategies, diagnosed distinct failure modes (encoding instability, fluorescence contamination and detector noise) and translated a published imaging protocol into an executable workflow on a non-original configuration. The discovered encoding yielded 16x16 measurements with effective rank 56.9 and classification accuracies of 76.97% on MNIST and 83.17% on Fashion-MNIST. Ablations show that Socratic interrogation improves physical consistency, hypothesis completeness, uncertainty calibration and experimental-plan validity. These results establish a route from workflow automation towards evidence-grounded, self-correcting autonomous discovery in complex physical environments.

</details>


### [35] [Scientific discovery as meta-optimization: a combinatorial optimization case study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26728)
*Yuan-Hang Zhang,Chesson Sipling,Massimiliano Di Ventra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将科学研究形式化为元优化问题，即优化目标本身也在被优化。通过“共识目标聚合”方法，将LLM生成的目标函数通过相关性加权投票组合，形成稳定自修正的评估标准。在基于数字MemComputing的3-SAT算法发现中，将问题规模缩放从O(N²·⁵¹)降至O(N¹·³³)，最大实例加速约67倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学研究本质上是在庞大状态空间中搜索理论与实验的优化问题，但现有方法仅关注自动探索状态空间，而忽略了评估标准本身也需要同步优化。作者认为同时修改评估标准同等重要。

Method: 提出“共识目标聚合”（consensus objective aggregation）方法：利用LLM生成多个目标函数，通过相关性加权投票（correlation-weighted voting）将其组合，得到一个稳定、自修正的评估标准，该标准随理解深化而演化。

Result: 在数字MemComputing机器的3-SAT算法发现任务中，将基线的问题规模缩放指数从~N²·⁵¹降至~N¹·³³，在最大测试实例上实现了约67倍的加速。

Conclusion: 该框架是问题无关的（problem-agnostic），有望大幅辅助科学发现。

Abstract: Scientific discovery is fundamentally an optimization problem, defined by a vast "state space" of theories and experiments, and an evaluation criterion based on quality, novelty, and validity. Large language models (LLMs) have enabled automated exploration of this space, but we argue that simultaneous modification of the evaluation criteria is equally important. Here, we propose formalizing research as meta-optimization, where the optimization objective itself is also being optimized. Our key contribution is "consensus objective aggregation," where LLM-generated objective functions are combined via correlation-weighted voting, yielding a stable, self-correcting evaluation criterion that evolves as understanding deepens. We apply this framework to algorithm discovery for 3-SAT problems based on digital MemComputing machines, reducing the baseline scaling with problem size $N$ from $\sim N^{2.51}$ to $\sim N^{1.33}$ and delivering a $\sim 67\times$ speedup on the largest instances tested. As a problem-agnostic framework, we hope this approach will considerably aid scientific discovery.

</details>


### [36] [EGG: An Expert-Guided Agent Framework for Kernel Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26758)
*Yaochen Han,Ke Fan,Hongxu Jiang,Wanqi Xu,Weiyu Xie,Runhua Zhang,Chenhui Zhu,Yixiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出EGG框架，通过专家指导的两阶段分层策略（算法结构设计+硬件特定调优）和阶段感知的多智能体协作机制，实现LLM GPU内核的高效自动生成，在KernelBench上相比PyTorch平均加速2.13倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高性能GPU内核开发依赖人工专家调优成本高昂，现有LLM方法虽可自动化生成内核但难以兼顾正确性与高性能，根本原因在于缺乏领域特定的优化指导，无法有效探索优化空间。

Method: 提出EGG框架，将内核生成分解为两个分层阶段：1) 算法结构设计阶段建立高质量计算结构基础；2) 硬件特定调优阶段通过并行映射、张量分块和内存优化进行针对性调整。设计阶段感知的多智能体协作机制实现阶段内外上下文管理，确保稳定优化轨迹。

Result: 在KernelBench和实际工作负载上，EGG相比PyTorch平均加速2.13倍，优于现有的基于智能体和基于强化学习的方法。

Conclusion: 引入专家优化原则指导LLM决策的分层分解策略能有效解决内核生成的正确性与高性能问题，阶段感知的多智能体协作机制为稳定优化提供了保障。

Abstract: High-performance GPU kernels are critical for reducing the exponentially growing computational costs of large language models (LLMs), but their development heavily relies on manual tuning by domain experts. While recent advances in LLM-based approaches show promise for automating kernel generation, they still struggle to achieve both correctness and high performance. This limitation primarily arises from the lack of domain-specific optimization guidance, hindering effective exploration of the optimization space. We propose EGG, an Expert-Guided Agent Framework for Kernel Generation, which incorporates expert optimization principles to guide LLMs' decisions. Inspired by expert workflows, we decompose kernel generation into two hierarchical stages: 1) algorithmic structure design, which establishes a high-quality computational structure foundation; 2) hardware-specific tuning, which performs targeted adjustments through parallel mapping, tensor tiling, and memory optimization. This staged decomposition defines explicit optimization objectives, structuring the design space to achieve progressive refinement. To this end, a stage-aware multi-agent collaboration mechanism is designed for inter and intra-stage context management, ensuring stable optimization trajectories. Experiments on KernelBench and real-world workloads show that EGG achieves a 2.13x average speedup over PyTorch, outperforming existing agent-based and RL-based approaches.

</details>


### [37] [ResilPhase: Plug-and-Play Phase Mapping and Noise-Resilient Macro-Trajectory Extrapolation for Diffusion Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26769)
*Qicheng Zhao,Yu Li,Qi Sun,Zheyu Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ResilPhase框架，通过将加速推理重新定义为ODE空间中的稳定宏轨迹外推，并采用无导数重心拉格朗日外推器和有界相位映射，解决了扩散模型加速中因离散外推导致的特征不匹配、数值不稳定和导数噪声等问题，在FLUX.1-dev和HunyuanVideo上实现了高加速比下的最优保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有"缓存-预测"方案通过基于导数的多项式加速DiT，但在高加速比下质量严重下降。分析发现其根源在于：在连续扩散轨迹上错位且数值不稳定的表示上执行离散外推，导致累积空间误差、噪声导数放大和高阶不稳定性。

Method: 1) 将加速推理重新表述为ODE空间中的稳定宏轨迹外推，利用全局漂移（GD）对齐预测，消除特征不一致和内存开销；2) 提出无导数重心拉格朗日外推器，规避导数不稳定性和近似误差；3) 提出有界相位映射（Phase Mapping）正则化外推域，抑制振荡误差增长。三者构成ResilPhase框架。

Result: 在FLUX.1-dev和HunyuanVideo上，ResilPhase在高加速比下实现了最先进的保真度，优于现有加速方案。

Conclusion: ResilPhase通过噪声鲁棒的加速框架，有效解决了扩散模型加速中的数值不稳定和特征不匹配问题，为高加速比下的高质量生成提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The adoption of powerful diffusion models is hindered by their significant inference latency. Recent ``cache-then-forecast'' schemes alleviate this issue by accelerating DiTs using derivative-based polynomials, but they suffer from severe quality degradation at high acceleration ratios. Our analysis reveals its root cause: the discrete extrapolation performed on representations that are misaligned with the continuous diffusion trajectory and are numerically unstable. Thus, accelerated DiTs suffer from accumulated spatial errors, noisy derivative amplification, and high-order instability. We therefore reformulate accelerated inference as stable macro-trajectory extrapolation in ordinary differential equation (ODE) space. Instead of predicting intermediate features, we align forecasting with the model's Global Drift (GD), i.e., the end-to-end state evolution, thereby eliminating feature inconsistency and memory overhead. However, even this smooth macro-trajectory remains vulnerable to the derivative fallacy: its higher-order temporal derivatives are intrinsically noisy. Thus, we introduce a derivative-free barycentric Lagrange extrapolator to effectively bypass derivative instability and approximation error. We further propose a bounded Phase Mapping that regularizes the extrapolation domain, suppressing oscillatory error growth. These elements collectively constitute ResilPhase, a noise-resilient acceleration framework. Experiments on FLUX.1-dev and HunyuanVideo demonstrate state-of-the-art fidelity under aggressive acceleration ratios.

</details>


### [38] [Memory Depth, Not Memory Access: Selective Parametric Consolidation for Long-Running Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26806)
*Haoliang Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出循环漂移协议（loop-drift protocol）来研究语言智能体的记忆深度问题，并评估了EVAF——一种基于惊喜度和效价门控的LoRA合并机制。实验表明，检索在浅层事实回忆上表现最佳，而EVAF在目标持久性和卸载后恢复方面表现最强，仅需极少的参数写入。机制控制实验揭示了选择性合并可分解为选择和驱动两个可控维度，并发现内部环路写入强度具有模型依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期运行的语言智能体需要超越简单记忆访问的能力。检索系统虽能在查询时获取过去事实，但无法决定哪些经验在上下文被卸载后应继续塑造行为。现有研究将这一问题独立定义为记忆深度，即写入小型参数存储中的持久性目标条件化倾向。

Method: 提出loop-drift协议：在检索索引保持完整但工作上下文被卸载、目标条件化行为需在长环路干扰下持续的条件下进行受控压力测试。评估EVAF（surprise- and valence-gated LoRA consolidation mechanism）。在GPT-2、TinyLlama和Mistral-7B上进行实验，包括机制控制实验：匹配随机门控、固定内部控制、匹配门控反转等。

Result: 检索在浅层事实回忆上最强（短事实准确率0.956-0.973），EVAF在目标持久性和卸载后恢复上最强（0.812-0.904），仅需每200事件2-3次参数写入。选择性合并可分解为选择和驱动两个可控维度。内部环路写入强度具有模型依赖性。发现非对称的选择-驱动耦合以及陈旧记忆失效这一未解决边界问题。

Conclusion: 选择性参数化合成为记忆深度提供了与检索访问不同且互补的能力。记忆深度（通过EVAF等选择性参数合并实现）和检索访问是互补而非替代的关系，两者结合能更好支撑长期运行的语言智能体。

Abstract: Long-running language agents need more than memory access. Retrieval systems can fetch past facts at query time, but they do not decide which experiences should continue to shape behavior after the working context is unloaded. We study this separate problem as memory depth: durable goal-conditioned tendencies written into a small parametric store. We introduce the loop-drift protocol, a controlled stress test in which the retrieval index remains intact while working context is unloaded and goal-conditioned behavior must persist under long-loop interference. We evaluate EVAF, a surprise- and valence-gated LoRA consolidation mechanism. Across GPT-2 and TinyLlama, retrieval is strongest on shallow factual recall (short-fact accuracy 0.956--0.973), while EVAF is strongest on goal persistence and post-unload recovery (0.812--0.904) with only 2--3 parametric writes per 200 events. Mechanism controls show that selective consolidation factorizes into two controllable dimensions: selection and actuation. Matched random gates isolate selection beyond sparse writing; fixed-inner controls across GPT-2, TinyLlama, and Mistral-7B show that inner-loop write strength is model-dependent; and a Mistral-7B matched-gate inversion reveals asymmetric selection-actuation coupling under miscalibrated actuation. Public Memora event streams serve as an external diagnostic, exposing stale-memory invalidation as an unresolved boundary. Within this probe, selective parametric consolidation supplies memory depth distinct from and complementary to retrieval access.

</details>


### [39] [AgentX: Towards Agent-Driven Self-Iteration of Industrial Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26859)
*Changxin Lao,Fei Pan,Guozhuang Ma,Han Li,Huihuang Lin,Jijun Shi,Kangzhi Zhao,Kun Gai,Mo Zhou,Qinqin Zhou,Quan Chen,Ruochen Yang,Shifu Bie,Shuang Yang,Shuo Yang,Wenhao Li,Wentao Xie,Xiao Lv,Xuming Wang,Yijun Wang,Yiming Chen,Yusheng Huang,Zhongyuan Wang,Zibo Zhao,Zijie Zhuang,Baoning Xia,Chao Liu,Chaoyi Ma,Chubo He,Dawei Cong,Feng Jiang,Gang Wang,Guilin Xia,Hanwen Xu,Jiahong Xie,Jiahui Qiao,Jian Liang,Jiangfan Yue,Jing Wang,Jinghan Yang,Jinghui Jia,Kan Qin,Lei Wang,Ming Li,Peilin Song,Pengbo Xu,Qiang Luo,Ruiming Tang,Shiyang Liu,Shuxian Jin,Tao Wang,Tao Zhang,Xiang Gao,Xianghan Li,Yingsong Luo,Yiwen Ning,Yongcheng Liu,Yuan Guo,Zhaojie Liu,Zhenkai Cui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgentX 是一个生产环境中部署的多智能体系统，通过自主生成、实现、评估和从推荐实验中学习，将推荐算法迭代从人工驱动的工艺过程转变为自我进化的开发引擎，实现了实验规模和人效的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐算法迭代从手工、依赖工程师的过程向工业化研究循环转型，但存在结构性的执行瓶颈：从想法到上线的周期仍然依赖人类工程师生成假设、修改生产代码、启动A/B实验并归因线上结果。创新因此与人员规模线性相关，而非随证据、算力和积累的实验知识复合增长。

Method: AgentX 是一个四阶段闭环的多智能体系统：(1) Brainstorm Agent 综合历史实验、系统架构、数据分析和外部研究生成可执行的排序提案；(2) Developing Agent 通过代码库接地生成和多维可靠性验证将提案转化为生产级代码；(3) Evaluation Agent 进行带有护栏否决机制的A/B实验安全在线部署，将成功与失败转化为结构化知识；(4) Harness Evolution 层（SGPO）将执行轨迹提炼为语义梯度更新，持续优化智能体自身能力。

Result: AgentX 实现了远超人工工作流所能维持的实验规模和节奏，系统不仅实现了自动化，更实现了自我进化。

Conclusion: AgentX 通过多智能体协作和闭环自我进化机制，从根本上重构了推荐算法的生产函数，突破了人工驱动的线性扩展瓶颈，使创新能够随证据、算力和实验知识复合增长。

Abstract: Recommendation algorithm iteration is moving from an artisanal, engineer-bound process toward an industrialized research loop, but this transition remains blocked by a structural execution bottleneck: the idea-to-launch cycle still depends on human engineers to generate hypotheses, modify production code, launch A/B experiments, and attribute online results. Innovation therefore scales linearly with headcount rather than compounding with evidence, compute, and accumulated experimental knowledge. We present AgentX, a production-deployed multi-agent system that fundamentally restructures this production function. AgentX operates as a self-evolving development engine: it autonomously generates, implements, evaluates, and learns from recommendation experiments at a scale and pace that no manual workflow can sustain.
  The system orchestrates four tightly coupled stages in a closed loop. A Brainstorm Agent synthesizes evidence from historical experiments, system architecture, data analysis, and external research into ranked, executable proposals. A Developing Agent translates each proposal into production-ready code through repository-grounded generation and multi-dimensional reliability verification. An Evaluation Agent conducts safe online rollout with guardrail-vetoed A/B judgment, converting both successes and failures into structured knowledge assets. A Harness Evolution layer (SGPO) then distills execution trajectories into semantic-gradient updates that continuously sharpen the agents themselves -- making the system not merely automated, but self-improving.

</details>

*Zhixiang Lu,Xiwei Liu,Sifan Song,Changkai Ji,Anh Nguyen,Jionglong Su,Imran Razzak,Jinfeng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TAVR-VLM提出了一种风险条件因果注意力机制（R-CGA），通过"风险→区域→词"的结构化因果路径，将多模态特征压缩为风险瓶颈，显著减少了TAVR手术规划中的诊断幻觉，在M³TAVR数据集上实现了SOTA性能（AUROC 0.896，CIDEr 0.936，幻觉率降至8.1%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TAVR手术规划需要精细的多模态推理，但现有多模态大语言模型在此高风险领域存在严重的诊断幻觉问题——生成的文本缺乏解剖学依据，阻碍了临床应用。

Method: 提出风险条件因果注意力（R-CGA）框架，构建模型内部的"风险→区域→词"结构化因果路径：将多模态输入压缩为因果风险瓶颈，净化密集视觉特征为全局风险掩码；在自回归生成中，通过支持投影的因果一致性目标约束词级定位在风险定义的支撑掩码内。

Result: 在1,482名患者的M³TAVR数据集上，TAVR-VLM取得AUROC 0.896，CIDEr提升至0.936，幻觉率大幅降至8.1%，均达到新SOTA。

Conclusion: TAVR-VLM通过因果结构化的注意力机制有效缓解了高风险医疗AI中的诊断幻觉问题，提升了模型的可解释性和证据基础，为循证手术AI提供了新范式。

Abstract: Transcatheter Aortic Valve Replacement (TAVR) planning requires meticulous multimodal reasoning. However, adapting Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) to this high-stakes domain is severely impeded by diagnostic hallucinations, where generated text lacks anatomical grounding. To address this, TAVR-VLM is introduced: a novel framework featuring Risk-Conditioned Causal Grounding Attention (R-CGA) that instantiates a model-internal ``Risk $\rightarrow$ Region $\rightarrow$ Word'' structural grounding pathway. R-CGA compresses multimodal inputs into a causal risk bottleneck, purifying dense visual features into a global risk mask. During autoregressive generation, a support-projected causal consistency objective constrains token-level grounding within the risk-defined support mask. Evaluated on $\text{M}^3\text{TAVR}$, a comprehensive 1,482-patient cohort, TAVR-VLM establishes a new state-of-the-art. It achieves an AUROC of 0.896, boosts CIDEr to 0.936, and drastically reduces the hallucination rate to 8.1\%, thereby improving interpretability for evidence-based surgical AI.

</details>


### [41] [A Pipeline for Generating Longitudinal Synthetic Clinical Notes Using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26879)
*William Poulett*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种合成临床笔记流水线和数据集，通过模块化方法生成纵向一致性高、风格多样的合成患者记录，以支持临床AI工具开发，同时避免真实患者数据的隐私风险。发布了包含70名合成患者、每名患者20-50条临床笔记的数据集，并提供多层级验证版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床AI开发依赖真实患者数据，但医疗数据具有高度敏感性，隐私风险限制了数据获取。现有合成数据方法难以保证纵向记录的内部一致性和临床真实性，亟需一种既能保护隐私又能生成高质量、多样化临床笔记的方法。

Method: 采用模块化流水线：1) 结构化患者生成（定义患者人口学与诊断信息）；2) 半结构化患者旅程模拟（构建时间线事件序列）；3) 使用大语言模型生成非结构化临床笔记。通过LLM验证和增强步骤提升忠实度、真实性和多样性，并提供多层级验证版本供用户按需权衡真实性与可扩展性。

Result: 生成了一个包含70名合成患者的数据集，每名患者关联20-50条临床笔记，覆盖完整住院旅程。数据集提供多层级验证版本，在纵向一致性、写作风格多样性、笔记结构差异性和临床细节丰富度方面表现良好。

Conclusion: 该合成临床笔记数据集可支持临床AI系统的开发、测试和评估（包括摘要工具、编码模型和决策支持系统），无需依赖真实患者数据，在保护隐私的同时提供了足够的临床真实性和数据多样性。

Abstract: Synthetic data is increasingly used to enable the development and evaluation of AI systems in domains where access to real-world data is restricted. In healthcare, clinical documentation presents particular challenges due to its sensitivity. This work introduces a synthetic clinical notes pipeline and dataset designed to support the development of clinical AI tools while avoiding the privacy risks associated with real patient data. The dataset is generated using a modular pipeline that combines structured patient generation, semi-structured patient journey simulation, and unstructured clinical note generation using large language models. The pipeline is designed to prioritise internal consistency across longitudinal patient records, while also capturing variation in writing style, note structure, and clinical detail. Additional mechanisms, including LLM-based validation and augmentation steps, are used to improve faithfulness, realism, and diversity of the generated notes. We release a dataset of 70 synthetic patients, each associated with 20-50 clinical notes spanning a full hospital journey. The dataset is provided at multiple levels of validation, enabling users to balance realism and scalability depending on their use case. This dataset supports the development, testing, and evaluation of clinical AI systems, including summarisation tools, coding models, and decision support systems, without reliance on real patient data.

</details>

*Chenghao Liu,Yu Zhang,Zhongtao Jiang,Kun Xu,Zhenwei An,Renzhi Wang,Zhao Wang,Jiachen Zhang,Yuxiao Zhang,Kun Xu,Songfang Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MO-DiT+HPPO框架，通过度量排序连续预训练和混合偏好优化，在保持模式的同时提升嵌入检索中的属性检索性能，在8个域-划分组合中有7个显著改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有嵌入检索按相似度排序返回最高分项，但在许多生产场景中，需要从种子集合出发检索既满足目标属性又保持原模式（pattern）的更多项。平均种子可保持模式但属性低，全局属性检索会漂移至无关模式，二者矛盾，需形式化为模式保持属性检索任务。

Method: 提出MO-DiT+HPPO渐进式框架，包含：原始序列预训练、多域度量排序连续预训练（将稀疏在线检索标签转为有序轨迹）、尾质心微调、以及HPPO（混合候选池标注在线交并集指标+参考锚定偏好优化+Pareto对筛选）。

Result: 在四个属性域、项留出和模式留出协议下，MO-DiT相比预训练生成检索器提升交并集指标，HPPO进一步改善，8个域-划分组合中7个显著增益，最难的划分为边际持平。

Conclusion: 度量预测器验证、排序消融、CPT/SFT对比和候选策略消融实验确认了各模块的贡献来源，证明该框架有效解决了模式保持属性检索的权衡问题。

Abstract: Embedding-based retrieval ranks items by their similarity to a query in a shared vector space and usually aims to return the highest-scoring items. In many production settings this is not what is wanted: given a seed set that expresses a fine-grained pattern, one needs more items that both satisfy a target attribute and stay within that pattern. We formalize this as pattern-preserving attribute retrieval. The two goals pull against each other: averaging the seeds preserves the pattern but stays in a low-attribute region, while global attribute retrieval drifts to unrelated patterns. We approach the task with continuous generative retrieval, where a model reads a sequence of item embeddings and generates query embeddings for nearest-neighbor search. We propose MO-DiT+HPPO, a staged framework with raw-sequence pretraining, multi-domain metric-ordered continuation pretraining, tail-centroid fine-tuning, and HPPO. Metric-ordered training turns sparse online retrieval labels into in-pattern trajectories ordered from low to high predicted attribute density, teaching one model the metric-improvement direction across domains. HPPO aligns the generated query distribution with the true online objective by labeling a hybrid candidate pool with the online intersection metric and applying reference-anchored preference optimization. A Pareto pair filter keeps only winner pairs that do not lower same-pattern purity, raising the attribute metric without sacrificing the pattern. Across four attribute domains under item- and pattern-holdout protocols, metric-ordered DiT improves the intersection metric over a pretrained generative retriever, and HPPO improves it further, with significant gains on seven of eight domain-split cells and a marginal tie on the hardest split. Metric-predictor validation, order ablations, CPT/SFT comparisons, and a candidate-policy ablation show where the gains come from.

</details>


### [43] [Learning to Recover Task Experts from a Multi-Task Merged Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26902)
*Jinwook Jung,Taegyu Kim,Kumju Jo,Sungyong Baik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ReTeX框架，通过预测参数偏移量来撤销多任务模型合并中的参数干扰，从单个合并检查点恢复任务专家性能，无需在推理时存储冗余专家组件。该方法在视觉和NLP领域恢复超过95%的个体专家性能，并能自适应插值专家知识处理未见任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多任务模型合并存在参数干扰问题，静态合并效果差；动态合并虽能缓解但需要在推理时存储和加载冗余专家组件，成本高昂。

Method: 1) 将参数干扰建模为仿射变换，近似为加性偏移；2) ReTeX框架预测这些偏移量以撤销干扰；3) 引入基于SVD子空间签名的无路由器任务标识器，离线计算，推理时选择投影残差最小的子空间对应任务。

Result: 在视觉和NLP领域恢复超过95%的个体专家性能；显著提升对未见任务的泛化能力；参数偏移预测能产生对OOD任务的自适应专家知识插值。

Conclusion: ReTeX从单个合并检查点有效恢复专家性能，无需冗余存储和推理开销，并能自适应处理未见任务，为多任务模型合并提供了一种高效且可泛化的解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-task model merging aims to consolidate several task-specific experts into a unified model, yet static merging consistently suffers from parameter interference. While dynamic merging models aim to bridge this gap, many works rely on the costly storage and loading of redundant expert components at inference. In this work, from the perspective of task expert, we view parameter interference as parameter perturbation introduced to each expert during merging process. We show that such parameter perturbations can be modeled as affine transformation, which can be approximated as additive offsets. Motivated by these, we propose Recover Task eXpert (ReTeX), a framework that predicts those offsets, in order to undo parameter interference and recover task-expert performance from a single merged checkpoint. To recover the appropriate expert when task identity is unknown, we introduce a router-free task identifier based on SVD subspace signatures computed offline before inference. At inference, the identifier selects the task whose subspace yields the smallest projection residual for a given input. As a result, ReTeX recovers over 95% of individual-expert performance in both vision and NLP domains, while significantly improving generalization to unseen tasks. Crucially, we also show that the parameter offset prediction leads to emergent adaptive interpolation of expert knowledge for out-of-distribution (OOD) tasks. ReTeX adaptively interpolates seen expert knowledge to handle unseen tasks. Our code is available at https://github.com/BAIKLAB/ReTeX

</details>


### [44] [Diagnosing Task Insensitivity in Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26918)
*Jingyu Liu,Xiaopeng Wu,Kehan Chen,Chuan Yu,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 大型语言模型在长程任务中存在任务不敏感性问题——面对相似但不同的任务时，模型会套用训练时的模式而忽略当前任务指令。作者发现模型在语义受损指令下仍执行原任务，且替换任务描述后模型输出不变，原因是训练时注意力从任务token漂移到局部观测。为此提出Task-Perturbed NLL Optimization对比正则化方法，显式增强动作对任务指令的依赖，提升了任务敏感性和OOD泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型作为长程智能体时，其分布外（OOD）泛化能力较弱。作者识别出这一失败的关键原因是任务不敏感性：面对相似但不同的任务时，模型会套用训练中学到的模式，而无法解决当前实际任务。

Method: 提出Task-Perturbed NLL Optimization，一种轻量级对比正则化方法，通过显式鼓励动作对任务指令的依赖来提升任务敏感性。

Result: 实验表明该方法提升了任务敏感性和OOD泛化能力，同时保持了更稳定的对任务token的注意力分布。

Conclusion: 任务的分布外泛化失败源于任务不敏感性和优化偏向捷径的注意力漂移，所提正则化方法能有效缓解这一问题。

Abstract: Large language models can serve as capable long-horizon agents, but their out-of-distribution (OOD) generalization remains weak. We identify a key source of this failure as task insensitivity: when faced with similar but distinct tasks, models might apply patterns learned during training and fail to solve the task at hand. We show that models often continue with actions aligned with the original task even when the instruction is semantically corrupted and cannot be directly answered. We further find that, when we replace the task description in a trained prompt with another similar but distinct task, the model may still output the same action. This behavior is accompanied by a consistent training-time attention drift away from task tokens and toward local observations, suggesting an optimization bias toward shortcuts. To mitigate this problem, we propose Task-Perturbed NLL Optimization, a lightweight contrastive regularizer that explicitly encourages action dependence on the task instruction. Extensive evaluations show that our intervention improves task sensitivity and OOD generalization while preserving more stable attention to task tokens.

</details>

*Jingyu Liu,Zhiwen Wang,Yuxin Jing,Huanyu Zhou,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究链式思维（CoT）推理训练对语言模型的作用机制，发现CoT训练主要提升了模型直接从提示中预测动作的能力，而非增强CoT推理本身的效果；后期检查点更少根据CoT修改动作，且选择性屏蔽动作监督可提升泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究CoT训练究竟是在改善模型通过生成推理来改变动作的能力，还是在提高模型直接从提示中预测动作的能力——因为已有工作表明CoT推理可能只是事后合理化，而非真正影响决策的过程。

Method: 对比不同检查点上模型的"提示动作"（无CoT直接预测动作）与"CoT动作"（带CoT预测动作）的质量变化；分析CoT训练过程中CoT推理相对于直接预测的优势是否扩大；考察后期检查点根据CoT修订动作的倾向性；在部分训练样本上选择性屏蔽动作标记监督进行干预实验。

Result: (1) 随训练进行，提示动作质量显著提升；(2) CoT推理相对于提示动作的优势比例保持稳定，未因CoT训练而扩大；(3) 后期检查点更少根据CoT修订动作，对提示的依赖增强；(4) 选择性屏蔽动作监督改善了模型的域外泛化能力。

Conclusion: CoT训练的主要效果是提升了模型直接从提示中预测动作的能力，而非增强了CoT推理过程本身的因果作用；基于这一发现提出的选择性屏蔽策略可改善泛化性能。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning is widely used in language-model agents, but prior work has shown that verbalized CoT is not always faithful and may instead reflect post-hoc reasoning, which means the model already knows the answer before reasoning. We therefore ask what CoT training is actually improving: is the model getting better at changing its action through generated reasoning, or is it getting better at predicting the action directly from the prompt? We study this question by comparing \emph{prompt actions} (predicting action without CoT) with CoT actions (predicting action with CoT). Across checkpoints, prompt-action quality improves substantially. While interacting with the environment, the relative advantage of CoT actions over prompt actions remains similar, showing that CoT training does not widen the advantage of CoT reasoning, and it helps to improve the quality of prompt actions. We further find that later checkpoints are less likely to revise the action in response to CoT, suggesting greater reliance on the prompt. Motivated by these patterns, we selectively mask action-token supervision on a fraction of training examples. This intervention improves out-of-domain generalization.

</details>


### [46] [Look-Before-Move: Narrative-Grounded World Visual Attention in Dynamic 3D Story Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26964)
*Jiaming Bian,Bingliang Li,Yuehao Wu,Pichao Wang,Zhi Wang,Hailan Ma,Huadong Mo,Zhenhong Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种"先看再动"（Look-Before-Move）的相机规划框架，通过叙事约束下的视觉注意力机制，在动态3D故事世界中先确定观察目标再生成运动轨迹，显著优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身AI和世界模型需要在动态3D环境中主动决定观察什么，而非被动处理观测。现有方法缺乏在叙事意图和物理约束下提前规划观察目标的能力。

Method: 1) 语义观察契约（Semantic Observation Contract）：将导演意图转化为可执行的视觉约束；2) 蒙特卡洛视点搜索（Monte Carlo Viewpoint Search）：寻找符合叙事且几何可行的视点；3) 语义轨迹接地（Semantic Trajectory Grounding）：将选定的视点连接为连续、防碰撞、时间连贯的相机运动。

Result: 基于StoryBlender构建了包含50个故事、457个场景、1585个镜头的动态3D故事世界基准。框架在主体感知、意图一致性和轨迹质量三个维度上均优于代表性基线方法。

Conclusion: 在生成相机运动之前先组织视觉注意力是提升叙事驱动下3D视觉感知质量的关键，验证了"先看再动"策略的有效性。

Abstract: As embodied AI and world models increasingly operate in dynamic 3D environments, visual perception must move beyond passively interpreting given observations toward actively deciding what to observe. We study this problem through camera planning in dynamic 3D story worlds, where the camera must not only generate smooth motion, but also decide what visual evidence should be acquired before it moves. We formulate this capability as Narrative-Grounded World Visual Attention, where the camera acts as an embodied observer that determines what to observe, how to compose the observation, and how to shift attention over time under narrative intent and physical 3D constraints. To realize this capability, we propose Look-Before-Move, a camera planning framework that separates observation specification from motion execution. It first builds a Semantic Observation Contract to convert directorial intent into executable visual constraints, then performs Monte Carlo Viewpoint Search to find narrative-compliant and geometrically feasible viewpoints, and finally applies Semantic Trajectory Grounding to connect selected viewpoints into continuous, collision-aware, and temporally coherent camera motion. We further construct a dynamic 3D Story World Benchmark based on StoryBlender, covering 50 stories, 457 scenes, and 1585 shots with animated characters, semantic scene configurations, and executable 3D environments. Experiments show that our framework improves subject perception, intent consistency, and trajectory quality over representative baselines, demonstrating the importance of organizing visual attention before generating camera motion.

</details>


### [47] [Einstein World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26969)
*Munachiso Samuel Nwadike,Zangir Iklassov,Ali Mekky,Zayd M. Kawakibi Zuhri,Kentaro Inui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出爱因斯坦世界模型（EWM），一种让LLM通过调用世界模块生成视觉-时间演，从而支持基于视觉反事实推理的框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索能否通过可视化反事实事件来补充语言机制，增强LLM处理复杂推理（尤其是语言难以直接表达的现象）的能力。

Method: EWM将视觉-时间演算嵌入推理轨迹中：LLM调用世界模块生成场景的短期演算，该演算作为可检验的假设而非最终答案，辅助后续推理步骤。

Result: EWM将LLM的工具调用能力（如网页搜索、代码执行）扩展至视觉思想实验领域，使模型能够进行基于视觉的假设性推理。

Conclusion: 视觉反事实演算可作为语言推理的补充机制，EWM为LLM提供了一种在推理过程中利用可视化假设的新范式。

Abstract: Does intelligence require the ability to reason about phenomena beyond direct experience? It is natural to suspect that some complex thought cannot be captured through language alone. However, of particular concern to this work, is whether visualising counterfactual events can complement language as a mechanism for complex thought. We ask whether LLMs can be trained to utilise such visualisation mechanisms, in a way that benefits their reasoning abilities. Motivated by this question, we propose Einstein World Models. EWMs are a blueprint for LLM-based reasoning systems that place visual-temporal rollouts inside the reasoning trace, allowing them to reason in ways that text alone may not support well. In an EWM, the LLM calls a world-module (not to be confused with a world model), to produce short rollouts of scenes under consideration. The returned rollout is treated not as the answer, but as an inspectable hypothesis that can support later reasoning. Einstein World Models extend the capability of LLMs for tool calling (such as web search or code execution), into the domain of visual thought experiments.

</details>


### [48] [Adaptive Utility driven Resource Orchestration for Resilient AI (AURORA-AI)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27005)
*Rahul Umesh Mhapsekar,Ilias Cherkaoui,Lizy Abraham,Indrakshi Dey*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AURORA-AI框架，融合Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman反馈控制、Lyapunov稳定性监控和公平感知复合效用，在动态扰动下动态分配计算资源以优化预测性能、公平性、成本、延迟、鲁棒性和可解释性。在压力测试仿真中，AURORA-AI相比五种基线方法实现了更快的黑天鹅事件恢复、更高的分位数效用、更低的群体公平差距以及更多Lyapunov稳定步数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代AI系统在非平稳的计算、人口统计和运行条件下部署时，静态资源分配策略会同时降低预测性能和以人为中心的关键属性（如公平性和可解释性），亟需一种能够动态适应扰动的资源编排框架。

Method: 提出AURORA-AI框架，将Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman最优反馈控制、Lyapunov稳定性理论以及包含预测性能、人口统计均等性、成本、延迟、鲁棒性和可解释性的公平感知复合效用函数统一为闭环策略，持续在异构AI模型群体间重分配计算预算。

Result: 在注入人口统计偏差冲击、渐进概念漂移和突发黑天鹅事件的离散时间仿真中，AURORA-AI实现黑天鹅事件后即时恢复（而Static基线需88步，PPO需22步），将alpha分位数和超分位数分别提升29%和25%，同时降低均值和最大人口统计均等差距，并增加Lyapunov稳定运行步数占比。

Conclusion: 基于公平感知的自适应编排结合稳定性理论，为实现弹性、以人为中心的AI部署提供了一条实用且具有理论支撑的路径。

Abstract: Modern AI systems are increasingly deployed under non-stationary computational, demographic, and operational conditions in which static resource allocation strategies degrade both predictive performance and human-centric properties such as fairness and explainability. This paper presents AURORA-AI, an Adaptive Utility-driven Resource Orchestration framework for Resilient AI that unifies Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman feedback control, Lyapunov-based stability monitoring, and a fairness-aware composite utility into a single closed-loop policy.The framework continuously redistributes computational budget across a population of heterogeneous AI models so that the global utility, defined jointly over predictive performance, demographic parity, cost, latency, robustness, and interpretability, remains maximised under disruption. The framework is evaluated in a stress-rich discrete-time simulation that concurrently injects demographic bias shocks, gradual concept drift, and abrupt black-swan disruptions, and is compared against five established controllers including Static, Round Robin, Greedy, LinUCB, and a deep reinforcement-learning agent based on Proximal Policy Optimisation. AURORA-AI achieves immediate recovery from the black-swan event compared to eighty-eight time steps for the Static baseline and twenty-two for Proximal Policy Optimisation, lifts the alpha-quantile and the super-quantile by twenty-nine and twenty-five percent respectively, simultaneously reduces the mean and maximum demographic parity gap, and increases the fraction of Lyapunov-stable operating steps. These results indicate that fairness-aware adaptive orchestration grounded in stability theory is a practical and theoretically motivated path toward resilient human-centric AI deployment.

</details>


### [49] [Semantic Early-Stopping for Iterative LLM Agent Loops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27009)
*Sahil Shrivastava*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于语义的早停策略替代固定迭代次数来终止多智能体LLM循环，在HotpotQA上实现38%的token节省且质量持平，并指出最优轮次选择是比何时停止更核心的开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体LLM循环（如Writer-Critic）使用固定最大迭代次数作为终止条件，这种语法级kill-switch无法感知答案是否仍在改进，导致简单问题浪费token、复杂问题过早截断。需要一种语义级别的早停机制。

Method: 1) 理论：证明确定性终止和良定义性，用机器验证替代Banach压缩的不当假设；2) 评估协议：生成完整轨迹后回放所有停止策略，缓存LLM评判调用，实现严格配对的效率-质量对比；3) 实证：在HotpotQA多跳检索问答上测试无评判器的语义停止器（基于连续draft余弦距离+耐心窗口）。

Result: 无评判器的语义早停相比固定最大迭代减少38%操作token且质量持平（Delta-IS=-0.004, p=0.81）；含质量门控的变体因每轮评判成本过高而适得其反；理论上界（oracle选择最佳轮次）比所有实际策略高出+0.115 Information Score (p~4e-11)。

Conclusion: 语义早停有效，但核心问题应从"何时停止"（已解决）转向"哪一轮最优"（开放问题）；需分离操作token与评估token来正确衡量策略效率。

Abstract: Multi-agent large language model (LLM) loops, for example a Writer that drafts and a Critic that revises, are almost always terminated by a fixed iteration cap (max_iterations). This is a syntactic kill-switch: it is blind to whether the answer is still improving, so it over-spends tokens on easy inputs and truncates hard ones. We study semantic early-stopping: the loop halts when consecutive draft embeddings stop changing in meaning (cosine distance with a patience window) and the answer's measured quality stops improving. Our work makes three contributions. First, an honest theoretical footing: we prove deterministic termination and well-definedness and machine-check these claims, while treating the convergence of the distance sequence as an empirically tested conjecture rather than a (previously over-claimed) Banach contraction. Second, a judge-efficient evaluation protocol: we generate each question's full trajectory once, replay every stopping policy over the identical drafts, and cache every LLM-judge call, yielding a strictly paired efficiency-versus-quality comparison at low cost; we further separate operational tokens (charged to a policy) from evaluation tokens (a measurement instrument). Third, an empirical study on multi-hop retrieval-augmented question answering (HotpotQA). On the 60-question test split, a judge-free semantic stopper reduces operational tokens by 38% relative to max_iterations at parity quality (Delta-IS = -0.004, p = 0.81), whereas the full quality-gated variant is counter-productive because its per-round judging dominates cost. An oracle that selects the best round attains +0.115 Information Score over every practical policy (p ~ 4e-11), reframing the problem from "when to stop" (easy) to "which round is best" (open).

</details>


### [50] [How to evaluate clustering with ground truth?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27061)
*Pasi Fränti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文回顾了基于集合匹配的外部聚类效度指标，推荐质心指数（CI）作为直观的聚类级度量，并讨论了不同场景下其他指标（如PSI、ACC）的适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在外部聚类评估中，当有真实标签时需要使用外部效度指标，但现有指标众多且各有特点，需要对常用指标进行系统回顾和比较，以帮助研究者选择合适的评估指标。

Method: 聚焦于基于集合匹配的外部效度指标，对质心指数（CI）、对集指数（PSI）、聚类准确率（ACC）等常见指标进行了回顾和比较分析，从聚类级和点级两个粒度维度进行评估。

Result: 质心指数（CI）作为聚类级度量具有直观性和可解释性的优势；对集指数（PSI）提供不受聚类大小偏差影响的归一化分数；聚类准确率（ACC）等其他集合匹配度量适用于所有点同等重要的情况。

Conclusion: 不同外部效度指标适用于不同场景：CI适合需要直观聚类级解释的场景，PSI适合需要精细点级且无偏度量的场景，ACC等集合匹配指标适合所有点同等重要的场景。

Abstract: External indexes can be used for cluster evaluation when ground truth is available. We review the most common external validity indexes focusing on set-matching-based measures. We recommend centroid index (CI), because it is an intuitive cluster-level measure with an explainable result. If we need a more fine-tuned, point-level measure, there are more choices. Pair-set index (PSI) provides a normalized score which is not biased by cluster sizes. If all points should matter equally, then clustering accuracy (ACC) or any other set-matching measure is suitable.

</details>


### [51] [Joint Learning of Experiential Rules and Policies for Large Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27136)
*Shicheng Ye,Chao Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: JERP提出了一种联合学习框架，在LLM智能体的多步交互中同步更新经验规则池和策略参数，使得规则与演化策略保持对齐，同时将稳定行为逐步内化到模型中，在AlfWorld和WebShop上取得一致性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将交互经验用于两种分离的方式：外部自然语言规则（易解释但易与策略脱节）或通过轨迹更新模型参数（改进广泛但对局部错误修正有限）。作者希望克服这一分离，实现规则与策略的协同进化。

Method: JERP维护一个长期经验规则池，决策时检索任务相关规则与交互历史共同作为条件。每轮结束后，利用收集的轨迹同时优化策略和修订规则池（通过将当前rollout与参考成功轨迹对比）。

Result: 在AlfWorld和WebShop两个复杂交互任务上，JERP相较基线方法取得一致的决策性能提升。

Conclusion: 将规则池与策略联合学习能使规则始终与演化策略对齐，同时将稳定有效的交互行为逐步吸收到模型内部，从而提升多步交互环境中的决策表现。

Abstract: For LLM agents in multi-step interactive environments, a key challenge is to make effective use of accumulated interaction experience. Existing work has typically separated two uses of such experience: keeping it outside the model as natural-language rules for later prompting, or using trajectories and feedback to update the model parameters. The former is easy to interpret but can fall out of sync with the evolving policy; the latter improves the policy more broadly but provides only limited correction for local mistakes in sparse-reward settings. We present Joint Learning of Experiential Rules and Policies for LLM Agents (JERP), which updates a long-term experiential-rule pool and the policy from the same interaction trajectories. At decision time, JERP retrieves task-relevant rules and conditions the agent on them together with the interaction history. After each episode, it uses the collected trajectories both to optimize the policy and to revise the rule pool by comparing current rollouts with reference successful trajectories. This coupling keeps the rule pool aligned with the evolving policy while allowing stable and effective behaviors to be gradually absorbed into the model itself. Experiments on AlfWorld and WebShop show that JERP yields consistent gains in decision performance for complex interactive tasks.

</details>


### [52] [OpenRCA 2.0: From Outcome Labels to Causal Process Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27154)
*Aoyang Fang,Yifan Yang,Jin'ao Shang,Qisheng Lu,Junjielung Xu,Rui Wang,Songhan Zhang,Yuzhong Zhang,Boxi Yu,Pinjia He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 现有RCA数据集仅标注根因而非因果传播路径，导致任务退化为模式匹配。本文提出PAVE协议，通过正向验证机制重建因果路径，构建带步骤级标注的OpenRCA 2.0基准。评测11个前沿LLM发现：根因集完全恢复仅20.7%成功率，虽76.0%能识别至少一个正确根因服务，但仅有61.5%能将根因通过已验证的因果路径连接到症状，揭示"非扎根诊断"这一失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RCA数据集存在根本性缺陷：仅标注根因标签，缺少从根因到观察症状的传播路径标注，这使任务被简化为简单的模式匹配，无法真实评估LLM智能体的长上下文理解、多步推理和工具使用等核心能力。

Method: 提出PAVE（逐步标注协议），利用故障注入中已知的干预来重建因果传播路径，核心机制为正向验证——从原因推理到结果，而非从症状向后推理。基于该协议构建OpenRCA 2.0（500个实例），首个跨系统、带步骤级因果标注的RCA基准。

Result: 在11个前沿LLM上，完全恢复根因集的成功率平均仅20.7%。放宽评估标准后发现：76.0%的案例中智能体能识别至少一个正确根因服务，但仅61.5%能将根因通过已验证的因果传播路径连接到观察到的症状。

Conclusion: 仅基于结果的评估隐藏了"非扎根诊断"这一关键失败模式——智能体可识别正确根因服务但无法将其因果地连接到症状。步骤级因果标注是构建可信LLM-Based RCA智能体的缺失环节。

Abstract: Root cause analysis (RCA) poses a holistic test of LLM agentic capabilities, such as long-context understanding, multi-step reasoning, and tool use. However, existing datasets suffer from a fundamental gap: they label only the root cause, not the propagation path connecting it to the observed symptom, which largely simplifies the task to naive pattern matching. To support rigorous evaluation, we introduce PAVE, a step-wise labeling protocol that leverages known interventions from fault injection to reconstruct causal propagation paths. The mechanism is forward verification: reasoning from cause to effect rather than inferring backward from symptoms. Applying PAVE yields OpenRCA 2.0 (500 instances), the first cross-system RCA benchmark with step-wise causal annotations for LLM agents. Across 11 frontier LLMs, recovering the exact root-cause set succeeds in only 20.7% of cases on average. To locate where this difficulty lies, we relax the criterion and find what we call the ungrounded diagnosis: agents identify at least one correct root-cause service in 76.0% of cases, but ground that service in a verified causal propagation path to the observed symptom in only 61.5%. Outcome-only evaluation hides this failure mode; step-wise causal ground truth is the missing piece for trustworthy LLM-based RCA agents.

</details>


### [53] [Ask, Don't Judge: Binary Questions for Interpretable LLM Evaluation and Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27226)
*Sangwoo Cho,Kushal Chawla,Pengshan Cai,Zefang Liu,Chenyang Zhu,Shi-Xiong Zhang,Sambit Sahu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BINEVAL 是一个将评估标准分解为原子化二元问题、聚合生成可解释多维分数的框架，在多个基准上匹配或超越强基线，并支持可诊断的评估和提示优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM输出评估是NLP的主要瓶颈：人工评估昂贵缓慢，词法指标与人类判断相关性差，整体式LLM评估器产生不透明的分数难以调试。

Method: 将评估标准分解为原子化二元问题，通过元提示生成细粒度评估问题，LLM独立回答每个问题，聚合判决结果生成可解释的多维分数。

Result: 在SummEval、Topical-Chat和QAGS上匹配或超越UniEval和G-Eval等强基线，在事实一致性基准QAGS上表现尤为突出；更好地匹配人类评分分布，避免天花板效应；问题级反馈支持迭代提示优化。

Conclusion: BINEVAL是一个任务无关、无需训练、可解释的评估框架，兼具强实证性能与实用的诊断和优化价值。

Abstract: Evaluating LLM outputs remains a major bottleneck in NLP: human evaluation is expensive and slow, lexical metrics correlate poorly with human judgments on open-ended generation, and holistic LLM judges often produce opaque scores that are hard to debug. We propose BINEVAL, a framework that decomposes evaluation criteria into atomic binary questions and aggregates the resulting verdicts into interpretable, multi-dimensional scores. Given a task prompt, a meta-prompt generates fine-grained evaluation questions, and an LLM answers them independently for each output, yielding transparent question-level feedback together with calibrated overall scores. This decomposition makes evaluation easier to inspect, easier to diagnose, and directly usable for prompt improvement. Across SummEval, Topical-Chat, and QAGS, BINEVAL matches or outperforms strong baselines including UniEval and G-Eval, with especially strong results on factual consistency benchmarks such as QAGS. Beyond competitive correlation with human judgments, BINEVAL better matches human score distributions and avoids the ceiling effects common in prior LLM judges, leading to better discrimination between borderline and clearly flawed outputs. We further show that the same question-level feedback supports iterative prompt optimization, improving evaluator prompts on summarization and generation prompts on IFBench under both self-update and cross-model update settings. Overall, BINEVAL provides a task-agnostic, training-free, and interpretable evaluation framework that combines strong empirical performance with practical diagnostic and optimization value.

</details>


### [54] [Language-Based Digital Twins for Elderly Cognitive Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27334)
*Mohammad Mehdi Hosseini,Mohammad H. Mahoor,Hiroko H. Dodge*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于语言模型（LLM）的数字孪生框架，通过融合文体特征和上下文元数据来模拟老年人的对话行为，并利用多头条件变分自编码器（cVAE）评估重建质量与认知得分预测能力。在I-CONECT数据集上的实验表明，该数字孪生能够保留个体特征，其重建误差和MoCA评分预测误差与真实数据相当，且优于基线GPT模型生成的结果，为认知健康的非侵入式持续监测提供了可扩展方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轻度认知障碍（MCI）的早期检测在认知健康领域仍具挑战，现有方法缺乏非侵入性、可扩展的监测手段。语言和对话模式作为非侵入性生物标记物具有潜力，但缺乏能忠实模拟个体语言特征并保持认知一致性的计算框架。

Method: 提出语言数字孪生框架，利用大语言模型（LLM）结合文体线索（stylometric cues）和上下文元数据生成个性化对话；引入多头条件变分自编码器（multi-head cVAE）联合评估重建质量并预测认知评分（如MoCA）。

Result: 在I-CONECT数据集上，数字孪生保持了身份特异性特征，重建误差和MoCA预测误差与真实数据相当，且明显优于基线GPT生成的回复。

Conclusion: 语言数字孪生作为一种可扩展、非侵入性的方法，在个性化、持续的认知健康监测方面具有显著潜力。

Abstract: Digital twins have emerged as a promising paradigm for personalized healthcare, enabling modeling of individual behavior and health trajectories. In cognitive health, early detection of Mild Cognitive Impairment (MCI) remains challenging, where language and conversational patterns serve as non-invasive biomarkers. In this work, we propose a language-based digital twin framework that leverages large language models (LLMs) to mimic the conversational behavior of elderly individuals by incorporating stylometric cues and contextual metadata. To evaluate fidelity and cognitive consistency, we introduce a multi-head conditional variational autoencoder (cVAE) that jointly measures reconstruction quality and predicts cognitive scores. Experiments on the I-CONECT dataset show that the digital twin preserves identity-specific characteristics and achieves reconstruction and MoCA prediction errors comparable to real data, while outperforming baseline GPT-generated responses. These results highlight the potential of language-based digital twins as a scalable and non-invasive approach for personalized and continuous cognitive health monitoring.

</details>


### [55] [EO-WM: A Physically Informed World Model for Probabilistic Earth Observation Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27277)
*Junwei Luo,Shuai Yuan,Zhenya Yang,Yansheng Li,Zhe Liu,Hengshuang Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EO-WM，一种用于多光谱地球观测预测的视频扩散变换器。通过物理信息引导的条件框架（分离气候基线与天气异常、累积异常强迫信号），将天气作为条件信号进行世界建模。引入极端夏季基准和季节性匹配对基准来评估天气响应行为。实验表明，EO-WM在NDVI下降幅度误差上降低5.63%，方向命中率提升7.80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EO预测方法存在三个不足：确定性模型将不确定性坍缩为单一预测；扩散方法将天气变量视为无差别条件信号；现有基准主要关注重建精度而非对天气强迫变化的正确响应。作者希望将EO预测视为部分观测、天气驱动的世界建模问题。

Method: EO-WM采用视频扩散变换器架构，核心创新在于物理信息条件框架：(1) 将气象强迫分解为气候基线和天气异常，通过不同条件路径注入；(2) 累积异常强迫信号以捕捉持续的热胁迫和干旱胁迫。此外设计两个诊断基准：极端夏季基准（评估极端天气下植被退化预测的严重性感知能力）和季节性匹配对基准（评估改变天气强迫下的响应保真度）。

Result: NDVI下降幅度误差相对降低5.63%，方向命中率相对提升7.80%，同时在标准像素级指标上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 将天气作为结构化条件而非无差别信号的物理信息条件框架能有效提升EO预测对天气强迫变化的响应能力，所提基准可用于更全面地评估预测模型的天气响应行为。

Abstract: Earth Observation (EO) forecasting aims to predict future Earth surface dynamics from satellite observations under changing meteorological conditions. In this paper, we view this task as a partially observed, weather-driven world modeling problem, in which weather acts as a conditioning signal, while forecasting remains uncertain due to sparse observations and unobserved land-surface states. However, existing methods do not fully capture this setting: deterministic models collapse uncertainty into a single future prediction, while diffusion-based methods typically treat weather variables as undifferentiated conditioning signals, and existing benchmarks focus mainly on reconstruction accuracy rather than whether forecasts respond correctly to changed weather forcing.We introduce EO-WM, a video diffusion transformer for multispectral EO forecasting. EO-WM incorporates a physically informed conditioning framework that represents meteorological forcing through a climatological baseline, weather anomalies, and cumulative physical stress signals. Specifically, it separates baseline and anomaly through distinct conditioning pathways, and accumulates anomalous forcing over time to capture sustained heat and drought stress. To evaluate weather-response behavior beyond standard metrics, we introduce two diagnostic benchmarks: an Extreme Summer Benchmark for severity-aware prediction of vegetation degradation under extreme weather, and a Seasonal Matched-Pair Benchmark for testing response fidelity under changed weather forcing. Experiments show that EO-WM reduces the error in predicted Normalized Difference Vegetation Index (NDVI) decline amplitude by a relative 5.63% and improves directional hit rate by a relative 7.80%, while remaining competitive on standard pixel-level metrics. The benchmarks and model will be made open-source at https://github.com/Luo-Z13/EO-WM.

</details>


<div id='cs.CV'></div>

# cs.CV [[Back]](#toc)

### [56] [DocArena: Turning Raw Documents into Controllable Training Environments for Document Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26122)
*Jiamian Wang,Ruiyi Zhang,Tong Yu,Jing Shi,Samyadeep Basu,Rajiv Jain,Zhiqiang Tao,Tong Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DocArena，一个全自动数据构建管道，将原始文档集合转化为搜索智能体的训练环境，无需人工标注。通过MLLM视觉感知结构化和索引文档，构建推理密集型QA对，并设计Doc-Search智能体架构将视觉感知与策略模型解耦。实验表明在多种多模态文档场景和文本QA基准上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法从(问题, 答案, 证据)元组通过强化学习训练搜索智能体，但这些训练环境主要基于文本，难以构建可控、可扩展且能处理多模态数据的训练环境。

Method: 提出DocArena全自动数据构建管道：(1) 通过MLLM视觉感知结构化并索引文档；(2) 分析跨页面信息分布构建推理密集型QA对；(3) 通过MLLM执行级联质量保证操作。同时设计Doc-Search智能体架构，将视觉感知与策略模型解耦，使基于文本的LLM能够作为多模态文档检索和QA的推理骨干。

Result: 构建DocArena-79K数据集，涵盖8,336个文档、16个领域、49种语言。在6种多模态文档场景和7个文本QA基准上，基于DocArena数据训练的智能体在检索准确率和QA质量上均达到最优性能。

Conclusion: DocArena能够构建可控、可扩展的训练环境，有效提升搜索智能体的检索和问答能力，且对智能体搜索行为的分析证实了训练环境的有效性和可控性。

Abstract: Recent methods train search agents via reinforcement learning from (question, answer, evidence) tuples without requiring expert trajectories. The tuples serve as the training environment, and whose properties directly shape what search strategies and generalization abilities the agent can develop. While prior works have made encouraging progress in improving training data quality, existing environments remain predominantly text-based and existing approaches can struggle to construct training environments that are controllable, scalable, and account for multimodal data. Given this, we propose DocArena, a fully automated data curation pipeline building on the practical need for multimodal document search and question-answering. It transforms raw document collections into training environments for search agents without any human annotation. The pipeline first structures and indexes documents through MLLM-based visual perception, then profiles and leverage the cross-page information distribution to construct reasoning-intensive QA pairs, as well as performs cascaded quality assurance operations via MLLM. We introduce DocArena-79K with QA pairs from 8,336 documents spanning 16 domains and 49 languages. We further design a Doc-Search agent infrastructure that decouples visual perception from the policy model, allowing text-based LLMs to serve as the reasoning backbone for multimodal document retrieval and QA. Under a unified evaluation framework where only the policy model differs, experiments on six multimodal document scenarios and seven text-based QA benchmarks show that agents trained on DocArena data achieve the best performance on both retrieval accuracy and QA quality. Further analysis on agent search behaviors confirms the effectiveness and controllability of the constructed training environment.

</details>


### [57] [Predicting Fruit Quality with a Hybrid Machine Learning and Image Processing Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26165)
*Amir Reza Hashemi,Shahram Amiri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合图像处理与深度学习的混合方法，用于水果新鲜度评估。通过图像处理算法量化腐败程度（0-100分），结合CNN进行二分类（新鲜/腐烂），并用逻辑回归融合二者结果，最终实现无需CNN的实时二分类。在苹果和橙子数据集上取得90%以上准确率，但要求水果在白色或透明背景上隔离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水果腐败导致重大经济损失，现有方法在实时性和资源消耗方面存在不足，亟需一种高效、低计算成本的自动化新鲜度评估方案。

Method: 1) 图像处理算法量化腐败分数（0-100）；2) CNN进行二元分类；3) 逻辑回归融合两者输出提升准确率；4) 将逻辑回归模型用于图像处理算法的分数输出，实现无需CNN的实时二分类。

Result: 在苹果和橙子数据集上验证，准确率超过90%；无需高计算资源，实现实时性能。

Conclusion: 融合简单图像处理与机器学习在农业领域具有实用潜力；主要局限在于需要白色或透明背景隔离水果，未来可通过高级分割模型实现自动化背景去除。

Abstract: Fruit spoilage is a significant issue in agriculture, leading to substantial economic losses. Addressing this, our study introduces a hybrid approach combining image processing and deep learning to assess fruit freshness. We developed an image processing algorithm that quantifies spoilage on a scale from 0 (fully fresh) to 100 (fully rotten). Alongside, we trained a convolutional neural network (CNN) to perform binary classification (fresh or rotten) using a large dataset of fruit images. The outcomes of both methods were synthesized using logistic regression to enhance the accuracy of freshness predictions. Subsequently, this logistic regression model was utilized to enable the image processing algorithm to provide binary classification based on its percentage output, thus eliminating the need for the CNN in real-time applications. Our approach, which does not require high computational resources, achieved real-time performance and was validated with over 90% accuracy on a dataset comprising apples and oranges. The primary limitation lies in the requirement for fruits to be isolated on a background that must be either white or transparent, suggesting future improvements could include advanced segmentation models to automate background removal. This study's results highlight the potential of integrating simple image processing techniques with machine learning to provide practical solutions in the agricultural sector.

</details>


### [58] [LCG: Long-Context Consistent Image Generation with Sparse Relational Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26171)
*Zihao Wang,Yijia Xu,Haoze Zheng,Xuran Ma,Haokun Gui,Harry Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出长上下文生成（LCG）框架，通过稀疏关系注意力（SRA）和路由一致性约束（RCC）机制，解决多图像生成中的语义一致性和计算可扩展性问题，并构建了大规模长上下文一致性数据集（LCCD）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像生成模型在单图像合成中表现优异，但在漫画、故事板等需要序列一致性的多图像生成场景中，难以保持跨图像的语义和外观一致性。

Method: 提出LCG框架，包含两个核心组件：1）稀疏关系注意力（SRA）机制，选择性关注跨扩展视觉上下文的核心特征；2）路由一致性约束（RCC），利用身份感知掩码对齐不同生成分支的结构模式，减少多角色场景中的外观漂移。同时构建了大规模合成数据集LCCD（含60万训练序列和1K测试集，每序列6-20张图像）。

Result: 实验表明，LCG在提示对齐和角色一致性方面优于对比基线，尤其在包含多角色的长上下文图像生成场景中表现突出。

Conclusion: LCG框架有效解决了长上下文多图像生成中的一致性和可扩展性问题，为漫画、故事板等视觉叙事应用提供了可行的技术方案。

Abstract: Recent image generation models achieve impressive quality in single-image synthesis, but often fail to maintain consistency across sequential outputs, as required in comics, storyboards, and visual narratives. We propose Long-Context Generation (LCG), a framework for long-context multi-image text-to-image generation, to improve consistency and scalability in long-context multi-image generation. LCG employs the Sparse Relational Attention (SRA) mechanism to selectively attend to core features across extended visual contexts, ensuring that the propagation of semantic and layout information remains computationally tractable. To enforce semantic alignment, we introduce the Routing Consistency Constraint (RCC), which leverages identity-aware masks to align structural patterns across generation branches, effectively mitigating drift in appearance even in complex multi-character scenes. To support training and evaluation in this setting, we construct the Long-Context Consistency Dataset (LCCD), a large-scale synthetic dataset comprising character-centric multi-image sequences spanning varied situational contexts. LCCD contains 600K training sequences and a separate 1K test set, with each sequence containing 6 to 20 images. The experiments demonstrate that LCG outperforms the compared baselines in prompt alignment and character consistency for long-context image generation, including multi-character scenes.

</details>


### [59] [Self-Supervised Tree-level Biomass Estimation in Urban Environments From Airborne LiDAR and Optical Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26194)
*Jose Bermudez,Zilong Zhong,Dominic Cyr,Camile Sothe,Alemu Gonsamo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于航空LiDAR和RGB正射影像的城市树木冠层地上生物量（AGB）估算框架，在安大略省810 km²区域实现了冠层级别的AGB制图。通过双流交叉注意力网络进行语义分割（Dice=0.84），结合多尺度分水岭分割进行冠层勾绘，并利用冠层面积-高度幂律模型估算AGB（R²=0.570）。结果显示2018年总AGB为1.73 Tg，2023年为1.81 Tg，五年间碳净增39 Gg C。该框架无需人工标注，生成了公开的双时相冠层级AGB数据库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市树木生物量在空间上的显式量化程度远低于人工林，因为多数估算依赖无法解析单木冠层或细尺度异质性的清查数据或粗分辨率产品。

Method: 使用落叶期航空LiDAR（8-10 pulses/m²）和近红外RGB正射影像（0.16-0.20 m）；构建双流交叉注意力网络，基于规则生成的伪标签训练，输出建筑、针叶树、阔叶树的语义掩膜；采用多尺度分水岭分割进行冠层勾绘；利用冠层面积-高度幂律代理模型，校准至21,921株清查树木的物种特异性异速生长方程（Lambert et al., 2005）估算AGB；通过深度集成不确定性图谱识别高认知不确定性区域，将不确定冠层归入合并异速生长方程。

Result: 语义分割全局平均精度0.86、召回率0.83、Dice 0.84；AGB预测在18,713对清查-分割匹配数据上，使用清查冠层几何时R²=0.609，使用操作化分割时R²=0.570；2018年总AGB 1.73 Tg，2023年1.81 Tg；沿尼亚加拉断崖局部密度达~140 Mg/ha；五年碳净增39 Gg C。

Conclusion: 该框架利用标准省级数据，无需人工标注，生成了管理相关分辨率的双时相冠层级城市树木AGB数据库，冠层勾绘是当前剩余的不确定性来源。

Abstract: Urban tree biomass remains less spatially explicitly quantified than biomass in managed forests because many estimates rely on inventories or coarse products that cannot resolve individual crowns or fine-scale heterogeneity. We present a crown-level above-ground biomass (AGB) framework for an 810~km$^2$ landscape in Ontario, Canada, using leaf-off airborne LiDAR (8--10~pulses~m$^{-2}$) and near-infrared RGB orthophotography (0.16--0.20~m) from 2018 and 2023. A dual-stream cross-attention network trained on rule-based pseudo-labels produced semantic marks for buildings, needleleaf trees, and deciduous trees, supporting crown delineation and functional-type assignment. On independently annotated withheld tiles, global/mean precision, recall, and Dice scores were 0.86, 0.83, and 0.84. Crowns were delineated with multiscale watershed segmentation in mapped tree areas, and AGB was estimated from a crown area--height power-law proxy calibrated to species-specific allometry (Lambert et al., 2005) for 21,921 inventory trees. For 18,713 inventory--segment matched pairs from a 90,726-tree held-out test set, AGB prediction achieved $R^2=0.609$ using inventory crown geometry and $R^2=0.570$ under operational segmentation, identifying crown delineation as the remaining uncertainty source. Aggregated to 30~m, estimates yielded total AGB stocks of 1.73~Tg in 2018 and 1.81~Tg in 2023 (811--850~Gg~C), local densities up to ${\sim}140$~Mg~ha$^{-1}$ along the Niagara Escarpment, and a net carbon gain of 39~Gg~C over five years. Deep-ensemble uncertainty maps highlighted high-epistemic-uncertainty areas linked to underrepresented land covers and guided assignment of uncertain crowns to a pooled allometric equation. The framework uses standard provincial data, requires no manual annotation, and produces a public bitemporal crown-level AGB database for trees outside forests at management-relevant resolution.

</details>


### [60] [A multi-task spatiotemporal deep neural network for predicting penetration depth and morphology in laser welding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26260)
*Sen Li,Haichao Cui,Chendong Shao,Yaqi Wang,Xinhua Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多任务深度学习模型（CNN+状态空间模型）的激光穿透焊质量监测方法，通过熔池图像和焊接参数预测穿透状态（准确率99.35%）、穿透深度（误差1.79mm）及焊缝截面形态（准确率95.65%），为原位质量控制提供了新方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激光穿透焊中，穿透状态和焊缝形态的评估对焊接质量至关重要，现有方法难以同时高精度预测多个关键指标。

Method: 采用CMOS相机采集顶部熔池图像，构建多任务深度学习模型，融合CNN与状态空间模型提取时空特征，并结合焊接参数进行预测，同时提出了增强鲁棒性和泛化能力的数据集构建方法。

Result: 穿透状态预测准确率99.35%，穿透深度预测误差1.79mm，焊缝横截面重构准确率95.65%。

Conclusion: 该研究为激光穿透焊系统的原位质量控制策略提供了新的思路和方法。

Abstract: In laser penetration welding, the assessment of penetration state and weld seam morphology plays a crucial role in determining the weld quality. This paper presents a comprehensive introduction of the innovative muti-task deep learning model that has the capability to predict penetration state, depth, and weld seam morphology with high accuracy. The monitoring platform relies on weld pool images captured during the laser welding process using a complementary metal-oxide-semiconductor camera. The proposed model integrates spatiotemporal features extracted from top weld pool images along with welding parameters, establishing a deep learning framework based on convolutional neural networks and state space models for more efficient extraction and processing of spatial-temporal information. Furthermore, a reliable method for constructing the dataset is proposed to enhance both robustness and generalization capability of the developed model. Validation results on the test set demonstrate that prediction accuracy for penetration state can reach 99.35%, while prediction error for penetration depth is 1.79 millimeter, and accuracy of reconstructing the weld cross-section is 95.65%. This study provides new insights and methodologies for in-situ quality control strategies in laser penetration welding systems.

</details>


### [61] [Beyond Single-Source Cognitive Taskonomy:Multi-Source Task Relations through fMRI Transfer Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26279)
*Junfeng Xia,Wendu Li,Mengjiao Zhang,Jie Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文利用掩膜fMRI重建和布尔整数规划，将认知任务学从单源迁移扩展到多源迁移，揭示了跨范式受限的运动聚类和工作记忆状态的高优先级分配模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于重建的任务学主要研究单一源任务到目标的一对一迁移，缺乏对多源迁移以及预算约束下任务分配的分析，因此需要扩展至多源迁移场景以更全面理解认知任务的共享与专有神经过程。

Method: 使用HCP 23个任务状态的fMRI数据，训练1,127个任务特定和迁移模型，采用掩膜fMRI重建作为自监督目标，并利用布尔整数规划分析预算约束下的任务分配。

Result: 单源迁移具有方向性和范式结构化特征：运动状态在运动范式内迁移良好，但对非运动目标支持有限。多源迁移依赖于源集组成。BIP在预算约束下多次将直接监督分配给0-back和2-back工作记忆状态，尽管它们并非最强的单源。

Conclusion: 发现了跨范式受限的运动聚类和工作记忆状态在全局分配目标下的高优先级，将任务学从一对一迁移扩展至多对一任务关系和预算约束任务依赖。

Abstract: Cognitive tasks are organized by shared and specialized neural processes. Masked fMRI reconstruction provides a common self-supervised objective for quantifying transfer relations among task states, but existing reconstruction-based taskonomies mainly study one-to-one transfer from a single source task to a target. Here, we extend an fMRI cognitive taskonomy from single-source to multi-source transfer across 23 Human Connectome Project task states and use Boolean Integer Programming (BIP) to analyze budget-constrained task allocation. We train 1,127 task-specific and transfer models. Single-source transfer is directional and paradigm structured: motor states transfer well within the motor paradigm but provide limited support to most non-motor targets, consistent with a shared sensorimotor execution system and effector-specific representations. Multi-source transfer depends on the composition of the source set, suggesting that many-to-one task relations are not fully captured by pairwise taskonomy alone. Across supervision budgets, BIP repeatedly allocates direct supervision to several 0-back and 2-back working-memory states, although these states are not consistently the strongest individual sources. This pattern may reflect the integration of perceptual, attentional, and executive processes in working-memory tasks. Together, these findings reveal a cross-paradigm-limited motor cluster and working-memory states with high priority under the specified global allocation objective. Our study extends reconstruction-based fMRI taskonomy from one-to-one transfer to many-to-one task relations and budget-constrained task dependencies.

</details>


### [62] [GeMoE: Gating Entropy is All You Need for Uncertainty-aware Adaptive Routing in MoE-based Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26287)
*Chaoxiang Cai,Minghe Weng,Jie Li,Yibo Jiang,Longrong Yang,Zequn Qin,Xi Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GeMoE，一种基于门控熵的自适应路由方法，通过最小描述长度（MDL）理论将令牌路由视为信息编码任务，动态决定每个令牌应激活的专家数量，在保持99.5%平均性能的同时将专家激活稀疏性提升36.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MoE采用Top-k静态路由策略，无法根据输入变化自适应选择专家数量，导致资源利用次优。

Method: 将令牌路由视为信息编码任务，将动态路由建模为最小描述长度（MDL）问题；验证了MDL与MoE场景中门控熵之间的关联；利用门控熵评估令牌复杂度，自适应决定每个令牌应参与的专家数量。

Result: 在多种骨干网络和基准测试上，相比原始静态路由，平均性能保持99.5%，专家激活稀疏性提升36.5%。

Conclusion: GeMoE显式建模了模型复杂度与性能之间的权衡，相比传统静态或启发式动态路由方法更优。

Abstract: With the increase in model parameters and training data, the instruction following and generalization capabilities of Large VisionLanguage Models (LVLMs) have been significantly improved. Based on the Mixture of Experts (MoE) architecture, LVLMs expand their parameter capacity while maintaining the inference cost. However, traditional MoE methods employ a Top-k static routing strategy, which fails to account for variations in the input and adaptively select the number of experts, resulting in suboptimal resource utilization. In this paper, we propose viewing token routing as an information encoding task, framing dynamic routing as a Minimum Description Length (MDL) problem in encoding By validating the connection between MDL and gating entropy in the MoE scenario, we introduce Gating Entropy-based Uncertainty-aware Adaptive Routing (GeMoE) for MoE. Unlike traditional static or heuristic-based dynamic routing methods, GeMoE explicitly models the trade-off between model complexity and performance. By using gating entropy to assess the complexity of tokens, GeMoE adaptively determines the number of experts each token should engage. On a wide range of backbones and benchmarks, our method achieves 99.5% average performance retention compared to the original static routing, while improving average expert activation sparsity by 36.5%.

</details>


### [63] [Beyond Aesthetics: Quantifying Information Loss in Turbid Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26295)
*Vasiliki Ismiroglou,Stefan H. Bengtson,Tasos Benos,Thomas B. Moeslund,Malte Pedersen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了Turbid Underwater Baseline (TUB)数据集（1320张极端浑浊条件下的图像和超过16000个高置信度真值分割掩码），以及一个基于相位一致性图、对对比度不变的指标PCD，用于捕捉真实浑浊环境中结构信息的损失。实验表明PCD与实例分割模型性能强相关，而常用指标则弱相关或无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下浑浊环境会快速降低可见度，但现有计算机视觉模型在浑浊条件下的表现尚不明确，且依赖合成浑浊数据集可能无法真实反映实际信息损失，因此需要真实浑浊数据集和更有效的评估指标。

Method: 构建TUB数据集（采集真实极端浑浊环境下的1320张图像及16000+分割掩码）；提出PCD指标（基于相位一致性图，对对比度不敏感，用于量化真实浑浊中的结构信息损失）。

Result: PCD指标与实例分割模型在真实和合成浑浊图像上的性能均呈现强相关性，而领域内常用指标则表现弱相关或不相关。

Conclusion: PCD是比传统指标更能有效评估浑浊条件下模型性能的度量标准；TUB数据集为相关研究提供了真实场景基准。

Abstract: Visibility in underwater environments degrades rapidly under turbid conditions, yet the effects on computer-vision models remain unclear. This issue is compounded by reliance on synthetic turbidity datasets, which may misrepresent real-world information loss. To address this gap, we introduce the Turbid Underwater Baseline (TUB) dataset, comprising 1,320 images captured under extreme turbidity and over 16,000 high-confidence ground-truth segmentation masks. We additionally propose PCD, a metric derived from phase congruency maps that is invariant to contrast and aims to capture the loss of structural information in real turbidity. We show that PCD correlates strongly with the performance of instance segmentation models on both real and synthetic turbid images, whereas common metrics in the field show weak to no correlation at all. The dataset and relevant code can be found on the project page: https://vap.aau.dk/pcd

</details>


### [64] [Layer-Specific Prompt Fusion Discovery via Differentiable Search in Vision Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26379)
*Xi Xiao,Xingjian Li,Yunbei Zhang,Cheng Han,Tianming Liu,Tianyang Wang,Runmin Jiang,Jihun Hamm,Xiao Wang,Min Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于可微分架构搜索的混合融合策略，用于视觉提示微调（VPT），通过联合优化可学习提示及其融合方式（拼接、加法、仿射变换、交叉注意力），在34个数据集上一致优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉提示微调方法中，提示与图像令牌的融合方案通常采用单一的拼接或加法，但尚未系统研究何种融合策略更优，以及混合融合方案是否更有益。

Method: 将视觉提示微调建模为双层优化问题，利用可微分架构搜索（DARTS）同时优化可学习提示及其融合方案；在搜索空间中新增仿射变换和交叉注意力两种融合策略，与拼接、加法共同构成候选集合。

Result: 在VTAB-1k、FGVC和HTA共34个数据集上，该方法一致优于VPT-Deep及其变体，在保持冻结ViT骨干的情况下实现了精度-延迟-参数量的有利权衡。

Conclusion: 提示与图像令牌的融合方式在视觉提示微调中起着关键作用，混合融合策略能更有效地利用ViT各层的语义信息，为视觉提示微调研究提供了新视角。

Abstract: Visual prompt tuning has emerged as a parameter-efficient fine-tuning approach for adapting large-scale Vision Transformers (ViTs) to downstream tasks. As its learnable prompts are applied in input and feature spaces, prior to jointly going through attention in transformer layers, the most commonly used scheme for fusing image and prompt tokens is concatenation or addition. In this paper, we aim to study a fundamental yet essential problem in visual prompt tuning: whether a single fusion scheme tends to yield better results, and whether that would be beneficial to develop a hybrid fusion scheme. To this end, we formulate the task as a bi-level optimization problem, and solve it leveraging differentiable architecture search. In this context, the learnable prompts and their fusion schemes are jointly optimized. To enrich the search space in the architecture search, we propose two additional fusion schemes, namely, affine transformation and cross-attention, in addition to concatenation and addition. Extensive experiments on 34 datasets spanning VTAB-1k, FGVC, and HTA show consistent gains over prompt-tuning baselines. With a frozen ViT backbone, our method delivers a favorable accuracy--latency--parameter trade-off compared with VPT-Deep and recent variants. Our findings reveal that how prompts fuse with image tokens plays a significant role in visual prompt tuning, and a hybrid fusion fashion can more effectively leverage layer semantics of ViTs, contributing a novel perspective for visual prompt-tuning research.

</details>


### [65] [What Do Deepfake Benchmarks Measure? An Audit Using Frozen Self-Supervised Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26384)
*Samuel Pagon,Yixuan Shen,Vishal Asnani,Feng Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对视频、图像和音频领域的深度伪造检测基准进行了审计。研究发现，通用自监督表示上的线性探针能够接近专用检测器的性能，这意味着现有基准更多地奖励通用模态理解而非取证理解，可能无法反映真实世界的威胁模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度伪造检测器在基准测试中表现良好，但在实际场景中频繁失效。基准测试驱动的检测器日益复杂，但如果基准本身不能反映真实世界的深度伪造，这种复杂性可能是在解决错误的问题。因此，有必要先搞清楚基准到底在衡量什么。

Method: 使用一种诊断方法：在冻结的通用自监督表示上训练线性探针，将其性能与专用检测器进行比较。如果线性探针能接近专用检测器的性能，则说明基准主要奖励通用模态理解而非取证理解。实验覆盖视频、图像、音频三种模态。

Result: 在三种模态上，通用自监督表示的线性探针均能紧密逼近专用检测器的性能。此外，生成器级别的难度部分可由相同表示空间中的Frechet几何解释。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持对深度伪造检测基准进行审计的观点：在将高分数解读为取证理解的证据之前，有必要先问一问基准中有多少已经被通用表示解决了。

Abstract: As deepfake generators approach perceptual indistinguishability, reliable detection becomes critical. Yet, detectors that score well on benchmarks routinely fail in the wild. A concerning feedback loop has emerged: benchmarks drive increasingly complex, engineered detectors, yet if those benchmarks do not reflect real-world deepfakes, this complexity may be solving the wrong problem entirely. This raises a prior question: what are these benchmarks actually measuring? We conduct an audit of video, image, and audio deepfake benchmarks using a deliberately simple diagnostic. If a linear probe on frozen, general-purpose self-supervised representations can approximate the performance of a bespoke detector, the benchmark is largely rewarding general modality understanding rather than forensic understanding. This has two implications: the benchmark may not reflect realistic threat models, and it raises the question of whether the bespoke detectors the probe approaches are truly learning forensic understanding. We observe, across three modalities, linear probes on general-purpose self-supervised representations closely approach the performance of bespoke detectors. We further show that generator-level difficulty is partly explained by Frechet geometry in the same representation space. Together, these results support a benchmark-audit view of deepfake detection: before high scores are read as evidence of forensic understanding, it is worth asking how much of the benchmark is already solved by general-purpose representations.

</details>


### [66] [Staying VIGILant: Mitigating Visual Laziness via Counterfactual Visual Alignment in MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26387)
*Xi Xiao,Chen Liu,Chih-Ting Liao,Yunbei Zhang,Qizhen Lan,Yuxiang Wei,Lin Zhao,Janet Wang,Jianyang Gu,Muchao Ye,Tianyang Wang,Hao Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VIGIL框架，通过强化学习后训练中的互信息最大化几何约束，解决多模态大模型在视觉信息与语言先验之间的"视觉懒惰"问题，在仅用25%偏好数据的情况下达到SOTA对齐性能，并展现出新兴的空间定位能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLMs虽继承LLMs的强推理能力，但存在视觉懒惰问题——模型内部编码了正确的视觉证据，却在生成时过度依赖语言先验，导致幻觉。现有对齐方法（如DPO）基于文本结果优化，偏向语言捷径，加剧了与视觉证据的矛盾。

Method: 提出VIGIL框架，一种基于强化学习的后训练对齐方法。核心创新是引入几何约束，显式最大化视觉输入与生成响应之间的互信息。具体通过惩罚"盲目自信"实例实现，这些实例通过掩码文本-视觉注意力构造反事实的盲目状态来识别。

Result: VIGIL在幻觉和推理基准上一致优于近期对齐方法，且不损害纯文本能力。仅用25%偏好数据即可达到SOTA全数据性能，并在无显式边界框监督下展现出新兴的空间定位能力。

Conclusion: VIGIL通过将对齐目标从数值奖励拟合转向因果视觉接地，有效缓解了MLLMs的视觉懒惰问题，为多模态对齐提供了更高效、更具泛化能力的新范式。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) extend large language models (LLMs) with visual perception, enabling joint reasoning over images and text. Despite inheriting strong reasoning capabilities from LLMs, they remain prone to hallucinations that contradict their visual inputs. Mechanistic studies indicate that this weakness stems from visual laziness: MLLMs encode the correct visual evidence internally, but overly rely on strong language priors during response. Existing alignment methods, such as direct preference optimization, primarily optimize outcome-level rewards based on text. This introduces an optimization bias toward linguistic shortcuts, leading to responses that often contradict the visual evidence. To address this, we propose Visual Information Gain In aLignment (VIGIL), a reinforcement-learning (RL) post-training framework that shifts the focus from numerical reward fitting to causal visual grounding. VIGIL introduces a geometric constraint that explicitly maximizes the mutual information between the visual input and the generated response. We achieve this by penalizing "blind confidence" instances where the model remains improperly certain even when textual-visual attention is masked to create a counterfactual blind state. Extensive experiments show that VIGIL consistently outperforms recent alignment methods across hallucination and reasoning benchmarks without compromising text-only capabilities. Our approach matches the full-data performance of state-of-the-art methods using only 25% of the preference data and even demonstrates emergent spatial grounding capabilities without explicit bounding box supervision.

</details>


### [67] [DinoLink: A Token-Centric Representation Compression Framework for Bandwidth-Constrained Collaborative V2X Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26398)
*Tianle Zhu,Haohua Que,Handong Yao,Hongyi Xu,Zhipeng Bao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DinoLink是一个基于令牌压缩的语义通信框架，通过显著性感知选择器和残差向量量化实现V2X场景下的高效远程感知，在nuScenes数据集上达到32.8% mAP的同时实现139倍比特率压缩，在LoRa等窄带环境下可获得34.5倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: V2X网络的严重带宽限制阻碍了高精度远程感知，传统方法传输原始像素流效率低下，亟需一种在有限带宽下仍能保持高保真感知能力的通信方案。

Method: 提出DinoLink双稀疏架构：1）显著性感知选择器剪枝冗余背景令牌；2）残差向量量化模块将特征压缩为紧凑码本索引，仅传输轻量级索引和位置先验信息。

Result: 相比未压缩传输实现139倍比特率降低；在nuScenes数据集上达到32.8% mAP；在LoRa等窄带环境中获得34.5倍加速。

Conclusion: DinoLink作为一种鲁棒且带宽高效的前端方案，适用于带宽受限V2X场景下的高保真远程感知，代码已开源。

Abstract: High-precision remote perception is often hindered by the severe bandwidth constraints of Vehicle-to-Everything (V2X) networks. We propose \textit{DinoLink}, a token-centric compression framework that replaces raw pixel streaming with discrete semantic communication for vehicle-cloud collaborative inference. DinoLink employs a dual-sparsity architecture: a saliency-aware selector prunes redundant background tokens, while a Residual Vector Quantization (RVQ) module collapses features into compact codebook indices. By transmitting only lightweight indices and positional priors, DinoLink achieves a $139\times$ bitrate reduction compared to uncompressed transmission while maintaining a competitive 32.8\% mAP on the nuScenes dataset. Deployment simulations further demonstrate a $34.5\times$ acceleration in narrow-band environments, such as LoRa. Our results substantiate DinoLink as a robust, bandwidth-efficient frontend for high-fidelity remote perception in constrained V2X scenarios. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/UGA-MOBILITY-LAB/dino_link.

</details>


### [68] [Neural Voxel Dynamics: Learning Implicit 3D Physics via Volumetric Feature Advection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26410)
*Zican Wang,Niloy Mitra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自监督框架，通过将视频预测瓶颈从2D图像空间转移到3D体素潜空间（Volumetric Latent Space），利用V-JEPA特征和单目深度先验学习隐式3D物理动力学，无需显式物理引擎监督即可实现异构物理现象的仿真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的生成式视频模型虽然视觉保真度高，但缺乏3D几何基础，导致物理不一致和物体永续性失败。作者希望通过将预测瓶颈从2D提升到3D空间来解决这一问题。

Method: 1）将V-JEPA语义特征通过单目深度先验反投影到体素化网格中，构建3D体素潜空间；2）在该空间中通过Volumetric Feature Advection学习动作条件化的状态平流算子，将物理建模为时空状态平流问题；3）仅通过端到端的视频信号和动作条件进行自监督训练，不依赖任何显式物理引擎内部状态。

Result: 在CLEVERER、PhysInOne、PhysGaia等多个基准测试中，模型展现出良好的长期结构稳定性和物理合理性，能够在一个统一框架内涌现仿真异构物理现象（如流体中的刚体运动）。

Conclusion: 这项工作为通过被动观察单目视频构建内化3D物理不变性的通用动态世界模型开辟了一条可扩展的路径。

Abstract: We present a self-supervised framework for learning implicit 3D physical dynamics directly from video-derived supervisory signals.
