While current generative video models achieve high visual fidelity, they lack a 3D geometric foundation, often resulting in physical inconsistencies and a failure to maintain object permanence. We address this by shifting the predictive bottleneck from 2D image space to a `lifted' 3D Volumetric Latent Space. Our method unprojects semantic features from a Video Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (V-JEPA) into a voxelized grid, grounded by monocular depth priors. This lifting enables a Volumetric Feature Advection to learn an action-conditioned transition operator that treats physics as a spatio-temporal state advection problem, i.e., learn implicit 3D physics. Unlike state-of-the-art hybrid models that rely on explicit classical simulators for training and/or inference, our architecture tracks material states implicitly within high-dimensional V-JEPA features. This allows for the emergent simulation of heterogeneous phenomena (e.g., rigid body motion in fluid flow) within a single, unified pipeline. Supervised solely via end-to-end video-derived signal plus action conditions, without access to physics engine internal states, labels, or surrogate models, our model demonstrates good long-term structural stability and physical plausibility on multiple benchmarks (CLEVERER, PhysInOne, PhysGaia). We believe that this work opens a scalable pathway toward general-purpose dynamic world models that internalize the 3D invariants of the physical world solely through passive observation of monocular videos.

</details>


### [69] [Methane-Plume Segmentation From Hyperspectral Satellite Imagery Via Multimodal Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26416)
*Brayan Quintero,Jeferson Acevedo,Samuel Traslaviña,Hoover Rueda-Chacón*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种多模态深度学习模型，通过特征引导的甲烷增强（FGME）机制，在多个语义尺度上将物理上有意义的甲烷线索注入基于Transformer的RGB表示中，在MPDataset上以更低的计算成本实现了比现有方法更高的分割精度（MIoU+0.92, MPrecision+0.87, Recall+1.01）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 甲烷羽流的高效检测对理解和减缓全球变暖至关重要，但在地球观测图像中准确识别和分割甲烷羽流仍是大规模监测的关键挑战。

Method: 提出多模态深度学习模型，核心是特征引导的甲烷增强（FGME）机制，该机制在多个语义尺度上将物理上有意义的甲烷线索注入基于Transformer的RGB表示中。

Result: 在MPDataset上，MIoU提升0.92，MPrecision提升0.87，Recall提升1.01，且计算成本显著低于其他高性能架构，实现了精度与效率的有利权衡。

Conclusion: 高效的多模态融合策略在真实遥感应用中实现准确且可扩展的甲烷羽流分割具有巨大潜力。

Abstract: Efficient detection of methane plumes is crucial for understanding and mitigating global warming, as accurately identifying and segmenting them in earth observation imagery remain essential for large-scale monitoring. In this work, we propose a multimodal deep learning model that integrates a feature-guided methane enhancement (FGME) mechanism which injects physically meaningful methane cues into transformer-based RGB representations at multiple semantic scales. Our method is evaluated on the MPDataset, where it outperforms the state-of-the-art with improvements of +0.92 in MIoU, +0.87 in MPrecision and +1.01 in Recall. Notably, these gains are obtained with a substantially lower computational cost than other high-performing architectures, resulting in a favorable accuracy-efficiency trade-off for large-scale methane monitoring. These results highlight the potential of efficient multimodal fusion strategies for accurate and scalable methane plume segmentation in real-world remote sensing applications.

</details>


### [70] [Active Adversarial Perturbation-driven Associative Memory Retrieval for RGB-Event Visual Object Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26455)
*Xiao Wang,Xufeng Lou,Zikang Yan,Lan Chen,Sibao Chen,Yaowei Wang,Yonghong Tian,Jin Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出APRTrack，一种针对RGB-Event跟踪的分层扰动与检索框架，通过模态级和空间级对抗扰动模拟现实信号退化，并利用足迹引导的通道校准Hopfield检索实现可靠的历史特征补偿，在多个数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中RGB-Event多模态跟踪面临结构化信号退化（如模态失效、目标不完整、遮挡等），导致传统多模态融合方法鲁棒性不足。

Method: APRTrack包含：1) 模态级和空间级两个对抗扰动分支，分别模拟全模态失效和局部目标缺失；2) 分层路由机制解耦两种扰动类型的训练流程，避免特征坍塌；3) 足迹引导的通道校准Hopfield检索模块，基于关联足迹评估检索置信度并校准度量空间，实现可控的历史特征补偿。

Result: 在FE108、COESOT、VisEvent和FELT四个数据集上的大量实验证明了所提策略在RGB-Event视觉目标跟踪中的有效性。

Conclusion: APRTrack通过分层扰动模拟和可控历史检索有效提升了RGB-Event跟踪在复杂退化场景下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: RGB-Event tracking improves localization robustness by fusing RGB appearance textures and dense temporal motion cues from event sensors. While this multi-modal scheme broadens tracking applicability, real-world scenes suffer diverse structured signal degradations that hinder traditional multi-modal fusion. In harsh environments, either modality can lose reliability drastically, and targets frequently appear incomplete due to occlusion, edge truncation and foreground clutter.To tackle the above challenges, we present a hierarchical perturbation and retrieval framework tailored for RGB-Event tracking with robustness against partial target missing and modal degradation, termed APRTrack. To mimic real-world signal corruption, APRTrack constructs structured degradation via two adversarial perturbation branches at the modality and spatial levels, which separately simulate full-modal failure and localized target region absence. A hierarchical routing mechanism is designed to disentangle the training pipelines of the two perturbation types, effectively eliminating feature collapse induced by superimposed degradation constraints. Furthermore, we devise Footprint-guided Channel-calibrated Hopfield Retrieval (FCHR) for reliable historical information compensation. This module evaluates retrieval confidence based on association footprints between queries and memory banks, and calibrates the retrieval metric space prior to Hopfield matching, realizing controllable historical feature compensation bounded to target regions. Extensive experiments on FE108, COESOT, VisEvent, and FELT datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of our proposed strategies for the RGB-Event visual object tracking. The source code and pre-trained models will be released on https://github.com/Event-AHU/OpenEvTracking

</details>


### [71] [Forget, Anticipate and Adapt: Test Time Training for Long Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26515)
*Rajat Modi,Sebastian Noel,Xin Liang,Yogesh Singh Rawat*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出帧遗忘网络(FFN)，通过仅处理滑动窗口中的三帧（退出帧、当前帧和下一帧）并定义数学上的"惊喜度"指标来量化新帧包含的信息量，实现了对长达数小时视频的高效测试时训练(TTT)，同时避免了传统方法随窗口大小线性增长的计算开销和冗余帧处理问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时训练(TTT)方法在处理长视频时存在两个主要问题：(1) 使用滑动窗口进行TTT更新，计算量随窗口大小线性增长，处理数小时视频时计算开销难以承受；(2) 对视觉上相似的时间邻近帧也执行TTT，造成大量无效计算。

Method: 提出帧遗忘网络(FFN)：(1) 滑动窗口内仅操作三个帧——退出帧、当前帧和下一帧，仍能保留时序上下文；(2) 数学定义"惊喜度"指标，量化新帧相对于过去帧包含的新信息量，据此动态调整有效窗口大小，构成自适应窗口算法的核心机制。同时构建了EpicTours数据集，包含长达3小时的城市步行游览视频。

Result: 在密集分割、视频分类任务上验证了FFN的有效性，并展示了其对深度估计任务的泛化能力以及对长达数小时视频的处理能力。

Conclusion: FFN通过精简帧处理和自适应窗口机制，在保持时序上下文的同时大幅降低长视频TTT的计算开销，为长视频在线适应提供了一种高效且可扩展的方案。

Abstract: Test Time Training (TTT) is a mechanism in which a model adapts to an incoming test-sample by performing some self-supervised (SSL) task and updating its weights even during inference. This procedure does not require labels at test-time. This paper focuses on TTT for long-videos. A major concern with existing approaches is: 1) they perform TTT updates using a sliding window containing frames in the past, whose compute increases linearly with the size of window. This becomes computationally intractable when the videos are hours long. 2) TTT is performed even when temporally close frames look similar, thereby consuming a lot of compute.
  We present the Frame Forgetting Network (FFN) that: 1) operates on only three frames within the sliding window, namely the frame that exits, the current frame and the frame after that. The model still manages to retain temporal context and work for hours long-videos; 2) mathematically define a surprise metric: how much new information the incoming frame contains with respect to the past seen frame. This facilitates determining how to modify the effective window size during TTT and constitutes the core mechanism of an adaptive windowing algorithm. Additionally, we curate a dataset EpicTours containing up to 3 hour long videos of walking city-tours, whereas earlier datasets on this problem were only 5 min long. We demonstrate FFNs empirical effectiveness on dense-segmentation, video classification tasks, generalization to depth-estimation, and multi-hour long videos.

</details>


### [72] [From Hallucination to Grounding: Diagnosing Visual Spatial Intelligence via CRISP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26535)
*Zhixing Li,Yinan Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CRISP是一个新型结构化诊断评估范式，通过一致性（隐式感知与显式推理的对齐）来评估视觉空间智能，揭示了大模型在空间推理中存在感知-推理脱节问题：闭源模型拥有强推理引擎但受限于度量估计不准确，开源模型则受困于缺乏多跳组合推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM评估常将语言先验与真正的空间推理混淆，导致无法准确评估模型的视觉空间智能水平。

Method: 提出CRISP评估范式，利用度量3D场景图和oracle干预协议，通过一致性（隐式感知与显式推理的对齐程度）来解耦潜在推理能力与感知瓶颈，实现对视觉空间智能的结构化诊断。

Result: 揭示了系统性的感知-推理脱节：闭源模型拥有强大的潜在推理引擎，但存在度量估计不准确以及无法利用隐式结构表示的问题；开源模型则主要受限于缺乏多跳组合推理能力。

Conclusion: CRISP将评估焦点从依赖语言先验的"猜对答案"转向真正的"感知、验证和推理"，为端到端后训练之外的多模态对齐提供了严格的路线图。

Abstract: Current VLM evaluations often conflate language priors with genuine spatial reasoning. To address this, we introduce CRISP, a novel structural-diagnostic evaluation paradigm that assesses visual spatial intelligence through consistency, the alignment between implicit perception and explicit reasoning. Unlike traditional black-box QA, CRISP utilizes metric 3D Scene Graphs and an oracle intervention protocol to decouple latent reasoning capabilities from perceptual bottlenecks. This granular diagnosis uncovers a systematic perception-reasoning disconnect. Crucially, we reveal that while proprietary models possess robust latent reasoning engines, they suffer from inaccurate metric estimation and a critical failure to leverage their implicit structural representations. Conversely, open-source models remain fundamentally bottlenecked by their lack of multi-hop compositional reasoning. By shifting the focus from merely ``guessing correctly'' via language priors to genuinely ``perceiving, verifying, and reasoning,'' CRISP offers a rigorous roadmap for multimodal alignment beyond end-to-end post-training. The code and dataset are available at https://github.com/iiyamayuki/CRISP-Bench.

</details>


### [73] [PhyEditBench: A Real-World Multi-Stage Benchmark for Physics-Aware Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26551)
*Shengbin Guo,Shaokang He,Chaoyue Meng,Shengpeng Xiao,Xunzhi Xiang,Shaofeng Zhang,Qi Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PhyEditBench基准，用于评估图像编辑模型对物理规律的理解能力，并在此基础上提出无需训练的PhyWorld基线方法，利用视频生成过程作为图像编辑的推理机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于指令的图像编辑基准缺乏对物理推理能力的全面评估，而这种能力对于处理真实场景至关重要。

Method: 构建包含238个真实世界实例和35个合成反物理实例的PhyEditBench基准，采用层级分类法（4个主类、12个子类）；提出PhyWorld基线，利用测试时扩展和潜在空间缩减策略，无需额外训练。

Result: 当前SOTA编辑方法在物理推理方面存在显著局限性；PhyWorld在无需训练的情况下优于同类模型。

Conclusion: 视频生成过程可以有效作为图像编辑的推理机制，为物理感知的图像编辑提供了新方向。

Abstract: While instruction-based image editing, enabled by multi-modal generative models, has advanced significantly, existing benchmarks lack a comprehensive evaluation of physics-based reasoning, a critical capability for handling real-world scenarios. To address this, we introduce PhyEditBench, a benchmark designed to assess the physical understanding of editing models. Guided by a hierarchical taxonomy, we establish 4 primary classes and 12 subclasses. It comprises 238 high-quality, high-resolution, real-world instances meticulously extracted from videos to capture authentic physical dynamics, alongside 35 synthetic Anti-Physics instances. Our empirical analysis of current SOTA editing methods exposes substantial limitations in their physics-based reasoning. We further propose a training-free baseline named PhyWorld that uses test-time scaling and a latent reduction strategy. PhyWorld outperforms comparable models and suggests that the video generation process can effectively serve as a reasoning mechanism for image editing. The project page is available at https://github.com/Previsior/PhyEditBench.

</details>


### [74] [Perception, Verdict, and Evolution: Hindsight-Driven Self-Refining Forensics Agent for AI-Generated Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26552)
*Yangjun Wu,Keyu Yan,Yu Liu,Jingren Zhou,Fei Huang,Rong Zhang,Zhou Zhao,Fei Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ForeAgent框架，通过感知-判决架构聚合多视图线索，并采用事后驱动自我优化策略实现AI生成图像检测的持续自进化，在多个基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AIGC检测方法存在两个关键局限：对细粒度取证线索的敏感性不足，以及依赖前沿模型的静态合成监督导致灵活性差且成本高。

Method: 1) 感知-判决架构：聚合语义、空间和频域特征，利用MLLM进行逻辑推理判决；2) 事后驱动自我优化：采用采样-反思-进化范式，通过真实标签作为事后知识引导agent反思失败案例并生成更高质量的推理轨迹，经双专家质量门控过滤后用于微调。

Result: Chameleon基准上准确率82.18%（+16.41%优于AIDE）；AIGCDetect-Benchmark上16个生成器平均准确率93.3%；外部评估显示推理一致性和因果性优于GPT-5和GPT-5-mini。

Conclusion: ForeAgent通过自进化机制有效解决了现有方法对细粒度取证线索不敏感和依赖静态监督的问题，实现了AIGC检测领域的新SOTA。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of generative models presents a significant challenge to existing deepfake detection methods, particularly given the widespread dissemination of highly realistic AI-generated images. Although Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) show strong potential for this task, existing approaches suffer from two key limitations: insufficient sensitivity to fine-grained forensic artifacts and reliance on static synthetic supervision from frontier models, leading to limited flexibility and high-cost. To address these issues, we propose ForeAgent, an agentic forensics framework for AI-generated image detection with iterative self-evolution. First, ForeAgent adopts a Perception-Verdict architecture that aggregates multi-view cues spanning semantic, spatial, and frequency-domain features, and leverages an MLLM as a verdict module to fuse these signals for a logical-grounded verdict. Second, to enable continual self-improvement, we introduce a Hindsight-Driven Self-Refining strategy following a Sampling-Reflection-Evolution paradigm. The agent performs inference rollouts on training instances. Guided by ground-truth labels as hindsight, it reflects on failure cases and low-quality reasoning trajectories to regenerate higher-quality reasoning traces. These synthesized samples are then strictly filtered through a dual-expert quality gating module. ForeAgent continuously evolves via fine-tuning on self-curated high-quality samples. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ForeAgent achieves state-of-the-art performance on the Chameleon benchmark, reaching 82.18% accuracy (+16.41% over AIDE), and achieves 93.3% mean accuracy on AIGCDetect-Benchmark across 16 generators. In addition, external evaluation shows that ForeAgent produces more consistent and causally grounded reasoning compared to GPT-5 and GPT-5-mini.

</details>


### [75] [Coarse-to-Fine: A Hybrid Self-Supervised Method for Non-rigid 3D Shape Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26557)
*Feifan Luo,Ting Li,Zhao Li,Hongyang Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于粗到细策略的混合自监督非刚性3D形状匹配方法，采用双分支网络结构（拉普拉斯基与弹性基），通过对比能量提升特征判别性，在保持计算效率的同时在非等距变形和拓扑噪声等挑战场景下达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非刚性3D形状匹配是计算机视觉和图形学中的基础任务，现有方法在处理非等距变形和拓扑噪声等挑战性场景时性能有限，需要更鲁棒且高效的匹配方法。

Method: 提出混合自监督学习方法，采用粗到细策略保证粗映射与精化模块输出的一致性；设计双分支网络结构，包含两个对称的功能映射学习流（拉普拉斯基和弹性基）；引入对比能量损失函数提升特征判别性。

Result: 在保持计算效率的同时，在非等距变形和拓扑噪声等多种挑战场景下达到最先进性能；对比能量能够提升特征判别性，且与现有方法集成后带来一致性的性能提升。

Conclusion: 该方法通过粗到细的混合自监督框架和双分支设计有效解决了非刚性3D形状匹配难题，对比能量的引入进一步验证了整体方法的有效性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Non-rigid 3D shape matching is a fundamental task in computer vision and graphics. In this paper, we propose a hybrid self-supervised method based on a coarse-to-fine strategy, which ensures consistency between the coarse mapping and the refined correspondence produced by our refinement module. The architecture features a dual-branch design, consisting of two symmetric functional map learning streams: one based on the Laplacian basis and the other utilizing the elastic basis. Extensive experiments show that our approach not only maintains computational efficiency, but also achieves state-of-the-art performance across a variety of challenging scenarios, including non-isometric deformations and topological noise. Finally, we rigorously demonstrate that contrastive energies promote feature discrimination. Furthermore, integrating these energies with existing methods yields consistent improvements, validating the overall efficacy of our approach. Our code is available at https://github.com/LuoFeifan77/Coarse-to-Fine-Hybrid-Self-Supervised-Matching.

</details>


### [76] [SpaceRipple: Lightweight Semantic Delivery for Mission-Oriented LEO Earth Observation Satellite Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26559)
*Ziyi Yang,Hao Yuan,Yunxiang Yi,Wenbo Wang,Xing Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SpaceRipple是一个轻量级框架，通过感知卫星自适应压缩和元数据生成、边缘计算卫星恢复表示并提取任务相关语义信息，实现面向任务的语义传输，显著降低卫星网络带宽开销，同时保持高质量重建和语义检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球观测卫星网络产生大量高分辨率图像，但星间及下行链路资源有限，且许多时效性任务中地面用户需要的是任务相关的语义信息而非完整原始图像，因此需要一种面向语义的高效传输方案。

Method: 提出SpaceRipple框架，采用协同流水线架构：感知卫星进行自适应压缩和元数据生成以减少星间流量；边缘计算卫星恢复接收到的表示并提取任务相关语义信息；引入压缩感知的混合专家（MoE）增强模块以提升退化视觉输入下的鲁棒性。

Result: 实验结果表明SpaceRipple在实现良好重建质量的同时，提升了语义检测性能，并大幅节省了带宽资源。

Conclusion: SpaceRipple展示了在资源受限的卫星网络条件下实现高效可靠地球观测的潜力，通过语义导向传输替代像素级图像传输，有效协调压缩、转发、恢复和语义推理。

Abstract: Earth observation satellite networks generate massive volumes of high-resolution imagery, whereas inter-satellite and downlink resources remain limited. In many time-sensitive missions, ground users require mission-relevant semantic information rather than a full raw-image downlink. This paper proposes SpaceRipple, a lightweight framework for mission-oriented semantic delivery and on-board processing in Earth observation satellite networks. A sensing satellite performs adaptive compression and metadata generation to reduce inter-satellite traffic, while an edge computing satellite restores the received representation and extracts task-relevant semantic information. Unlike fidelity-driven image transmission, SpaceRipple coordinates compression, forwarding, restoration, and semantic inference within a collaborative pipeline, enabling semantic-oriented delivery instead of pixel-level image delivery. A compression-aware MoE enhancement module is further introduced to improve robustness under degraded visual inputs. Experimental results show that SpaceRipple achieves favorable reconstruction quality, improved semantic detection performance, and substantial bandwidth savings, demonstrating its potential for efficient and reliable Earth observation under constrained satellite-network resources.

</details>


### [77] [DiCoBench: Benchmarking Multi-Image Fine-Grained Perception via Differential and Commonality Visual Cues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26602)
*Geng Li,Yuxin Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DiCoBench是一个面向跨图像细粒度感知的高分辨率多图像基准测试，包含765个精心筛选的样本，分为差异视觉线索和共性视觉线索两个渐进轨道，覆盖8种感知任务。对18个多模态大模型的评估显示，顶级模型与人类准确率（98.3%）之间存在显著差距，尤其在微观细节捕捉方面表现不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试主要依赖显式文本线索或低分辨率输入，无法评估模型在自主感知高分辨率图像中隐式视觉线索的能力，因此需要引入新的高分辨率多图像基准来填补这一空白。

Method: 构建包含765个样本的DiCoBench基准，分为差异视觉线索和共性视觉线索两个渐进轨道（覆盖8种感知任务），采用多项选择题形式，使用接近2K的高分辨率图像，对18个多模态大模型进行系统评估。

Result: 人类准确率为98.3%，而当前最先进的多模态大模型表现显著低于人类水平，尤其在微观细节捕捉方面存在严重困难。

Conclusion: DiCoBench能够作为一个具有挑战性的测试平台，推动未来在自主高分辨率多图像感知领域的研究发展。

Abstract: Recent advancements in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated impressive fine-grained perception capabilities. However, existing benchmarks predominantly rely on explicit textual cues or low-resolution inputs, failing to evaluate a model's ability to autonomously perceive implicit visual cues in high-resolution. To bridge this gap, we introduce DiCoBench, a comprehensive, multi-image high-resolution benchmark designed for cross-image fine-grained perception. DiCoBench consists of 765 meticulously curated samples categorized into two progressive tracks: Differential Visual Cues and Commonality Visual Cues, covering 8 distinct perception tasks. By formulating the benchmark as a multiple-choice question task and utilizing high-resolution imagery (approaching 2K), we eliminate evaluation metric bias and pose a substantial challenge to current state-of-the-art MLLMs. Our extensive evaluation of 18 diverse MLLMs reveals a striking performance gap compared to human accuracy (98.3\%), with top-performing models struggling significantly with micro-scale detail capture. We believe DiCoBench will serve as a challenging testbed to drive future research in autonomous, high-resolution multi-image perception.

</details>


### [78] [LogicIR: Logic Gate Networks for Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26609)
*Hongjae Lee,Myungjun Son,Jaeseong Yu,Seung-Won Jung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LogicIR，首个专为图像恢复任务设计的逻辑门网络（LGN），采用完全由逻辑门构成的UNet架构，并通过可微比特解码层和索引洗牌机制提升信息传播，在多个基准上以极低计算成本实现优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像恢复模型计算需求日益增长，需探索轻量高效架构；逻辑门网络在高效计算方面展现出潜力，但尚未被应用于图像恢复领域。

Method: 提出LogicIR，采用完全由逻辑门（NAND、XOR等）构成的UNet架构；设计可微比特解码层实现梯度传播；引入索引洗牌机制改善逻辑门间信息流动。

Result: 在多个图像恢复基准上，LogicIR以显著降低的计算成本取得了强竞争力的性能，证明了其在效率和效果间的良好平衡。

Conclusion: LogicIR首次将逻辑门网络成功应用于图像恢复，验证了其作为高效替代方案的可行性，为后续轻量级图像恢复研究提供了新方向。

Abstract: Image restoration aims to reconstruct high-quality images from degraded low-quality inputs. As the computational demands of image restoration models continue to rise, there is growing interest in lightweight architectures optimized for fast and efficient inference. Logic gate networks (LGNs), which operate using fundamental logic operations such as NAND and XOR, have recently emerged as a promising direction for achieving highly efficient computation. However, their potential remains largely untapped in the domain of image restoration. In this work, we introduce LogicIR, the first LGN specifically designed for image restoration tasks. LogicIR incorporates a UNet-inspired architecture composed entirely of logic gates. In addition, we propose a differentiable bit decoding layer and an index shuffling mechanism that improves information propagation across logic gates. Experimental results across multiple image restoration benchmarks demonstrate that LogicIR achieves strong performance with significantly reduced computational cost, establishing LogicIR as a viable and efficient alternative for image restoration. The source code is available at https://github.com/jimmy9704/LogicIR

</details>


### [79] [TaskTok: Delving into Task Tokens for Task-driven Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26615)
*Hongjae Lee,Sojung Kang,Jaeseong Yu,Seung-Won Jung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TaskTok框架，通过选择性恢复任务相关令牌而非全部令牌，在保证下游任务性能的同时大幅提升计算效率，在图像分类、语义分割和目标检测任务上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图像恢复聚焦于人眼感知质量，而任务驱动图像恢复(TDIR)旨在最大化下游高级视觉任务的性能。现有方法使用生成先验虽有效，但无差别更新所有潜在令牌导致计算效率低下和潜在的语义改变。本文发现并非所有视觉信息对机器感知同等重要，任务相关线索在令牌序列中呈索引级不均匀分布。

Method: 提出TaskTok框架，包含：1) 可学习的令牌开关(learnable token switch)，用于选择任务相关的令牌子集；2) 轻量级令牌精炼模块(lightweight token refinement module)，仅对选中的令牌进行选择性恢复。

Result: 在图像分类、语义分割和目标检测等多个下游任务上，TaskTok显著提升任务性能，同时保持高计算效率。

Conclusion: 验证了仅选择性恢复任务相关令牌即可有效实现任务驱动图像恢复，为平衡计算效率与任务性能提供了新思路。

Abstract: While traditional image restoration focuses on perceptual quality, Task-Driven Image Restoration (TDIR) aims to maximize the performance of downstream high-level vision tasks. Recent approaches leveraging generative priors have shown promise for TDIR; however, they typically suffer from computational inefficiency and potential semantic alteration by indiscriminately updating all latent tokens. In this paper, we posit that not all visual information is equally important for machine perception. Through an analysis of the latent token space, we observe that task-relevant cues are unevenly distributed across the token sequence, exhibiting index-wise specialization. This suggests that selectively refining a subset of tokens can be sufficient for task-driven objectives. Leveraging this insight, we propose TaskTok, a novel framework that selectively restores only task-relevant tokens via a learnable token switch and a lightweight token refinement module. Extensive experiments across image classification, semantic segmentation, and object detection demonstrate that TaskTok significantly enhances task performance with high computational efficiency. The source code is available at https://github.com/jimmy9704/TaskTok

</details>


### [80] [Position Rebinding Cache Reuse: Replay-Free Visual Revisiting for Interleaved Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26631)
*Mengzhao Wang,Yanli Ji,Wangmeng Zuo,Peng Ye,Chongjun Tu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出位置重绑定缓存复用（PRCR）方法，通过存储原始视觉KV缓存及其空间坐标，在解码时重新分配位置兼容的坐标并重绑定键，实现无重放的视觉证据复用，避免了直接复用历史缓存导致的定位失真和自回归解码崩溃问题。实验表明，PRCR在多个多模态推理基准上达到或超越重放水平，平均准确率提升5%，视觉重放计算量减少数万倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交错多模态推理方法依赖token重放（反复前馈选中的视觉token），计算开销大。直接复用历史视觉KV缓存虽可降低计算，但存在严重问题：缓存的视觉键已绑定原始位置上下文，这种过时的位置绑定会在后续解码上下文中扭曲注意力，甚至引发自回归解码崩溃。

Method: PRCR方法：1) 存储原始视觉KV缓存及其空间坐标；2) 根据当前解码状态分配位置兼容的坐标；3) 选择对应缓存条目并重绑定其键；4) 将重构后的缓存注入当前解码器缓存。核心思想是以位置兼容的方式重构视觉证据，而非直接复制已绑定位置的历史缓存。

Result: 在多个多模态推理基准上，PRCR达到或超越重放级性能，平均准确率提升5%，视觉重放计算量减少数万倍。

Conclusion: 有效复用历史视觉证据的关键在于以与当前解码状态位置兼容的方式重构视觉信息，而非直接复制位置绑定的历史缓存条目。PRCR通过位置重绑定策略，在保持文本位置连续性和相对视觉结构的同时，实现了高效的无重放视觉复用。

Abstract: Interleaved multimodal reasoning improves visual grounding by revisiting visual evidence during multi-step generation, yet existing methods typically rely on token replay, repeatedly forwarding selected visual tokens. A natural shortcut is to reuse the historical visual key-value (KV) cache directly. However, we identify a critical failure mode of this strategy: cached visual keys are already bound to their original positional context. Such stale positional binding distorts attention under later decoding contexts and can trigger severe autoregressive decoding collapse. This failure suggests that effective cache reuse requires reconstructing visual evidence under positions compatible with the current decoding state, rather than directly copying position-bound historical cache entries. To this end, we propose Position Rebinding Cache Reuse (PRCR), a cache-level framework for replay-free visual revisiting. PRCR stores raw visual KV cache together with their original spatial coordinates, then reassigns position-compatible coordinates to select entries and rebinds their keys before injecting the reconstructed cache into the active decoder cache. This design reuses historical visual evidence while preserving textual positional continuity and relative visual structure. Experiments across multiple multimodal reasoning benchmarks show that PRCR achieves replay-level or better performance, improving average accuracy by 5 percent and reducing visual-revisiting computation by up to tens of thousands of times.

</details>


### [81] [Temporally Consistent Label Interpolation for Robust Surgical Multi-Task Learning under Challenging Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26634)
*Garam Kim,Juyoun Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FAROS框架，利用光流引导的标签插值（结合零样本分割掩码传播和光流估计）将稀疏的关键帧标注生成为密集伪标签，解决多任务手术场景理解中时序任务（密集帧标注）与空间任务（稀疏关键帧标注）的标注粒度不匹配问题，在统一Transformer多任务框架下实现时序与空间任务的联合优化，在GraSP、MISAW、AutoLaparo等基准上显著提升跨任务表示学习和手术场景理解性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手术场景理解中的多任务学习面临严重的标注粒度不匹配问题：时序任务（阶段识别、步骤识别、预测）需要密集的帧级监督，而像素级空间任务（器械分割、动作识别）因标注成本过高仅在稀疏关键帧上有标注，这种监督不平衡损害了共享表示学习，限制了异构手术任务的联合优化。

Method: 提出FAROS（Flow-guided Annotation for Robust Operating Scenes）框架，采用光流引导的标签插值方法，结合零样本分割掩码传播与光流估计，克服基于外观的传播在手术场景中遇到的遮挡、烟雾、运动模糊等挑战，从稀疏关键帧标注生成时序一致的密集伪标签；将密集化的器械掩码和动作标签集成到统一Transformer多任务框架中，联合学习手术阶段识别、步骤识别、预测、器械分割和动作识别，实现密集时序监督与稀疏空间监督的平衡优化。

Result: 在DAVIS 2017基准上使用稀疏标注协议验证了标签插值质量，证明其在手术领域之外的鲁棒传播能力；在GraSP、MISAW、AutoLaparo基准上的大量实验表明，FAROS显著提升了跨任务表示学习，并在各个时空任务上增强了整体手术场景理解性能。

Conclusion: FAROS通过光流引导的标签插值有效解决了手术场景多任务学习中的标注粒度不匹配问题，为异构任务的联合优化提供了可行方案，且其标签插值方法具有跨领域泛化能力。

Abstract: Effective multi-task learning for surgical scene understanding is fundamentally hindered by annotation granularity mismatch; temporal workflow tasks such as phase recognition, step recognition and anticipation benefit from dense frame-level supervision, whereas pixel-level spatial tasks including instrument segmentation and action recognition are only sparsely annotated on selected keyframes due to prohibitive labeling costs. This supervision imbalance undermines shared representation learning and limits joint optimization across heterogeneous surgical tasks. To address this, we propose Flow-guided Annotation for Robust Operating Scenes (FAROS), a flow-guided label interpolation framework, that combines zero-shot segmentation-based mask propagation with optical flow estimation to overcome the limitations of appearance-based propagation under challenging surgical conditions such as occlusion, smoke, and motion blur, generating temporally consistent dense pseudo labels from sparse keyframe annotations. The densified instrument masks and action labels are integrated into a unified Transformer-based multi-task framework that jointly learns surgical phase recognition, step recognition, anticipation, instrument segmentation, and action recognition, enabling balanced optimization between dense temporal supervision and sparse spatial supervision. The label interpolation quality of FAROS is first validated on the DAVIS 2017 benchmark under a sparse ground-truth protocol, confirming robust propagation beyond the surgical domain. Extensive experiments on GraSP, MISAW, and AutoLaparo benchmarks further demonstrate that FAROS significantly improves cross-task representation learning and enhances holistic surgical scene understanding performance across spatio-temporal tasks.

</details>


### [82] [FracEvent: Event-Camera Simulation via Fractional-Relaxation Pixel Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26636)
*Langyi Chen,Chuanzhi Xu,Haoxian Zhou,Pengfei Ye,Ziyu Luo,Haodong Chen,Qiang Qu,Xiaoming Chen,Weidong Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FracEvent是一种基于分数阶松弛电压动力学的事件相机模拟器，通过建模像素级生命周期中的电压弛豫过程，生成具有更精确时间结构的事件流，在图像重建和光流估计等下游任务中优于现有模拟方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实事件数据难以大规模采集（需要专用传感器、精确同步和任务特定标注），而现有模拟器虽然有效，但通常简化了每个像素的生命周期产生的时间结构，这会扭曲事件时序并削弱下游迁移性能。

Method: FracEvent模拟像素级生命周期中的分数阶松弛电压动力学：给定对数强度轨迹，驱动一个紧凑的松弛模式堆栈，将它们的响应组合成电压状态，通过在连续电压轨迹上定位阈值交叉来发射ON/OFF事件，并在更新参考值的同时保留底层记忆模式。

Result: 在多个数据集上，FracEvent生成的事件具有更优的时间结构，在图像重建和光流估计的下游迁移任务中显著优于竞争性模拟基线。

Conclusion: FracEvent通过保留与后续事件时序相关的残余电压响应状态，改善了生成事件的时间结构，证明了其在事件相机模拟中的实用价值。

Abstract: Event cameras asynchronously report brightness changes with microsecond-level temporal resolution, but real event data remain difficult to collect at scale because specialized sensors, careful synchronization, and task-specific annotations are required. Event-camera simulation is therefore important to event-based vision tasks. Most practical simulators build on contrast-threshold event generation, some with additional filtering, stochastic noise, or hand-tuned sensor parameters. While effective, such formulations often simplify the temporal structure produced by the lifecycle of each pixel, which can distort event timing and weaken downstream transfer. We introduce FracEvent, an event simulator that models this pixel-level lifecycle with fractional-relaxation voltage dynamics. Given a log-intensity trajectory, FracEvent drives a compact stack of relaxation modes, combines their responses into a voltage state, emits ON/OFF events by localizing threshold crossings on the continuous voltage trajectory, and updates the reference while retaining the underlying memory modes. This retained state links residual voltage response to later event timing. We evaluate FracEvent through event-stream comparison and downstream transfer on image reconstruction and optical flow estimation. Across multiple datasets, FracEvent improves the temporal structure of generated events and achieves stronger downstream-transfer results than competing simulator baselines, showing its practical value for event-camera simulation.

</details>


### [83] [LayersReg: A Layer-by-Layer Progressive Regressor for Reliable Intraoperative 3D/2D Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26647)
*Xiyuan Wang,Zhenchao Wang,Xinran Chen,Junkai Liu,Chuan Chen,Feng Yin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LayersReg，一种渐进式逐层回归范式，通过模拟经典配准的迭代位姿搜索优化准则，在特征空间中搜索移动与固定图像的相关性并捕捉像素流趋势，逐步收敛到正确的空间位姿变换。在大偏移和多模态条件下，X光/CT配准（0.68°, 1.41mm）和切片定位（0.73°, 1.55mm）任务上均达到SOTA精度，满足术中实时性要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统迭代优化算法在术中效率低、失败率高；现有深度学习方法将配准重构为回归问题，虽然提升了实时性和精度，但局限于记忆驱动的特定姿态特征检索，而非理解图像对齐任务本身，在复杂场景下泛化能力受限。

Method: 提出LayersReg：一种渐进式逐层回归范式，赋予模型3D解剖感知能力。核心思路是模拟经典配准的迭代位姿搜索优化准则，在特征空间中搜索移动与固定图像间的相关性，捕捉像素流趋势，逐步迭代收敛至正确空间位姿变换。进一步设计了节点回归与渐进配准框架的耦合，增强模型对空间位姿变化的感知能力。

Result: 在大偏移和多模态条件下，X光/CT配准任务达到0.68°、1.41mm精度；切片定位任务达到0.73°、1.55mm精度，均超越现有SOTA方法，同时满足术中精度和实时性需求。

Conclusion: LayersReg通过引入渐进式逐层回归机制，有效克服了现有可学习方法记忆驱动的局限，在复杂场景下实现了高精度、高鲁棒的3D/2D配准，具备良好的术中应用潜力。

Abstract: 3D/2D registration serves as a cornerstone technique in surgical navigation. Traditional iterative optimization algorithms suffer from low efficiency and high failure rates in intraoperative settings. Deep learning-based methods reformulate registration from iterative optimization to a regression problem that maps image appearance features to spatial pose, typically achieving improved real-time performance and accuracy. However, such learnable methods are confined to memory-driven retrieval of specific pose features rather than understanding the task of image alignment itself, which limits their generalization in complex scenarios. We propose LayersReg, a pioneering regression paradigm that endows the model with 3D anatomical awareness and searches for the correct pose in a progressive, layer-by-layer manner. Inspired by the iterative pose-searching optimization criterion of classical registration, LayersReg searches for correlations between the moving and fixed images in feature space, capturing the trend of pixel flow and thereby converging iteratively toward the correct spatial pose transformation. We further design a coupling of node-wise regression with the progressive registration framework to enhance the model's perception of spatial pose changes. Experimental results demonstrate that under large offsets and multimodality conditions, LayersReg achieves high accuracy on both X-ray/CT registration (0.68°, 1.41 mm) and slice localization (0.73°, 1.55 mm) tasks, outperforming existing state-of-the-art methods while meeting the intraoperative demands for precision and real-time capability.

</details>


### [84] [Disco-LoRA: Disentangled Composition of Content, Style, and Motion for Multi-concept Video Customization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26668)
*Xuancheng Xu,Gengyun Jia,Bing-Kun Bao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Disco-LoRA框架，通过两阶段解耦策略实现视频生成中对内容、风格和运动的多概念联合控制，并构建了相应的基准数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2V模型在图像风格化和视频运动定制方面取得了进展，但同时对内容、风格和运动等多个概念进行联合控制仍是一大挑战，缺乏统一的解决方案和评估基准。

Method: 提出Disco-LoRA统一框架，采用两阶段策略：(1)将目标分解为Content-Style和Content-Motion两个子任务，分别使用迭代双LoRA解耦框架进行概念解耦；(2)发现层间权重趋势决定LoRA身份而权重幅度决定可组合性，提出基于Z分数的统计正则化方法对齐权重分布，在保留层间趋势的同时最小化不同LoRA间的干扰。

Result: 大量实验表明Disco-LoRA在多概念视频定制中表现出色，能够有效保留外观、风格和运动特征，实现可控的文本到视频生成。

Conclusion: Disco-LoRA通过解耦和灵活重组不同概念，成功实现了多概念视频定制任务，为可控视频生成提供了统一有效的框架。

Abstract: Video customization based on Text-to-Video (T2V) models aims to learn specific features from reference data to generate controllable videos. While significant strides have been made in image stylization and video motion customization, simultaneously controlling multiple concepts, such as content, style, and motion, remains a major challenge. In this work, we systematically define the task of multi-concept video customization, which requires the joint control of content, style, and motion. To facilitate research in this area, we construct a comprehensive benchmark and propose Disco-LoRA, a unified framework designed to tackle this problem by disentangling and flexibly recombining different concepts in two stages: (1) We decompose the objective into two sub-tasks: Content-Style and Content-Motion. Each sub-task is addressed using our Iterative Dual-LoRA Disentanglement Framework, which effectively disentangles distinct concepts within the data. (2) We identify layer-wise weight trends as crucial for LoRA identity, while weight magnitudes dictate composability. To harmonize these scales, we propose a Z-score-based statistical regularization that aligns weight distributions, preserving layer-wise trends while minimizing interference between different LoRAs. Extensive experiments show that Disco-LoRA excels in multi-concept video customization, effectively preserving appearance, style, and motion for controllable text-to-video generation.

</details>


### [85] [DeCoFlow: Structural Decomposition of Normalizing Flows for Continual Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26687)
*Hun Im,Jungi Lee,Subeen Cha,Pilsung Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DeCoFlow方法，通过将归一化流子网分解为冻结通用基座和任务特定低秩适配器，实现连续工业异常检测中的零遗忘，在MVTec-AD和VisA上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业环境中新品类顺序到达，需持续进行异常检测但无法访问历史数据。归一化流虽能精确估计密度，但参数更新会导致灾难性遗忘，而参数隔离需保持严格可逆性和雅可比有效性。

Method: 利用仿射耦合层子网参数化不影响变换有效性的特性，将子网分解为冻结通用基座和任务特定低秩适配器（LoRA）以隔离更新；进一步引入任务特定对齐、辅助耦合层和尾感知损失补偿冻结基座的刚性。

Result: MVTec-AD上图像级AUROC达98.40%，VisA上达93.00%；正确路由下参数级遗忘率为0.00%（FM），每个任务仅需2.27M参数。

Conclusion: DeCoFlow在保持归一化流严格可逆性的同时实现了参数级零遗忘，以极低参数量达到SOTA性能，有效解决了持续异常检测中的灾难性遗忘问题。

Abstract: In industrial environments, new product categories arrive sequentially, requiring continual anomaly detection without access to past data. Normalizing Flows (NFs) provide exact density estimation but suffer from catastrophic forgetting as parameter updates across tasks distort the density manifold. While parameter isolation can prevent interference, it must preserve the strict invertibility and Jacobian validity of NFs. To satisfy these requirements, we exploit the inherent property that affine coupling layers maintain transformation validity regardless of subnet parameterization. Based on this, we propose DeCoFlow, which decomposes subnets into a frozen universal base and task-specific low-rank adapters to isolate updates. We further introduce Task-Specific Alignment, Auxiliary Coupling Layers, and Tail-Aware Loss to compensate for frozen-base rigidity. DeCoFlow achieves state-of-the-art image-level AUROCs of 98.40% on MVTec-AD and 93.00% on VisA, while maintaining parameter-level zero forgetting (0.00% FM under correct routing) with only 2.27M parameters per task.

</details>


### [86] [PhysEditWorld: A Large-Scale Dataset Toward Physics-Editable World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26694)
*Bin Hu,Yanwen Ma,Jiehui Huang,Ziliang Zhang,Haoning Wu,Ruicheng Zhang,Yaokun Li,Zijun Wang,Yuechen Zhang,Chun-Mei Tseng,Hanhui Li,Shengju Qian,Jun Zhou,Kaipeng Zhang,Xiaodan Liang,Jiaya Jia,Xiu Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhysEditWorld是一个基于UE5的多模态物理参数数据集（初始版本聚焦重力），包含12个电影级场景、100小时以上交互和6000万渲染帧，提供RGB、深度、法线、音频、动作轨迹、相机轨迹、引擎状态、语义标注和重力标签等同步多模态信号，通过重放范式实现可控物理变化，在生成视频模型和世界理解模型上验证了其提升重力忠实动力学建模和物理编辑一致性的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有游戏世界模型能合成视觉上合理的动作条件展开，但交互行为局限于探索或漫游轨迹，物理动力学通常作为数据中的隐式相关性而非可控变量学习，限制了在精心设计物理规则的创作游戏环境中的应用。

Method: 基于UE5重放-渲染管线的重放范式：记录标准化动作轨迹，在多种重力配置下重放相同的初始状态、角色控制器、动作序列和相机策略，实现可控且可归因的物理变化。

Result: 包含12个UE5电影级场景、100小时以上游戏交互、6000万渲染帧；每样本提供RGB、深度、法线、音频、动作轨迹、相机轨迹、引擎状态、语义标注和重力标签；初步实验表明可提升重力忠实动力学建模和物理编辑一致性。

Conclusion: PhysEditWorld为可控世界建模研究提供了可扩展基础，使物理规则成为显式可控变量，弥补了现有世界模型在物理可控性方面的不足。

Abstract: Recent game world models can synthesize visually plausible, action-conditioned rollouts. However, their interaction behaviors often remain limited to exploratory or wandering trajectories, and physical dynamics are typically learned as implicit correlations from data rather than as controllable variables. This limitation hinders their applicability to authored game environments, where physical rules are deliberately designed and require explicit manipulation. We introduce PhysEditWorld, a multimodal dataset with physical parameters, with a primary focus on gravity in this initial version. At its core, PhysEditWorld is built upon a replay paradigm implemented with a UE5 replay-and-rendering pipeline. Each scenario records a normalized action trace and replays the same initial state, character controller, action sequence, and camera policy under multiple gravity configurations, enabling controlled and attributable physical variation. PhysEditWorld contains 12 cinematic UE5 scenes, over 100 hours of gameplay interactions, and more than 60 million rendered rollout frames. Each sample provides synchronized multimodal signals, including RGB, depth, normals, audio, action traces, camera trajectory, engine states, semantic annotations, and explicit gravity labels. We further conduct initial utility studies on both generative video models and world understanding models, demonstrating that PhysEditWorld enables improved gravity-faithful dynamics modeling, enhances consistency under physical edits, and provides a scalable foundation for controllable world modeling research.

</details>


### [87] [Intracranial Aneurysm Classification and Segmentation via Tri-Axial ROI and Multi-Task Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26706)
*Pengcheng Shi,Kaiyuan Yang,Houjing Huang,Jiawei Chen,Yan Lu,Jiaqi Liu,Murong Xu,Bjoern Menze,Xinglin Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个多任务框架，同时进行13个解剖位置的颅内动脉瘤多标签分类、多类别分割和血管多类别分割，支持CTA、MRA、T2、T1-post四种模态。采用2D三轴ROI提取+3D多任务nnU-Net的双阶段方法，通过双解码器设计解决动静脉瘤体积严重不平衡问题，辅以交叉注意力池化和模态特异性辅助头，最终在RSNA 2025挑战赛中获得第二名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有颅内动脉瘤自动化方法仅限于二元检测，缺乏细粒度解剖分类和多类别分割能力，而破裂风险评估和治疗规划高度依赖动脉瘤的形态学和解剖位置信息。

Method: 两阶段方法：第一阶段用快速2D三轴ROI提取方法定位感兴趣区域；第二阶段使用3D多任务nnU-Net骨干网络，采用双解码器设计缓解动静脉瘤体积极不平衡问题，通过交叉注意力池化和模态特异性辅助头增强异构输入的特征学习。

Result: 在RSNA 2025颅内动脉瘤检测挑战赛中，两折集成模型获得第二名。

Conclusion: 该多任务框架在多个解剖位置和多模态数据上实现了动脉瘤的细粒度分类与分割，代码、模型权重和3D Slicer插件均已开源。

Abstract: Intracranial aneurysms are often asymptomatic until rupture, which carries high mortality. Rupture risk assessment and treatment planning depend on both aneurysm morphology and anatomical location, yet existing automated methods remain limited to binary detection without fine-grained anatomical classification or multi-class segmentation. We present a multi-task framework that simultaneously performs multi-label classification, multi-class aneurysm segmentation, and multi-class vessel segmentation across 13 anatomical locations and four imaging modalities (CTA, MRA, T2, T1-post). Our two-stage approach combines a fast 2D tri-axial Region of Interest (ROI) extraction method with a 3D multi-task nnU-Net backbone. A dual-decoder design mitigates the extreme volume imbalance between aneurysm and vessel classes, while cross-attention pooling and modality-specific auxiliary heads improve feature learning across heterogeneous inputs. Our two-fold ensemble achieved 2nd place in the RSNA 2025 Intracranial Aneurysm Detection challenge. Code, model weights, and a 3D Slicer plugin are publicly available.

</details>


### [88] [Mask to Concept: Auto-Promptable SAM3 via Efficient Test-Time Concept Embedding Search for Few-Shot Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26711)
*Quan Zhou,Shaoqing Zhai,Qiang Hu Jia Chen,Qiang Li,Zhiwei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Mask to Concept (M2C)框架，无需外部模块、参数重训练或手动文本工程，仅用少量标注图像即可将SAM3适配为医学图像少样本自动标注工具。通过可学习概念嵌入、混合不确定性估计(HUE)模块和自增强标注循环，实现最先进的少样本分割性能和标注效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将基础分割模型从人工提示工具转变为可自动提示的标注器对于可扩展的医学数据标注至关重要，现有方法依赖外部特征匹配器或辅助网络，引入架构开销且限制了性能可扩展性；SAM3虽原生支持通过文本提示进行概念分割，但在医学图像中缺乏细粒度临床知识且人工描述存在歧义。

Method: M2C框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 可学习概念嵌入——在SAM3冻结架构内初始化可学习嵌入，通过梯度反向传播最小化概念分割误差来更新嵌入；(2) 混合不确定性估计(HUE)模块——计算预测熵并将概念预测映射回边界框提示，度量概念-几何提示不一致性；(3) 自增强标注循环——高不确定性样本被标记供人工修正，修正后的掩码反馈回M2C以持续搜索更精确的概念嵌入。

Result: 在医学分割基准上，该方法实现了最先进的少样本分割性能和出色的标注效率。

Conclusion: M2C提供了一种无需外部模块、参数重训练或手动文本工程的高效途径，为可扩展的医学图像标注提供了实用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Transforming foundation segmentation models from human-prompted tools into auto-promptable annotators is critical for scalable medical data annotation. Current methods commonly depend on external feature matchers or auxiliary networks to automate geometric prompting, but introducing architectural overhead and limiting performance scalability. Although SAM3 natively supports concept segmentation via reusable text prompts, its direct use in medical imaging is hindered by a lack of fine-grained clinical knowledge and the ambiguity of human-written descriptions. In this work, we propose Mask to Concept (M2C), an efficient framework that adapts SAM3 for medical few-shot annotation without external modules, parameter retraining, or manual text engineering. Using only a few labeled images, M2C enables SAM3 to automatically search for transferable visual concepts entirely within its frozen architecture: it initializes a learnable concept embedding, uses it to prompt segmentation, and updates the embedding by gradients of minimizing the concept segmentation error. We further introduce a Hybrid Uncertainty Estimation (HUE) module that calculates the prediction entropy and maps concept predictions back to the box prompts, measuring concept-geometry prompting inconsistency. Highly uncertain samples are flagged actively for human correction, and the corrected masks are then fed back to M2C to continuously search for more precise concept embeddings, forming a self-enhancing annotation loop with minimal expert effort. Experiments on medical segmentation benchmarks show that our method achieves SOTA few-shot segmentation performance and outstanding annotation efficiency, offering a practical and efficient pathway toward scalable medical image labeling. Codes are at https://github.com/Huster-Hq/M2C.

</details>


### [89] [Extracting Neural Materials from Multi-view Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26715)
*Kim Youwang,Jon Hasselgren,Peter Kocsis,Andrea Weidlich,Tae-Hyun Oh,Jacob Munkberg*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NeuMatEx提出了一种可微逆渲染方法，通过训练大型材料重建模型（LMRM）预测初始神经材料潜码和不确定性引导，再结合逆路径追踪优化，从图像中提取高质量的空间变化神经材质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经材质能以紧凑通用基表示复杂镜面反射和散射效果，但获取和编辑此类材质仍然困难；且神经材质潜空间的非线性结构导致朴素逆渲染优化不可行。

Method: 1) 训练大型材料重建模型（LMRM），直接从图像预测初始基色、神经材料潜码和偶然不确定性引导；2) 利用该材料先验提供良好初始化并约束后续优化；3) 使用逆路径追踪进行优化；4) 利用预测的不确定性将高置信区域更紧密地锚定到LMRM预测，防止光照和复杂镜面效果被烘焙到材质中。

Result: 在合成和真实资产上的实验表明，NeuMatEx提取的复杂材质在视觉质量和材质分解上均优于基于PBR的方法。

Conclusion: 通过将数据驱动的材料先验与可微逆渲染相结合，NeuMatEx有效解决了神经材质提取的优化难题，实现了更高质量的材料分解。

Abstract: Neural materials can represent complex specular reflections and scattering effects in a compact, universal basis. However, acquiring and authoring such materials remains challenging. We present NeuMatEx, a differentiable inverse rendering method for extracting spatially varying neural materials from images. The nonlinear structure of neural material latent spaces makes optimization with naive inverse rendering infeasible. To address this, we train a Large Material Reconstruction Model (LMRM) that directly predicts initialbase color, neural material latents, and aleatoric uncertainty guides from images. This material prior provides a good initialization and better constrains our subsequent optimization using inverse path tracing. The predicted uncertainty further helps by anchoring high-confidence regions more tightly to the LMRM prediction, preventing lighting and complex specular effects from being baked into materials. Experiments on synthetic and real assets show that NeuMatEx extracts complex materials with better visual quality and material decomposition than PBR-based methods.

</details>


### [90] [Full spectrum Unlearnable Examples via Spectral Equalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26719)
*Jiale Cai,Gezheng Xu,Zhihao Li,Ruiyi Fang,Ruizhi Pu,Di Wu,Qicheng Lao,Charles Ling,Boyu Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 现有不可学习样例（UEs）的扰动信号主要集中在高频，一旦经过低通滤波就会失效。为此，本文提出全频谱不可学习样例（FUSE），通过频谱均衡策略生成频谱无关的扰动，使保护在全频段保持有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有UEs的扰动集中在高频，低频滤波后保护失效，缺乏全频谱鲁棒性。

Method: 提出FUSE方法，包含两个核心策略：1) 随机频谱掩码（RSM）：在生成器训练时随机移除一个连续频带，迫使剩余频带维持不可学习性；2) 跨频带引导（CBG）：强制高、低频分量之间保持一致性，增强低频不可学习性并调控高频扰动以保留语义保真度。

Result: 在多个数据集、网络架构和频谱滤波设置下，FUSE均展现出强大的保护效果。

Conclusion: 可靠的不可学习样例应覆盖全频谱，FUSE通过频谱均衡实现了这一目标，有效解决了现有UEs的低频脆弱性问题。

Abstract: Unlearnable examples (UEs) protect training data by injecting imperceptible perturbations so that models fail to extract exploitable representations. In this paper, we reveal that existing UEs exhibit a critical failure once low-pass filtering is applied, indicating that the effective perturbation signals for unlearnability concentrate predominantly in high frequencies. Hence, we argue that reliable UEs should remain effective across the full spectrum. To this end, we propose Full-spectrum Unlearnable examples via Spectral Equalization (FUSE), which aims to generate spectrum-agnostic perturbations by equalizing the contributions from different bands and enforcing cross-band consistency. Specifically, FUSE adopts a Random Spectral Masking (RSM) strategy during generator training, which randomly removes a contiguous frequency band, forcing the remaining bands to maintain unlearnability. In addition, FUSE further integrates Cross-Band Guidance (CBG), which enforces mutual consistency between high- and low-frequency components, thereby further enhancing low-frequency unlearnability and regulating high-frequency perturbations to preserve the semantic fidelity of images. Extensive experiments across multiple datasets, architectures, and spectral filtering demonstrate the strong protection achieved by FUSE.

</details>


### [91] [Robust Onion: Peeling Open Vocab Object Detectors Under Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26734)
*Priyank Pathak,Mukilan Karuppasamy,Aaditya Baranwal,Shruti Vyas,Yogesh S Rawat*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过受控合成视觉退化实验，系统分析了开放词汇目标检测器在真实噪声下的鲁棒性，发现模型鲁棒性主要由视觉骨干网络和图像域决定，而非标注或训练策略，并提出了轻量级NN & TK0方法提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于开放词汇目标检测器(OV-ODs)架构复杂，其在真实噪声下的鲁棒性表现尚不明确，缺乏系统性分析。

Method: 采用Robust Onion方法，通过受控合成视觉退化逐层剥离分析OV-ODs，研究特征坍塌的发生位置、原因及方式，并提出轻量级NN & TK0插件方法。

Result: 1) 视觉骨干网络相似的模型鲁棒性相似，受特征坍塌主导；2) 预训练策略、架构细节、文本监督影响小；3) 鲁棒性由图像域而非标注域决定；4) NN & TK0方法以仅端到端训练1/96的参数实现鲁棒性提升。

Conclusion: OV-ODs鲁棒性主要由视觉骨干和图像域决定，所提轻量级方法可有效提升真实场景鲁棒性，并解释了以往工作的鲁棒性观测现象。

Abstract: The impact of real-world noise on Open Vocabulary Object Detectors (OV-ODs) remains poorly understood due to their architectural complexity. We present our comprehensive analysis Robust Onion, an empirical study that uses controlled synthetic visual degradations to peel OV-ODs layer-by-layer, revealing how, why, and where robustness degrades, systematically analyzing feature collapse. Our findings reveal that models with similar vision backbones exhibit comparable robustness, driven by similar feature collapse at similar layers, while factors such as pretraining strategy, architectural nuances, and caption supervision contribute little. Robustness is primarily governed by the image domain rather than annotations, explaining the similar robustness impact on COCO and LVIS, and why datasets like ODinW-13 can give an impression of inflated robustness due to large, isolated objects. Finally, we validate our insights by improving robustness on real-world BDD100K, WiderFace, and VisDRONE via our lightweight plug-and-play NN & TK0 approach, using 96x fewer trainable parameters than end-to-end training. We also explain the prior works' robustness observations.

</details>


### [92] [Do Image Editing Models Understand Lighting?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26738)
*Tim Küchler,Johann-Friedrich Feiden,Matthias Nießner,Carsten Rother*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了3D锚定光探针(3DLP)基准，包含1K对真实世界灯光变化的高保真HDR图像数据集，用于评估生成式图像编辑模型对真实光照物理的理解能力。实验发现不同模型性能差异显著，最佳模型与真实物理相当吻合但仍有改进空间，而视觉语言模型不适用于像素级光传输分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式图像编辑模型虽能生成高视觉保真度的图像，但其是否具备真实世界光照的内在知识尚不明确。现有基准要么依赖VLMs或人工判断评估宏观光照合理性，要么使用合成数据集，缺乏对真实物理光照变化的细粒度、像素级评估方法。

Method: 构建3DLP基准：采集1K对真实室内场景HDR图像（灯光探针物理开关前后）；对图像区域进行细粒度标注（如投射阴影、金属表面）；提出两个新评分指标以补偿AI生成的摄影效果（如白平衡调整）；对多种SOTA图像编辑模型进行像素级光照一致性评估。

Result: 不同模型性能差异显著，镜面高光区域差异略小；最佳模型与真实物理高度一致但仍存在改进空间；所有模型在接收光探针光照较少的区域更容易出错；VLMs不适合像素级光传输分析。

Conclusion: 真实光照物理理解仍是生成式图像编辑的开放问题；现有模型虽表现良好但远非完美；基准和数据集将公开以推动该领域研究。

Abstract: While recent advancements in generative image editing models have achieved stunning visual fidelity, it remains an open question whether these systems possess an intrinsic knowledge of real-world lighting. Existing benchmarks typically evaluate high-level plausibility of perceptual light transport on curated internet imagery, using VLMs or human judgement, or they rely on synthetically generated datasets. In this work, we introduce the 3D-anchored Light Probe (3DLP) benchmark, for which we have captured a new high-fidelity HDR dataset of real-world lighting changes. The dataset consists of 1K image pairs of diverse indoor scenery in which light probes are physically turned on and off. To allow for a granular performance analysis, we annotated specific image regions such as cast shadows or metallic surfaces. With this data, we evaluate a range of state-of-the-art image editing models by measuring how well their light probe edits align with reality. The evaluation uses two new scores to compensate for AI-generated photographic effects, such as adjusted white balance. Our results show that the overall performance of models differs considerably, with differences slightly less pronounced for specular highlights. The best image editing models are remarkably consistent with real-world physics, however, they still leave room for improvement. We observe that image regions that receive less light from the light probe are more prone to errors for all models. Furthermore, building on their success in evaluating macroscopic lighting plausibility, we test VLMs on our task but find that they are unsuitable for pixel-level light transport analysis. We will make the benchmark, together with the real-world dataset, publicly available to encourage future research on this topic.

</details>


### [93] [LiveEdit: Towards Real-Time Diffusion-Based Streaming Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26740)
*Xinyu Wang,Chongbo Zhao,Fangneng Zhan,Yue Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种流式视频编辑框架，通过三阶段蒸馏流水线将双向基础模型的编辑能力迁移到高效单向流式编辑器，并结合AR掩码缓存机制，在保持高质量内容的同时实现12.66 FPS的实时推理速度，适用于交互式与增强现实场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流式视频编辑面临两大核心瓶颈：1) 难以在长时间编辑中保持背景和非编辑区域的稳定性；2) 无法满足实时交互场景所需的低延迟。同时，现有的流式视频生成方法主要为合成任务设计，因缺乏严格的内容保留和区域控制能力，无法直接应用于编辑任务。

Method: 1) 三阶段蒸馏流水线：将强双向基础模型的编辑能力逐步蒸馏到高效的单向流式编辑器中，实现因果帧级编辑；2) AR掩码缓存：面向增强现实场景，跨帧复用区域相关计算，减少冗余处理，加速推理；3) 建立流式视频编辑专用基准评测集。

Result: 在流式视频编辑基准上达到视觉质量最优，推理速度提升至12.66 FPS，满足交互式和增强现实应用需求。

Conclusion: 提出了一种兼具强内容保持与实时响应能力的流式视频编辑框架，通过蒸馏与掩码缓存两大核心设计突破了现有方法的性能瓶颈，为实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Streaming video editing has made rapid progress, yet practical deployment is still limited by two core issues: maintaining stable backgrounds and non-edited regions over time, and achieving the low latency required for real-time interactive scenarios. Meanwhile, recent streaming video generation methods are mostly developed for synthesis and cannot be directly applied to editing due to the strict preservation requirement and region-specific control. In this work, we present a novel streaming video editing framework that performs causal, frame-by-frame editing with strong content preservation and real-time responsiveness. Our key design is a three-stage distillation pipeline that progressively transfers editing capability from a powerful bidirectional foundation model to an efficient unidirectional streaming editor, enabling stable long-horizon edits without sacrificing visual fidelity. To further support real-time deployment, we introduce an AR-oriented mask cache that reuses region-related computation across frames, substantially reducing redundant processing and accelerating inference. Finally, we establish a dedicated benchmark for streaming video editing. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art visual quality among streaming baselines while drastically boosting inference speed to 12.66 FPS, making it suitable for interactive and augmented reality applications.

</details>


### [94] [Depth-Semantic Alignment and Affinity-Guided Fusion for Structured Radar Point Cloud Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26743)
*Amjad Hussain,Xin Qiu,Fuyuan Ai,Yuchen Tan,Zecheng Li,Chunyi Song,Wenjie Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于视觉-雷达融合的多模态点云生成方法，利用图像语义信息对雷达点云进行结构约束与空间对齐，并采用稀疏补全策略提升点密度、恢复缺失结构，实验证明该方法能有效提升点云质量及下游检测、跟踪任务的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 毫米波雷达点云通常稀疏、噪声大且结构不完整，这些问题严重制约了目标检测与跟踪等下游感知任务的性能。

Method: 提出多模态点云生成方法：1）利用图像语义信息对雷达点云施加结构约束并实现空间对齐；2）引入稀疏补全策略以增强点密度、恢复缺失结构。

Result: 在目标检测与跟踪任务中，所生成的点云有效提升了感知模型在复杂环境下的检测精度与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该方法为多传感器点云生成与智能感知系统提供了一种实用方案。

Abstract: Point clouds are an important carrier of three-dimensional spatial information, and their quality directly affects the performance of downstream perception tasks such as object detection and tracking. However, millimeter-wave radar point clouds are typically sparse, noisy, and structurally incomplete. To address these limitations, this paper proposes a multimodal point cloud generation method based on vision-radar fusion. The proposed method leverages image semantic information to impose structural constraints and achieve spatial alignment for radar point clouds, while incorporating a sparse completion strategy to enhance point density and recover missing structures. The generated point clouds are further evaluated in object detection and tracking tasks. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method effectively improves point cloud quality and enhances the detection accuracy and robustness of perception models in complex environments, providing a practical solution for multisensor point cloud generation and intelligent perception systems.

</details>


### [95] [Capacity-Controlled Multi-View Stylization of 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26754)
*Zhihao Wen,Yixin Yang,Bojian Wu,Yang Zhou,Dani Lischinski,Daniel Cohen-Or,Hui Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于最优传输的容量控制框架，用于3D高斯溅射的多视角风格化。通过半平衡最优传输问题中的列容量约束来避免多对一特征匹配，结合跨视角匹配引导和几何正则化，实现了跨视角风格一致且语义保持的3D风格化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D风格化方法在每渲染视图上独立应用2D特征匹配损失，导致风格分配不稳定、多对一特征重用以及跨视角一致性差的问题。

Method: 1) 将局部风格匹配重新表述为半平衡最优传输问题，引入可调强度的列容量约束，实现风格特征的可控分配；2) 提出跨视角匹配引导来约束场景内容与风格模式之间的对应关系；3) 引入几何正则化增强高斯基元，以表示更精细的纹理。

Result: 实验表明该方法显著提升了多视角风格一致性，生成了稳定且富有表现力的3D风格化结果，同时保留了场景的核心语义结构。

Conclusion: 基于最优传输的容量控制框架为解决3DGS风格化中的跨视角一致性问题提供了理论上有原则的方法，平衡了特征覆盖与风格多样性。

Abstract: While 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) provides an efficient and explicit representation for novel view synthesis, enforcing stylistic coherence across viewpoints remains challenging. Existing 3D stylization methods typically apply 2D feature-matching losses independently per rendered view, which leads to unstable style allocation, many-to-one feature reuse, and limited cross-view consistency. We propose a capacity-controlled framework for multi-view stylization of 3DGS, grounded in optimal transport. Specifically, we reformulate local style matching as a semi-balanced optimal transport problem. By introducing explicit column-capacity constraints with tunable strength, our formulation mitigates many-to-one matching and enables controllable allocation of style features. This transport-based objective provides a principled mechanism for balancing feature coverage and stylistic diversity while maintaining stable correspondences across viewpoints. To further enhance cross-view coherence, we incorporate a novel cross-view matching guidance to constrain correspondences between scene content and style patterns. In addition, we introduce several geometric regularizations to enhance the vanilla 3DGS, thereby enabling optimized Gaussian primitives to represent finer-grained textures during stylization. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach significantly improves multi-view stylistic consistency and produces stable, expressive 3D stylizations while preserving the core semantic structure of the scene.

</details>


### [96] [ProtoKV: Streaming Video Understanding under Delayed Query with Summary-State Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26762)
*Le Tu Ngoc Minh,Jinyeong Lim,Dongsu Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ProtoKV提出了一种恒定内存占用的流式视频理解方法，通过将远历史压缩为固定容量的语义-空间原型库（含残差统计），而非保留token实例，解决了延迟查询下关键线索被驱逐或稀释的问题。在相同预算下，ProtoKV在长延迟场景中准确率提升高达12.5个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式视频理解面临异步查询与连续视频流之间的冲突：在严格GPU内存和查询延迟约束下，关键判决线索可能短暂出现，但随后的大量更新会导致这些线索在查询到达前被逐出或稀释，现有方法难以在有限内存中保留长程依赖中的决定性信息。

Method: ProtoKV采用双区域设计：维护精确的近窗口KV缓存，同时将更早的历史内容聚合为一个固定容量的语义-空间原型库（含残差统计信息）。查询时，每个原型通过一个边界伪token接口暴露，该接口与标准注意力机制兼容，无需修改架构。

Result: 在相同内存预算和可比查询时间成本下，ProtoKV在长延迟场景的SVU基准测试上相比token保留基线方法准确率提升高达12.5个百分点，且增益随查询延迟增加而增大。

Conclusion: ProtoKV通过原型化远历史状态，有效解决了流式视频理解中延迟查询导致的关键信息丢失问题，验证了固定容量摘要状态相比保留token实例在长延迟场景下的显著优势。

Abstract: Streaming video understanding (SVU) must answer queries that arrive asynchronously while visual tokens stream continuously under strict GPU-memory and query-time latency budgets. A key challenge is delayed query: decisive cues may appear briefly, yet many subsequent updates occur before the query arrives, increasing the risk that those cues are evicted or diluted under bounded memory. We propose ProtoKV, a constant-footprint SVU memory that represents far history as a fixed-capacity summary state rather than retaining token instances. ProtoKV keeps an exact near-window KV cache and aggregates older content into a semantic-spatial prototype bank with residual statistics. At query time, each prototype is exposed through a bounded pseudo-token interface that is drop-in compatible with standard attention. Under matched budgets and comparable query-time cost, ProtoKV improves accuracy by up to 12.5 points over token-retention baselines on SVU benchmarks in the long-delay regime, with gains that grow as query delay increases.

</details>


### [97] [Calibrated Harmonic Overlaid Implicit Neural Representations for Multi-Dimensional Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26763)
*Honghang Chen,Xiujun Zhang,Xiaoli Sun,Mingqing Xiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为CHOIR的隐式神经表示方法，通过协调谐波叠加替代传统函数组合以解决深度网络优化不稳定问题，并引入感知频谱校准来缓解频谱偏差，在多维数据恢复任务上取得了领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有使用周期激活函数的隐式神经表示（如Sine）主要依赖函数组合，随着网络加深会导致优化不稳定；同时缺乏适当的物理先验来有效缓解频谱偏差。

Method: 提出CHOIR框架，包含两个核心组件：1) 协调谐波叠加（CHS），替代传统函数组合，确保网络深度增加时的优化稳定性；2) 感知频谱校准（PSC），嵌入自然图像的幂律频谱先验，将全局固定频谱校准为物理上合理的对数均匀分布。

Result: 在多维数据恢复问题上，该方法在性能上超越了当前最先进的方法。

Conclusion: CHOIR通过谐波叠加和频谱校准有效解决了深度周期网络的优化不稳定和频谱偏差问题，为多维数据隐式神经表示提供了新的有效范式。

Abstract: Implicit neural representation (INR) has emerged as a powerful prior for multi-dimensional data (e.g., multispectral images and videos). However, most INR methods employing periodic activation functions (e.g., Sine) predominantly rely on function composition. This mechanism introduces optimization instability as network depth increases, thereby limiting their performance. Meanwhile, these methods fail to incorporate proper physical priors to effectively alleviate spectrum bias. To address these issues, inspired by the commonalities between deep periodic networks and generalized Fourier series, we propose a novel Calibrated Harmonic Overlaid Implicit Neural Representation (CHOIR). Specifically, we utilize Coordinated Harmonic Superposition (CHS) to replace the conventional function composition used in most INRs, thereby ensuring optimization stability when scaling network depth. Furthermore, we introduce a Perceptual Spectrum Calibration (PSC) to mitigate spectrum bias. This calibration embeds the ubiquitous power-law spectrum prior of natural images and adjusts the globally fixed spectrum towards a physically plausible log-uniform distribution. Extensive experiments on various multidimensional data recovery problems demonstrate that our method achieves superior performance over state-of-the-art approaches. Code is available at https://github.com/chorl0229/CHOIR.

</details>


### [98] [Anatomy-Guided Residual Motion Diffusion for Controllable 4D Cardiac MRI Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26764)
*Yiheng Cao,Gustavo Andrade-Miranda,Jiatian Zhang,Lingxiao Zhao,Xin Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向4D医学影像的受控生成框架，通过级联潜扩散模型解耦解剖结构与时间动态，实现解剖一致的数据增强。在心脏电影MRI上验证，该框架能生成高可控性的静态解剖和强时间连贯性的4D序列，在跨厂商泛化实验中显著提升下游分割性能（Dice提升1.4%，Hausdorff距离减少3.0mm）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4D医学影像（3D+时间）中标注数据稀缺、设备间域偏移以及隐私限制严重制约了鲁棒AI模型的开发，现有方法难以生成解剖一致且时间连贯的合成数据。

Method: 采用半监督变分自编码器学习解剖体积的紧凑潜表征并联合预测对齐的分割掩膜，然后通过级联潜扩散模型解耦静态解剖与时间动态：静态LDM基于临床先验（诊断和体积测量）生成主体特异性解剖，运动LDM估计残差潜运动以确保4D序列的时间一致性。

Result: 在心脏电影MRI上，静态解剖控制达到Pearson r > 0.8，时间连贯性FVD=288.08。跨厂商实验中，使用nnU-Net时合成数据增强使平均Dice提升1.4%，Hausdorff距离减少3.0mm，左心室Dice提升2.8%，边界误差减少5.4mm。

Conclusion: 该框架为4D医学图像合成提供了可扩展且受控的解决方案，能够在标注有限和跨厂商变异性条件下支持更鲁棒模型的开发。

Abstract: Developing robust artificial intelligence models for 4D (3D + time) medical imaging is constrained by limited annotated data, inter-device domain shifts, and privacy restrictions. To address this, we propose a 4D controllable generative framework for anatomically consistent data augmentation. A semi-supervised variational autoencoder learns a compact latent representation of anatomical volumes while jointly predicting aligned segmentation masks in a unified framework. Anatomical structure is then disentangled from temporal dynamics through a cascaded latent diffusion model (LDM). A static LDM generates subject-specific anatomy conditioned on clinical priors (diagnosis and volumes measures) and a subsequent motion LDM estimates residual latent motions, ensuring strict temporal coherence across the 4D sequence. The proposed approach was evaluated on cine cardiac MRI as a representative 4D imaging application. Experiments across multiple datasets demonstrate high controllability of static anatomy (Pearson r > 0.8) and strong temporal coherence (FVD = 288.08). In cross-vendor generalization experiments, augmenting training sets with synthetic 4D sequences significantly improves downstream segmentation performance. Using nnU-Net, the proposed augmentation strategy improves the average Dice score by 1.4% and reduces the Hausdorff Distance by 3.0mm compared to training on real data alone, for the left ventricle, Dice improves by 2.8% with a 5.4mm reduction in boundary error. Overall, this framework provides a scalable and controllable solution for 4D medical image synthesis, supporting the development of more robust models with limited annotations and cross-vendor variability. Code available on https://github.com/cyiheng/4DCardiacMRISynthesis.

</details>


### [99] [LearniBridge: Learnable Calibration of Feature Caching for Diffusion Models Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26778)
*Xuyue Huang,Zhe Chen,Wang Shen,Xiao-Ping Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LearniBridge提出了一种基于可学习低秩校正机制的特征缓存方法，通过LoRA更新桥接多个时间步，仅需3-5个训练样本即可实现高效推理加速，在FLUX、HunyuanVideo和WAN2.1上分别达到5.87×、5.75×和4.10×加速比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Diffusion Transformers在图像和视频生成中计算成本极高。现有特征缓存方法在高加速比下存在严重的误差累积问题，需要探究最优校正更新的本质结构以解决这一限制。

Method: 首先证明了最优校正更新在不同提示词下共享低秩子空间。基于此结构洞察，提出LearniBridge，这是一个可学习的轻量级校正机制，通过LoRA更新桥接多个时间步，仅需3-5个训练样本即可实现有效校正。

Result: 在FLUX上实现5.87×加速，HunyuanVideo上5.75×加速，WAN2.1上4.10×加速。在WAN2.1上，VBench指标比先前SOTA提升1.28%（4.10×加速比下）。

Conclusion: LearniBridge通过可学习的低秩校正机制有效解决了高加速比下的误差累积问题，以极少训练样本实现了显著的推理加速，在图像和视频生成任务上均超越了现有方法。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) have driven substantial progress in image and video generation but suffer from prohibitive computational costs. Feature caching accelerates inference by reusing intermediate representations. Existing methods rely on historical features for implementation simplicity, yet suffer from severe error accumulation at high acceleration ratios. To address this limitation, we investigate the nature of the requisite feature correction. We demonstrate that the optimal calibration update is characterized by a shared low-rank subspace across diverse prompts. Guided by this structural insight, we propose LearniBridge, a learnable calibration mechanism for feature caching that bridges multiple timesteps through lightweight LoRA updates. This mechanism enables effective calibration requiring only 3-5 training samples. Extensive experiments on image and video generation show that LearniBridge achieves up to $5.87\times$, $5.75\times$, and $4.10\times$ acceleration on FLUX, HunyuanVideo, and WAN2.1, respectively. On WAN2.1, it improves VBench by 1.28% over the previous SOTA at $4.10\times$ acceleration. Our code is available at https://github.com/Iiiiiiirene/LearniBridge.

</details>


### [100] [Event-based Gaze Control System for Accurate Real-time Spin Estimation in Professional Ball Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26780)
*Yunpu Hu,Fabian Schilling,Valentina Cavinato,Asude Aydin,Agis Politis,Ricardo Tapiador Morales,Kirk Y. W. Scheper,Peter Dürr,Naoya Takahashi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于事件相机的主动视觉系统，可在真实比赛中实时跟踪未修改的球并测量其旋转。系统结合事件相机、高速振镜和可调焦镜头，通过离线球面对比度最大化方法（s-CMax）实现高精度自旋估计（平均大小误差2.1%，轴向误差4.0°），并开发了不确定性感知CNN与GPU加速相结合的在线方法，在职业乒乓球比赛中达到8.8%大小误差、6.4°轴向误差、3ms延迟和750Hz吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在球类运动中，旋转对球的轨迹有重要影响，但使用传统相机在比赛中基于视觉估计球的自旋极具挑战，原因在于球体小、速度快且旋转迅速。现有方法难以同时满足高速、高精度和实时性的需求。

Method: 系统采用事件相机（高时间分辨率、低运动模糊）、高速俯仰/偏转振镜（跟踪球）和低延迟可调焦长焦镜头（提高空间分辨率并保持对焦）。跟踪采用混合方法：2D事件检测用于居中，3D定位系统用于重初始化。离线自旋估计使用球面对比度最大化（s-CMax）；在线方法使用不确定性感知CNN（以离线结果为伪标签）结合GPU加速的批量对比度最大化进行精炼。

Result: 离线方法在多种球类（乒乓球、棒球、网球、高尔夫）上达到SOTA精度：平均大小误差2.1%，轴向误差4.0°。在线方法在职业乒乓球比赛中达到8.8%大小误差、6.4°轴向误差、3ms延迟、750Hz吞吐量。

Conclusion: 基于事件的主动视觉系统能够有效解决传统相机在球类运动自旋估计中的难题，在真实比赛场景中实现了高精度、低延迟、高吞吐量的实时自旋测量，为运动分析和体育科技提供了新的技术方案。

Abstract: Spin plays a crucial role in many ball sports due to its effect on the trajectory of the ball. Vision-based estimation of the ball's spin during a game with conventional cameras is challenging due to the ball's small size, high speed, and fast rotation. To address these challenges, we propose an event-based active vision system that can track unmodified balls and measure their spin in real-time. The system consists of an event camera for its high temporal resolution and minimal motion blur, high-speed pan/tilt galvanometer mirrors to keep the ball in the field of view, and a low-latency focus-tunable telephoto lens to increase the spatial resolution on the ball and keep it in focus. To track the ball, we use a hybrid approach that combines 2D event-based detection for centering and 3D positions from a ball localization system for re-initialization. For high-accuracy spin estimation, we propose an offline method that performs contrast maximization on the sphere (s-CMax). This method achieves state-of-the-art accuracy on static balls across multiple sports (table tennis, baseball, tennis, and golf), with mean magnitude and axis errors of 2.1% and 4.0 degrees, respectively. We then develop a low-latency online method for table tennis as a case study in real-time applications. This method uses an uncertainty-aware convolutional neural network trained on pseudo-ground-truth spin labels from the offline approach, combined with a GPU-accelerated batch implementation of contrast maximization for refinement. We demonstrate reliable tracking and spin estimation with a three-view setup during professional table tennis matches, with high accuracy (8.8% magnitude and 6.4 degrees axis mismatch), 3 ms latency, and 750 Hz throughput.

</details>


### [101] [ReasonCLIP-58M: Visually Grounded Commonsense Reasoning Supervision for CLIP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26794)
*Sicheng Zhang,Muzammal Naseer,Binzhu Xie,Naufal Suryanto,Shi Qiu,Jamal Bentahar,Naveed Akhtar,Mubarak Shah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ReasonCLIP-58M持续预训练框架，通过两阶段策略将大规模推理监督整合进CLIP风格模型，构建了ReasonLite-42M、ReasonPro-16M两个互补数据集和RCLIP-Bench基准，训练出的ReasonCLIP系列模型在视觉常识推理、组合推理和零样本检索上均有提升，作为LLaVA-NeXT等MLLM的即插即用视觉编码器能带来一致增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP及其变体在下游应用中对视觉常识推理和组合推理的需求日益增长，但现有CLIP风格的编码器是否能在不改变架构的情况下支持这类推理尚不明确。

Method: 提出ReasonCLIP-58M持续预训练框架，采用两阶段策略：第一阶段逐步整合推理信号同时保持描述性对齐，第二阶段引入类别结构化推理监督；并构建了开放形式推理描述数据集ReasonLite-42M和类别特定推理监督数据集ReasonPro-16M。

Result: ReasonCLIP系列模型在视觉常识推理、组合推理及零样本检索性能上均有提升，作为LLaVA-NeXT等MLLM的视觉编码器时无需额外推理成本即可带来一致性增益。

Conclusion: 结构化推理监督能够增强CLIP风格视觉表征的表达能力，且所提出的框架、数据集和基准均开源可用。

Abstract: CLIP and its variants are widely adopted visual backbones in multimodal systems, but their pretraining remains dominated by descriptive image-text alignment. As downstream applications increasingly demand visually grounded commonsense inference and compositional reasoning, it remains unclear whether CLIP-style encoders can support such reasoning without architectural changes. To address this, we present ReasonCLIP-58M, a continual pretraining framework that integrates large-scale reasoning supervision into CLIP-style models through our two-stage strategy, which progressively integrates reasoning signals while preserving descriptive alignment, followed by category-structured reasoning supervision. To support this framework, we construct two complementary datasets and a benchmark: ReasonLite-42M, with open-form, visually verifiable reasoning captions; ReasonPro-16M, with category-specific reasoning supervision; and RCLIP-Bench for diagnostic evaluation of visually grounded reasoning. We train a family of ReasonCLIP that improves visually grounded commonsense and compositional reasoning while also enhancing zero-shot retrieval performance. As a drop-in visual encoder for multimodal large language models such as LLaVA-NeXT, ReasonCLIP delivers consistent gains without additional inference cost, demonstrating that structured reasoning supervision enhances the expressive capacity of CLIP-style visual representations. All datasets, models, and training code are available at https://github.com/RISys-Lab/ReasonCLIP.

</details>


### [102] [NaviCache: Test-Time Self-Calibration Caching for Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26795)
*Zheqi Lv,Zhibo Zhu,Jinke Wang,Qi Tian,Shengyu Zhang,Zhengyu Chen,Chengxi Zang,Zhou Zhao,Fei Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出NaviCache，一种即插即用的测试时自校准方法，将特征演化重构思为惯性导航系统问题，通过双状态估计架构和不确定性感知的测量更新机制，实现有误差界的计算跳过加速视频扩散模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散模型计算成本巨大。离线校准的加速方法存在依赖校准数据、校准时间长、易受分布偏移影响等问题；离线无校准方法虽克服了这些障碍，但依赖瞬时零阶近似，容易受到观测噪声影响，且忽略了扩散轨迹中的内在动量。

Method: 将特征演化重构为惯性导航系统问题，引入双状态估计架构自适应跟踪特征变化比率及其潜在漂移，通过初始对齐阶段初始化，结合时间依赖的噪声调度和不确定性感知的测量更新机制，实现有误差界的计算跳过。

Result: 在HunyuanVideo、Wan和Open-Sora系列上的大量实验表明，NaviCache在计算跳过中表现出更准确的误差判断，并取得了卓越的综合性能。

Conclusion: NaviCache作为一种即插即用的测试时自校准方法，通过理论上的误差界机制有效加速视频扩散模型，在多个主流视频扩散模型上展示了优越性能。

Abstract: Video Diffusion Models (VDMs) is constrained by immense computational costs. While offline calibration-based acceleration suffers from calibration data dependency, prohibitive calibration duration, and susceptibility to distribution shifts, offline calibration-free methods eliminate these hurdles. However, since they rely on instantaneous zero-order approximations where the mapping between input and output differences varies in real-time, they are susceptible to observational noise and ignore the intrinsic momentum within the diffusion trajectory. In this paper, we propose NaviCache, a plug-and-play test-time self-calibration method re-conceptualizing feature evolution as an Inertial Navigation System (INS) problem. NaviCache bridges the fundamental domain gap and the non-stationary nature of diffusion by modeling the relative coupling between input and output variations. We introduce a dual-state estimation architecture that adaptively tracks the feature change ratio and its latent drift, initialized via a specialized Initial Alignment phase. By integrating a time-dependent noise schedule with an uncertainty-aware Measurement Update mechanism, NaviCache provides a theoretically grounded mechanism for error-bounded computation skipping. Extensive experiments on the HunyuanVideo, Wan, and Open-Sora series demonstrate that NaviCache exhibits more accurate error judgment for computation skipping and achieves outstanding comprehensive performance.

</details>


### [103] [Multi-modality Image Fusion under Adverse Weather: Mask-Guided Feature Restoration and Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26812)
*Xilai Li,Xiaosong Li,Haishu Tan,Tao Ye,Huafeng Li,Hongbin Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种掩码引导的多模态图像融合方法（AMG-Fuse），通过引入伪真实标签简化训练、设计掩码生成机制量化各模态贡献、结合掩码引导的交叉注意力机制选择性关注信息特征，并采用任务耦合的退化感知学习策略平衡特征恢复与交互，在合成与真实数据集上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在恶劣天气条件下，多模态图像融合面临图像退化导致特征表示受损、以及需要同时进行特征恢复和跨模态互补性学习的挑战，现有方法难以实现有效的表示学习，限制了实际性能。

Method: 1) 引入"伪真实标签"简化训练过程；2) 设计基于融合结果与源图像映射关系的掩码生成机制，量化各模态的相对贡献；3) 提出掩码引导的交叉模态交叉注意力机制，选择性关注信息特征；4) 提出掩码引导学习策略和任务耦合的退化感知学习策略，平衡特征恢复与交互。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法在视觉质量、定量指标和下游任务上均优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的掩码引导多模态图像融合方法有效解决了恶劣天气下的特征恢复与跨模态互补性学习问题，提升了融合性能。

Abstract: Multi-modality image fusion (MMIF) enhances scene representation by exploiting complementary cues from different modalities. Adverse weather, however, causes significant image degradation, disrupting feature representation and requiring simultaneous feature restoration and cross-modal complementarity. Existing methods often struggle with effective representation learning under such conditions, limiting their practical performance. To address these challenges, we propose a mask-guided MMIF method that integrates feature restoration and interaction. We first introduce "Pseudo Ground Truth" to simplify training, promoting faster and more effective feature learning. Then, we design a mask generation mechanism based on the mapping relationship between the fused result and the source images, quantifying the relative contribution of each modality during the fusion process. By incorporating the proposed mask-guided cross-modal cross-attention mechanism, the network is encouraged to selectively attend to informative features during modality interaction, mitigating the risk of overfitting to the static distribution of the "Pseudo Ground Truth". Additionally, we propose a mask-guided learning strategy and a task-coupled degradation-aware learning strategy to balance feature restoration and interaction. Extensive experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that our method surpasses state-of-the-art approaches in visual quality, quantitative metrics, and downstream tasks. The source code is available at https://github.com/ixilai/AMG-Fuse.

</details>


### [104] [SpatialFlow-GRPO: Where Spatial Credit Drives Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26872)
*Yankai Yang,Yancheng Long,Wei Chen,Xingyu Lu,Hongyang Wei,Bin Wen,Fan Yang,Tingting Gao,Han Li,Shuo Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SpatialFlow-GRPO，通过引入空间细粒度奖励反馈解决图像编辑中全图奖励无法区分不同区域贡献的问题，在多个 benchmarks 上超越 Flow-GRPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Flow-GRPO-style方法依赖单一全图奖励，存在空间均匀性假设，无法区分不同空间区域对图像质量的贡献，导致细粒度编辑优化困难。

Method: 提出SpatialFlow-GRPO框架：将区域感知奖励转化为语义区域级优化信号，在策略更新时将区域优势与对应潜在位置对齐；训练区域感知奖励模型SFReward，构建SFReward-14K数据集，引入MultiEditBench评估多区域编辑能力。

Result: 在OmniGen2和FLUX.2-klein-4B上，SpatialFlow-GRPO在GEdit-Bench、ImgEdit-Bench和MultiEditBench上均优于Flow-GRPO。

Conclusion: SpatialFlow-GRPO将局部反馈转化为空间对齐的更新信号，有效提升了图像编辑质量。

Abstract: Recent online reinforcement learning has substantially improved image editing quality. However, existing Flow-GRPO-style methods usually rely on a single whole-image reward, which makes fine-grained editing optimization difficult. We observe that a key obstacle in image editing is this spatial uniformity assumption: a whole-image reward cannot distinguish how different spatial regions contribute to image quality. To address this issue, we propose SpatialFlow-GRPO, a training framework that introduces spatially fine-grained reward feedback.
