Method: 设计五层评估框架：1) 目标Checkov规则移除；2) 全扫描器清洁度；3) Terraform计划有效性；4) 计划行为变更对比；5) 人类裁决。对68个真实模块和28个注入缺陷模块，使用gemini-2.5-flash-lite、GPT-4o和Claude 3.5 Sonnet生成修复，进行系统评估。引入四维欺骗性修复分类法。

Result: 目标移除率83.3%但全扫描清洁度仅10.4%；计划成功39.6%，计划对比可达38.5%；人类裁决显示71.4%为欺骗性修复；三个模型欺骗性修复率57.1%-71.4%，无统计显著差异；CKV2_AWS_11案例中通配符Resource权限全部保留。

Conclusion: 现有LLM辅助Terraform安全修复评估存在严重夸大，欺骗性修复是普遍现象。TerraProbe提供了可靠的多层评估方法，揭示了"通过扫描即成功"的评估标准需要被更严格的意图对齐评估取代。

Abstract: Security misconfigurations in Terraform Infrastructure-as-Code are a growing risk in cloud deployments, and large language models are increasingly used as automated repair agents. Existing evaluations often treat a repair as successful when the targeted static-analysis finding disappears, without checking planning validity, behavioral change, or security intent. This paper presents TerraProbe, a five-layer oracle framework for evaluating LLM-assisted Terraform security repair. We apply TerraProbe to 288 first-pass repairs generated by gemini-2.5-flash-lite, GPT-4o, and Claude 3.5 Sonnet across 68 real-world TerraDS modules and 28 controlled injected-defect modules. The results show that targeted Checkov removal overstates repair success. Although targeted removal reaches 83.3 percent for the primary model, full-scanner cleanliness drops to 10.4 percent, Terraform planning succeeds for 39.6 percent, and plan comparison is reachable for 38.5 percent. Human adjudication further shows that 71.4 percent of plan-compared real-world repairs are deceptive fixes that pass automated checks while leaving the underlying vulnerability in place. This pattern is statistically indistinguishable across the three models, with deceptive-fix rates from 57.1 percent to 71.4 percent and pairwise Fisher exact p-values above 0.10. The paper introduces a four-dimensional taxonomy of deceptive fixes, validated with Cohen kappa of 0.78 and Krippendorff alpha of 0.76. IAM permission analysis confirms that wildcard Resource grants persist in all nine CKV2 AWS 11 deceptive-fix cases. TerraProbe contributes an evaluation methodology, a replication package, and the Multi-Layer Oracle Evaluation framework for distinguishing intent-aligned security repairs from scanner-passing false successes.

</details>


### [211] [Sketched Linear Contrastive Learning: Approximation, Optimization, and Statistical Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26617)
*Ziyan Chen,Zhongzhu Zhou,Ding-Xuan Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在草图线性模型下推导了对比表示学习的标度律，给出了风险分解并分析了模型大小、数据量和优化计算之间的显式标度关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标度律描述了学习性能如何随模型大小、数据大小和计算量变化。尽管近期理论工作已建立了草图线性回归的标度律，但对对比表示学习的标度行为理解尚不充分，本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 在配对高斯潜变量设置下，构建草图线性模型进行对比学习；观察两个相关变量的草图视图，通过全批量经验梯度下降训练双线性对比分数；采用高斯负二次对比替代损失，结合对齐幂律谱和对比源条件进行分析。

Result: 推导了关于草图维度M、样本量N和有效优化视界L_effγ的显式标度律；将风险分解为不可约风险、逼近误差、GD偏差、GD方差和交叉项，其中交叉项由偏差和方差控制不影响上界标度；与传统线性回归标度律相比，对比设置需学习双视图交互，改变了优化和有限样本噪声的标度方式。

Conclusion: 本文推导了对比表示学习在草图线性模型下的标度律，揭示了在双视图交互场景中，模型大小、数据量和优化计算之间的权衡关系，其中交叉项受偏差和方差控制不影响上界标度，而对比设置改变了优化和有限样本噪声随模型、数据和训练时间的标度方式。

Abstract: Scaling laws describe how learning performance varies with model size, data size, and compute. While recent theoretical work has established scaling laws for sketched linear regression, much less is understood for contrastive representation learning. In this paper, we study a sketched linear model for contrastive learning under a paired Gaussian latent-variable setup. The learner observes only sketched views of two correlated variables and trains a bilinear contrastive score by full-batch empirical gradient descent. We analyze a Gaussian-negative quadratic contrastive surrogate under aligned power-law spectra and a contrastive source condition, where we derive a risk decomposition into irreducible risk, approximation error, GD bias, GD variance, and a cross term. The cross term is controlled by the bias and variance and therefore does not affect the upper-bound scaling. Our main theorem gives an explicit scaling law with respect to sketch dimension $M$, sample size $N$, and effective optimization horizon $L_{\mathrm{eff}}γ$. Compared with standard linear-regression scaling laws, the contrastive setting must learn interactions between two views, and this changes how optimization and finite-sample noise scale with model size, data, and training time. This provides a first theoretical step toward understanding scaling behavior in contrastive learning and gives guidance for balancing model size, data, and optimization compute.

</details>


### [212] [Discovering Millions of Interpretable Features with Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26620)
*XinYang He,Wei Wang,Bing Zhao,Xuan Ren,WenBo Li,WeiXu Qiao,Hu Wei,Lin Qu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍Qwen3-Instruct SAE，一套针对Qwen3指令微调模型家族训练的稀疏自编码器，覆盖1.7B、4B和8B三个规模，在残差流、MLP输出、注意力输出等多个激活位置训练逐层SAE，并系统评估了其稀疏性与保真度之间的权衡，最后通过拒绝行为引导案例展示了其因果干预能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏自编码器（SAE）虽然能有效分解语言模型的叠加表示为稀疏可解释特征，但训练SAE计算成本高昂，且目前可用的开源SAE模型仍然有限，因此需要构建并公开高质量的大规模SAE资源。

Method: 在Qwen3-1.7B和Qwen3-4B上，在残差流、MLP输出和注意力输出三个激活位点逐层训练SAE；在Qwen3-8B上，在残差流部分层训练SAE。使用激活级重建指标和模型级恢复指标进行系统评估。

Result: 揭示了不同层和不同组件之间稀疏性与保真度的不同权衡关系；通过拒绝行为引导案例表明，选定的SAE特征能够因果性地引导指令微调Qwen3模型产生拒绝行为。

Conclusion: 该工作为研究指令微调语言模型中的稀疏表示、特征级机制和行为干预提供了实用的开源资源。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) have emerged as a powerful tool for decomposing superposed language model representations into sparse and interpretable features. However, training SAEs is computationally expensive, and available open-source SAE models remain limited. In this work, we introduce \textbf{Qwen3-Instruct SAE}, a comprehensive suite of SAEs trained on the Qwen3 instruction-tuned model family, covering Qwen3-1.7B, Qwen3-4B, and Qwen3-8B. For Qwen3-1.7B and Qwen3-4B, we train layer-wise SAEs at three key activation sites: residual streams, MLP outputs, and attention outputs. For Qwen3-8B, we train SAEs on a subset of residual stream layers. We systematically evaluate these SAEs using both activation-level reconstruction metrics and model-level recovery metrics, revealing distinct sparsity--fidelity trade-offs across layers and components. Finally, we demonstrate the utility of Qwen3-Instruct SAE through a refusal-steering case study, showing that selected SAE features can causally steer instruction-tuned Qwen3 models toward refusal behavior. Our release provides a practical resource for studying sparse representations, feature-level mechanisms, and behavioral interventions in instruction-tuned language models

</details>


### [213] [From Weights to Features: SAE-Guided Activation Regularization for LLM Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26629)
*Evan Ning,Wei Xue,Dong Lou,Yike Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出在激活空间中使用预训练稀疏自编码器（SAE）作为单语义特征字典进行正则化，以解决大型语言模型在持续学习中因多语义性问题导致EWC等权重空间正则化方法效果不佳的问题。该方法无需存储先前任务数据，在特征空间进行保护，比权重空间方法更高效，在TRACE和MedCL基准上取得了最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有权重空间正则化方法（如EWC）在大型语言模型的持续学习中表现不佳，作者认为部分原因是LLM的"多语义性"——每个权重参与多种功能，基于权重的保护过于粗糙，无法有效隔离需要保护的知识。

Method: 利用预训练的稀疏自编码器（SAE）将模型激活映射到单语义特征空间，在特征空间中进行正则化。从约束优化角度推导新损失函数，使用SAE特征字典显式平衡稳定性与可塑性；当前任务数据用于计算紧凑的SAE特征掩码，后续训练仅保留该掩码，无需重放先前任务数据。

Result: 在TRACE和MedCL持续学习基准上，该方法在不引入任务特定架构组件的前提下取得最强结果，超越EWC等传统权重空间正则化方法。实证表明任务相关表示在SAE特征基中线性可分，而在权重基中与随机无异，权重空间保护在概念层面几乎无选择性。

Conclusion: 激活空间正则化相比权重空间正则化更适合LLM的持续学习，SAE提供的高维单语义特征字典能更精细地隔离和保护知识，且该方法无需存储历史数据、内存效率更高。

Abstract: Weight-space regularization methods such as Elastic Weight Consolidation (EWC) are the standard approach to catastrophic forgetting in continual learning. However, those methods tend to underperform when applied to large language models. We argue that such underperformance can be partly explained by the ``polysemantic'' nature of large language models: per-weight importance estimates utilized by EWC-style regularization are too coarse and cannot isolate the knowledge that needs protection. In this paper, we propose regularizing instead in the model's activation space, using pretrained Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) as a monosemantic feature dictionary. From the perspective of constrained optimization, we derive a new loss function that uses the SAE feature dictionary to explicitly balance stability and plasticity, and show that EWC is a special case in the one-sided weight-space penalty setting. Unlike replay-based methods that store or revisit examples from earlier tasks, our method requires no previous-task data after mask construction: current-task data is used to compute a compact SAE feature mask, and only this mask is retained for later training. Further, since the feature space has significantly lower dimensionality than the parameter space, the proposed method is more memory efficient. On the TRACE and MedCL continual learning benchmarks, the method achieves the strongest result among approaches without introducing task-specific architectural components, also surpassing traditional weight-space regularization methods like EWC. Beyond performance comparisons, we provide empirical evidence for the polysemanticity thesis: task-relevant representations are linearly separable in the SAE feature basis but indistinguishable from chance in the weight basis, and weight-space protection is nearly non-selective at the concept level.

</details>


### [214] [Target-Aware Bandit Allocation for Scalable Surrogate Optimization in Chemical Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26657)
*Mohammad Haddadnia,Yuvan Chali,Abhilash Jayaraj,Constance Kraay,Joana Reis,Felix Strieth-Kalthoff,Haribabu Arthanari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BOBa是一种基于多臂老虎机引导的代理优化框架，通过将动作空间分区并自适应分配计算资源，消除了全库推理的瓶颈，在超大规模分子库虚拟筛选中实现了筛选性能与推理成本之间的可调平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在结构药物发现等场景中，从海量离散空间（数十亿至万亿级化合物）中识别高效用候选物面临昂贵评估的挑战。现有代理优化虽能提高样本效率，但全库代理推理本身已成为主要计算瓶颈。

Method: 提出BOBa框架，将动作空间划分为多个分区，每个分区视为多臂老虎机的一个臂，通过自适应分配计算资源，将推理和评估集中在经验上有前景的分区上，同时保持原则性探索。

Result: 在真实合成按需库上的实验表明，基于不确定性乐观原则的老虎机算法与有意义的动作空间分区相结合，对于推理和评估的有效分配至关重要。发现筛选性能与代理推理成本之间存在可调的权衡关系。

Conclusion: BOBa框架支持对当前超大型库进行实用优化，并为超大规模虚拟筛选建立了一条可行路径。

Abstract: Identifying high-utility candidates from massive discrete spaces under expensive evaluations is a recurring challenge across the sciences, with structure-based drug discovery as a prominent example. While surrogate-based optimization can increase sample efficiency by reducing the number of expensive evaluations, modern molecular libraries have reached billions to trillions of compounds, making full-library surrogate inference itself a major computational bottleneck. We introduce BOBa, a bandit-guided surrogate optimization framework that eliminates full-library inference by adaptively allocating computation across partitions of the action space. By treating partitions as arms in a multi-armed bandit, BOBa concentrates inference and evaluations on empirically promising partitions while maintaining principled exploration. Experiments on real-world synthesis-on-demand libraries demonstrate that optimism-under-uncertainty bandits, combined with meaningful action space partitioning, are essential for effective allocation of inference and evaluations. Our findings reveal a tunable tradeoff between screening performance and surrogate inference cost, which supports practical optimization over current libraries, and establishes a viable route to ultra-large library virtual screening.

</details>


### [215] [Reproducibility Study of "AlphaEdit: Null-Space Constrained Knowledge Editing for Language Models"](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26783)
*Ananth K S,Arya Hariharan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对知识编辑方法AlphaEdit进行了可重复性研究，成功复现了其在原始模型上的指标，但发现三个扩展问题：(1) 在新模型架构上优势不通用；(2) 长序列编辑下性能显著下降；(3) 大规模编辑会损害下游任务能力和安全行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证AlphaEdit的可重复性，并系统评估其在更广泛场景（新架构、更大编辑规模、额外下游基准）下的泛化能力和局限性。

Method: 在原始实验设置下复现AlphaEdit结果，然后沿三个维度扩展评估：新模型架构（检查定位-编辑范式的前提假设是否成立）、更长的序列编辑长度（超出原始论文的编辑数量）、以及三个额外下游基准（BoolQ、HellaSwag、XSTest）的性能和安全行为评估。

Result: 成功复现了原始指标，但发现流畅性和一致性指标存在差异；AlphaEdit的优势在新架构上不普及，原因是违背了定位-编辑范式的架构假设；在远超原始规模的编辑次数下，性能显著退化，表明零空间投影的保护是有界的；大规模编辑会损害下游任务能力和安全相关的拒绝行为。

Conclusion: AlphaEdit在其原始范围内表现如报告所述，但其核心理论保证对模型架构和编辑规模敏感，实际部署时需考虑这些局限性。

Abstract: Fang et al. (2025) introduced a null-space constrained projection, named AlphaEdit, for locate-then-edit knowledge editing methods, theoretically guaranteeing that edits do not disrupt previously preserved knowledge, and reports substantial gains over existing editing methods on LLaMA3, GPT2-XL, and GPT-J. In this work, we present a reproducibility study of AlphaEdit, reproducing its reported results under the original experimental setup and extending the evaluation along three axes: new model architectures, additional downstream benchmarks, and substantially longer sequential editing horizons. We successfully reproduce AlphaEdit's reported metrics across the original models, though we identify a discrepancy in the reported fluency and consistency metric. Extending AlphaEdit to newer model families, we find that its advantage does not generalize uniformly, which we trace to architectural assumptions in the locate-then-edit paradigm that are violated by these newer models. We further stress-test AlphaEdit's central sequential-editing claim by extending the number of edits well beyond those evaluated in the original paper, and find that performance, which is stable at the originally reported scale, degrades as edits reach a much higher count, indicating that the null-space projection's protection against catastrophic forgetting is bounded rather than unconditional. Finally, we extend evaluation of edited models on three extra benchmarks, namely, BoolQ, HellaSwag, and XSTest, and we find that large-scale sequential editing degrades both general downstream task competence and safety-relevant refusal behavior. Our results confirm that AlphaEdit performs as reported within its original scope, while showing that its core theoretical guarantees are sensitive to model architecture and editing scale in ways that have practical implications for its deployment.

</details>


### [216] [AIGP: An LLM-Based Framework for Long-Term Value Alignment in E-Commerce Pricing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26787)
*Chennan Ma,Yanning Zhang,Siqi Hong,Xiuchong Wang,Fei Xiao,Keping Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AIGP框架，利用大语言模型结合领域知识、结构化数据和文本上下文进行可解释的定价决策，通过监督微调、离线强化学习训练的长效价值评估器(LTVE)和直接偏好优化(DPO)实现与长期业务目标对齐，在淘宝工厂大规模实验中GMV提升13.21%、ROI提升7.59%、里程碑达成率提升8.20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统大规模电商动态定价模型存在可解释性差、非结构化信息利用不足、与累计GMV、ROI和里程碑达成等长期业务目标对齐不足的问题。

Method: 1) 使用LLM结合领域知识、结构化数据和文本上下文进行定价决策；2) 采用监督微调进行知识蒸馏以实现高效部署；3) 提出LTVE（通过离线强化学习在历史数据上训练）作为奖励模型；4) 利用LTVE评分选择偏好对进行DPO训练，使定价策略与长期目标对齐。

Result: 在淘宝工厂的大规模在线A/B测试中，相比生产基线，GMV提升13.21%，ROI提升7.59%，14天里程碑达成率提升8.20%，同时提供可解释的定价理由。

Conclusion: AIGP框架有效解决了传统定价模型在可解释性、信息利用和长期目标对齐方面的不足，在大规模电商场景中取得了显著的业务指标提升。

Abstract: Traditional dynamic pricing models in large-scale e-commerce suffer from limited interpretability, poor utilization of unstructured information, and misalignment with long-term business objectives such as cumulative Gross Merchandise Value (GMV), Return on Investment (ROI) and milestone achievement. We propose AIGP, a novel framework that leverages a Large Language Model (LLM) prompted with domain knowledge, structured data and textual context to make interpretable, knowledge-aware pricing decisions. For efficient deployment while maintaining high-quality outputs, we employ supervised fine-tuning for knowledge distillation. Central to AIGP is the Long-Term Value Estimator (LTVE), trained via offline reinforcement learning on historical data, which serves as a reward model to score candidate pricing actions and select preference pairs for Direct Preference Optimization (DPO), thereby aligning the pricing policy with long-term business objectives. Extensive offline evaluations and large-scale online A/B tests on Tao Factory demonstrate that AIGP achieves significant improvements: +13.21% in GMV, +7.59% in ROI, and +8.20% in milestone achievement rate over 14 days compared to the production baseline, while simultaneously providing interpretable and transparent pricing rationales.

</details>


### [217] [Reasoning Quality Emerges Early: Data Curation for Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26797)
*Hongyi Henry Jin,Wenhan Yang,Meysam Ghaffari,Carlos Morato,Baharan Mirzasoleiman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种仅利用初始推理token即可识别高质量监督微调数据的方法，通过在预训练模型的随机扰动检查点上评估前100个推理token的loss来检测难题，并利用前1k推理token的loss模式在多个扰动检查点上的相似性来确保梯度一致性。在M23K医学推理和OpenThoughts-Math数据集上，该方法在Qwen2.5-7B和Llama3.1-8B模型上以91%的token效率提升实现了最高1.7%的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高质量SFT数据筛选方法严重依赖强推理模型基于多样性和难度过滤样本，导致筛选成本高昂且数据质量次优。

Method: 利用预训练模型随机扰动检查点上的初始推理token（前100个）的loss检测难题；利用前1k推理token在多个扰动检查点上的loss模式相似性确保梯度一致性。

Result: 在M23K和OpenThoughts-Math数据集上，Qwen2.5-7B和Llama3.1-8B模型性能提升最高1.7%，token效率提升91%。

Conclusion: 仅利用初始推理token即可高效识别高质量推理数据，无需依赖强推理模型进行昂贵筛选。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on a small, high-quality set of long reasoning traces is an effective approach for eliciting strong reasoning capabilities in Large Language Models (LLMs). However, existing methods for curating high-quality SFT data rely heavily on strong reasoning models to filter examples based on diversity and difficulty, making the curation process costly while often yielding suboptimal data quality. In this work, we show that diverse and challenging reasoning examples can be identified using only the initial reasoning tokens. Specifically, we demonstrate that difficult problems can be reliably detected based on the loss of the first 100 reasoning tokens evaluated at a randomly perturbed checkpoint of the pretrained model. We further show that examples exhibiting similar loss patterns over their first 1k reasoning tokens across a small number of perturbed checkpoints extrapolating along the fine-tuning trajectory provably induce similar gradients. We validate our approach through extensive experiments on fine-tuning Qwen2.5-7B and Llama3.1-8B models on the M23K medical reasoning and OpenThoughts-Math datasets. Our method outperforms existing baselines by up to 1.7% while being 91% more token efficient.

</details>


### [218] [Just how sure are you? Improving Verbalized Uncertainty Calibration in Medical VQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27023)
*Eren Senoglu,Federico Toschi,Nicolo Brunello,Andrea Sassella,Mark James Carman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对多模态大语言模型在医学VQA中过度自信的问题，提出了一种基于训练的校准框架，通过复合损失函数（含Brier校准项、锚定正则化、对比图文对齐、KL模型稳定项）微调模型，在三个医学VQA基准和两种架构上将校准误差降低60%以上，判别力提升26%以上，同时保持预测准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在医学视觉问答中倾向于产生过度自信的输出，而现有的口头置信度校准方法主要针对纯文本LLM开发，未能考虑医学图像理解的多模态特性。

Method: 提出基于训练的微调框架，使用复合损失函数：(1) Brier风格的校准项；(2) 锚定正则化防止置信度坍缩；(3) 对比图像-文本对齐项，通过2×2因子扰动设计（图像存在性与文本完整性交叉）探测模型对视觉模态与语言先验的依赖；(4) KL模型稳定项；以及(5) Top-K KL散度正则化保护模型回答能力。

Result: 在三个医学VQA基准和两种架构（MedGemma 4B IT和Qwen2 VL 7B Instruct）上，校准误差降低60%以上，判别力提升26%以上，且保持预测准确性。在平均性能上优于基于提示、基于采样和基于训练的方法，消融实验证实损失函数各组件均不可或缺。

Conclusion: 所提出的训练框架能有效改善多模态大语言模型在医学VQA任务中的置信度校准，代码已公开。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) applied to Medical Visual Question Answering (VQA) tend to produce overconfident outputs regardless of actual correctness, and existing verbalized confidence calibration methods, developed primarily for text only LLMs, do not account for the multimodal nature of medical image understanding.
  This work proposes a training based framework that finetunes MLLMs to improve their calibration using a composite loss function combining a Brier style calibration term, an anchor regularizer that prevents confidence collapse toward extreme values, a contrastive image text alignment term, and a KL based model stabilization term. The alignment signal is derived from a $2 \times 2$ factorial perturbation design that crosses image presence with text integrity, probing the reliance of the model on visual modality input versus language priors. Finally, a top K KL divergence regularizer is used to protect the answering ability of the model during finetuning.
  Across three Medical VQA benchmarks and two architectures (MedGemma 4B IT and Qwen2 VL 7B Instruct), our method reduces calibration error by 60% or more, and improves discrimination by 26% or more, while preserving predictive accuracy. On average across benchmarks, the technique outperforms prompting based, sampling based, and training based approaches, and ablation experiments confirm that each component of the loss function is indeed necessary for improving the calibration. All code for the experiments is publicly available.

</details>


### [219] [Quantization in Federated Learning: Methods, Challenges and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26822)
*Farwa Ikram,Dipanwita Thakur,Antonella Guzzo,Giancarlo Fortino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次从联邦学习特有维度（如客户端异质性、聚合一致性、通信调度自适应、非独立同分布鲁棒性、隐私/安全集成、硬件/能耗协同优化）对量化技术进行了系统性综述，将量化从单纯的压缩技术重新定义为影响联邦学习性能、鲁棒性和实用性的基础系统组件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习虽已成为隐私保护分布式智能的基础范式，但其可扩展性受到通信瓶颈、设备异质性和统计非独立同分布数据训练的严重制约。量化是缓解这些限制的最有效机制之一，但缺乏从联邦学习特有角度出发的系统性综述。

Method: 提出了一种围绕联邦学习特有维度组织的新型分类法，包括客户端异质性、聚合一致性、通信调度自适应、非独立同分布鲁棒性、隐私/安全集成和硬件/能耗协同优化。系统梳理了现有量化方法，并分析了量化与客户端漂移、部分参与、收敛稳定性、安全聚合和差分隐私等核心联邦学习行为之间的交互关系。

Result: 识别了交叉方法洞察、开放研究空白，并为在移动、物联网和边缘平台上部署量化联邦学习的实践者提供了设计指南。

Conclusion: 量化不仅是压缩技术，更是塑造现代联邦学习性能、鲁棒性和实用性的基础系统组件。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) has become a foundational paradigm for privacy-preserving distributed intelligence, yet its scalability remains fundamentally constrained by communication bottlenecks, device heterogeneity, and the challenges of training under statistically non-IID data. Quantization is one of the most effective mechanisms for mitigating these limitations, reducing both uplink/downlink payloads and on-device computation. This paper provides the first FL-centric systematic review of quantization, introducing a novel taxonomy organized around FL-specific dimensions, including client heterogeneity, aggregation consistency, communication-scheduling adaptation, non-IID robustness, privacy/security integration, and hardware/energy co-optimization. Beyond cataloging existing methods, we analyze how quantization interacts with core FL behaviors such as client drift, partial participation, convergence stability, secure aggregation, and differential privacy. We further identify cross-method insights, open research gaps, and design guidelines for practitioners deploying quantized FL on mobile, IoT, and edge platforms. This survey thus establishes quantization not merely as a compression technique, but as a fundamental systems component shaping the performance, robustness, and practicality of modern FL.

</details>


### [220] [Asymptotically Optimal Learning for Parametric Prophet Inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26893)
*Jung-hun Kim,Anna Grebennikova,Vianney Perchet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了指数型参数族（包含指数分布、Pareto分布、有界支撑幂族分布）中独立同分布回报的先知不等式问题，刻画了最优全信息渐近竞争比（无界支撑时为 $(θ/(θ-c_+))^{c_+/θ}/Γ(1-c_+/θ)$，有界支撑时为1），并提出了一种基于置信度的动态规划在线学习策略，无需外部离线样本即可达到相同的最优渐近竞争比，同时给出了典型分布下的收敛率与数值实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在解决参数未知的指数型参数族中先知不等式的在线学习问题，期望在不依赖离线样本的前提下，实现在线策略的最优渐近竞争比，填补已有研究在参数化指数型分布族上的理论空白。

Method: 首先从理论上刻画了该参数族在全信息情形下的最优渐近竞争比，然后设计了一种基于置信度的动态规划在线学习策略，利用显式的参数化结构仅通过在线观测数据自适应地逼近最优策略，并推导了典型分布下的收敛速率。

Result: 无界支撑情形下最优渐近竞争比为 $(θ/(θ-c_+))^{c_+/θ}/Γ(1-c_+/θ)$，有界支撑情形下为1；所提在线学习策略无需离线样本即可达到该最优竞争比，并在合成数据上验证了算法性能。

Conclusion: 本文成功将先知不等式从非参数化设置推广到参数化指数型分布族，证明了在线学习策略可以匹配全信息最优渐近性能，为实际应用提供了可操作的理论保证与算法框架。

Abstract: We study learning in prophet inequalities with i.i.d. rewards drawn from an exponential-type parametric family with an unknown parameter $θ$, a class that includes exponential, Pareto, and bounded-support power-family distributions. We first characterize the optimal full-information asymptotic competitive ratio for this family. In the unbounded-support case, the limit is $ {\left(θ/({θ-c_+})\right)^{c_+/θ}}/ {Γ(1-c_+/θ)},$ while in the bounded-support case, the limit is $1$. We then propose a confidence-based dynamic-programming policy for online learning. By exploiting the explicit parametric structure, the policy achieves the same optimal asymptotic competitive ratio using only online observations, without external offline samples. We further derive distribution-specific convergence rates for canonical examples. Finally, numerical experiments on synthetic instances illustrate the performance of our algorithm.

</details>


### [221] [Hallucination in World Models is Predictable and Preventable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27326)
*Nicklas Hansen,Xiaolong Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出世界模型中的幻觉本质是数据覆盖问题，通过MMBench2数据集和三种幻觉检测信号，结合覆盖感知采样和好奇心驱动的数据收集，仅需50条真实轨迹即可将预训练世界模型微调至全新环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代生成式世界模型虽能生成逼真且动作可控的未来帧，但常出现幻觉——视觉流畅却偏离真实动力学。作者假设幻觉集中在状态-动作空间的低覆盖区域，轻量级数据驱动信号可同时检测并指导缓解。

Method: 构建427小时、210任务的MMBench2数据集，训练350M参数世界模型；识别三种幻觉模式（感知、动作边缘化、场景发散），开发对应预测信号；提出覆盖感知采样技术（训练时）和基于好奇心奖励的在线数据收集策略（微调时）以弥合覆盖缺口。

Result: 三种幻觉预测信号能准确预测模型失败位置；覆盖感知采样提升训练效率；以好奇心奖励指导数据收集，仅需50条真实环境轨迹即可将预训练世界模型适配到全新环境。

Conclusion: 世界模型中的幻觉本质是数据覆盖问题，用于检测幻觉的信号同样可用于缓解；通过数据驱动的方式可高效弥合覆盖缺口，实现少样本跨环境迁移。

Abstract: Modern generative world models render increasingly realistic action-controllable futures, yet they frequently hallucinate: rollouts remain visually fluent while drifting from the ground-truth dynamics. We hypothesize that hallucination concentrates in low-coverage regions of the state-action space, where lightweight data-centric signals can both detect it and guide mitigation. To test this, we introduce MMBench2, a 427-hour, 210-task dataset for visual world modeling with ground-truth actions, rewards, and live simulators, and train a 350M-parameter world model on it. We identify three distinct hallucination modes: perceptual, action-marginalized, and scene-diverging -- each anchored to a different stage of the pipeline, and develop three signals that accurately predict where the model will fail. To close coverage gaps at training time, we develop a coverage-aware sampling technique; to close them online, our hallucination predictors serve as curiosity rewards for targeted data collection, yielding a data-efficient finetuning recipe that adapts the pretrained world model to entirely unseen environments with as few as 50 real environment trajectories. Overall, our findings reveal that hallucination in world models is inherently a data coverage issue, and that the same signals used to detect it can also be used for mitigation.
  An interactive web version of our paper is available at https://www.nicklashansen.com/mmbench2

</details>


### [222] [GEOALIGN: Geometric Rollout Curation for Robust LLM Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26917)
*Ting Zhou,Zhenqing Ling,Yiyang Zhao,Ying Shen,Daoyuan Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Geoalign方法，通过检测和修正方向不一致的rollout来解决在线RL训练LLM时的奖励噪声问题，提升训练稳定性和最终性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线强化学习训练大语言模型时，奖励信号存在噪声或规格错误会导致训练不稳定。作者发现一种称为"方向不一致性"的失败模式：批次中少数高奖励rollout在表征空间中产生的偏好方向与多数rollout严重冲突，导致高方差和破坏性更新。

Method: Geoalign包含三个步骤：(i) 在prompt内形成偏好对；(ii) 学习在线投影仪，基于每个rollout的隐藏状态集中奖励排序的位移方向；(iii) 通过rollout与批次共识原型之间的角度偏差检测方向不一致的rollout，并用同一prompt内的稳定替代方案进行修正。该方法仅需前向传播，开销极小。

Result: 在对话对齐（使用学习到的奖励模型）和数学推理（使用二值验证奖励）任务上，Geoalign提升了最终性能并减少了训练震荡，优于PF-PPO、PAR、PODS和Seed-GRPO等基线方法。

Conclusion: 潜空间中的方向共识可以作为在线LLM强化学习中的有效可靠性信号，Geoalign通过修正方向不一致性实现了更稳定、更优的对齐训练。

Abstract: Online reinforcement learning is widely used to align large language models (LLMs) with reward signals, yet training can be unstable under noisy or misspecified rewards. We identify a failure mode we call directional inconsistency: within a batch, a small set of high-reward rollouts induces representation-space preference directions that sharply disagree with the batch majority, resulting in high-variance and destabilizing updates. We propose geoalign, a lightweight plug-in for rollout curation in iterative policy optimization. Geoalign (i) forms within-prompt preference pairs, (ii) learns an online projector on per-rollout hidden states to concentrate reward-ordered displacement directions, and (iii) detects directionally inconsistent rollouts via their angular deviation from a batch consensus prototype and rectifies them with within-prompt stable alternatives. Geoalign is forward-pass only and adds negligible overhead. Across dialogue alignment with a learned reward model and mathematical reasoning with binary verified rewards, Geoalign improves final performance and reduces training oscillation, outperforming PF-PPO, PAR, PODS, and Seed-GRPO. These results suggest latent directional consensus as an effective reliability signal for online LLM RL.

</details>


### [223] [Error-Conditioned Neural Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27354)
*Haina Jiang,Liam Wang,Peng-Chen Chen,Min Seop Kwak,Seungryong Kim,Brian Bell,Jeong Joon Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出误差条件神经求解器（ENS），将PDE残差场作为网络输入而非优化目标，使网络通过迭代学习修正自身预测。在四个PDE族中，ENS在大部分设定下达到最高预测精度，对湍流Kolmogorov流提升达10倍，且能零样本泛化到分布外参数变化和跨方程迁移，尤其在残差最小化不可靠的病态系统中优势最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经代理模型将求解视为纯统计任务，训练后难以纠正自身约束违反和分布外泛化；混合方法虽通过梯度下降或高斯-牛顿步针对PDE残差优化，但继承经典优化器的高计算成本和不稳定性，且在病态系统中残差最小化与重建精度之间不可靠。

Method: 提出误差条件神经求解器（ENS）：不是将PDE残差作为优化目标，而是将残差场作为网络的直接输入，使其读取自身误差的空间结构，学习迭代更新策略以逐步修正预测。

Result: 在四个PDE族中，ENS在大部分设定下达到最高预测精度，对湍流Kolmogorov流提升达10倍；避免混合方法的高计算成本；学习到的修正策略在分布偏移下具有零样本泛化能力，包括参数变化和跨方程迁移，在病态系统中相对优势最大。

Conclusion: 理论上和实验上证明数值最小化PDE残差在病态系统中可能不可靠，而ENS通过将残差作为输入而非目标，实现了更高的预测精度、更好的泛化能力和更低的计算成本。

Abstract: Neural surrogate models offer fast approximate mappings from PDE parameters to solutions, but they typically treat solving as a purely statistical task: once trained, they struggle to correct their own constraint violations and extrapolate beyond the training distribution. Recent hybrid methods promote physical correctness by targeting the PDE residual via gradient descent or Gauss--Newton steps, but inherit the compute cost and instability of the underlying classical optimizers. We show, theoretically and empirically, that numerically minimizing the PDE residual can be an unreliable proxy for reconstruction accuracy in ill-conditioned systems, explaining why these methods often do not make accurate predictions despite achieving low residuals. We propose error-conditioned Neural Solvers (ENS), built on a different principle: rather than an optimization target, the PDE residual field is passed as a direct input to the network at each iteration, enabling it to read the spatial structure of its own errors and learn an update policy to iteratively correct its predictions. Across four PDE families, ENS attains the highest prediction accuracy in the large majority of settings, with gains reaching $10\times$ on turbulent Kolmogorov flow, while avoiding the expensive compute cost of hybrid methods. ENS's learned correction policy generalizes under distribution shift, including zero-shot parameter changes and cross-equation transfer, where its relative advantage is largest in the ill-conditioned regimes where residual minimization is least reliable. Project website: https://neuralsolver.github.io/.

</details>


### [224] [Decision-Aligned Evaluation of Uncertainty Quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26990)
*Annika Schneider,Tommy Rochussen,Joshua Stiller,Vincent Fortuin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"决策对齐"标准，揭示现有不确定性量化（UQ）评估指标（如负对数似然、预期校准误差）与下游决策效用之间的错位问题，并提出了与决策对齐的先验加权效用指标（一种特殊类型的适当评分规则），实验证明该指标比传统指标更能反映实际决策效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不确定性估计评估指标（如NLL、ECE）虽然在通用指标上表现良好，但它们与下游决策的实际效用之间可能存在严重错位，导致模型选择不当或决策效果不佳。

Method: 引入"决策对齐"准则来检验评估指标是否与下游决策效用一致；基于此框架证明广泛使用的UQ指标要么与常见决策问题错位，要么编码了病态的先验信念；提出先验加权效用指标作为一类决策对齐的评估指标。

Result: 在基准实验和真实世界案例研究中，所提出的先验加权效用指标始终与实际决策效用保持一致，而传统指标则未表现出这种一致性。

Conclusion: 当前UQ评估协议存在缺陷，需要向决策相关的UQ评估方向扩展；先验加权效用指标为现有指标提供了原则性的改进方案。

Abstract: Uncertainty estimates in machine learning are typically evaluated using generic metrics such as the negative log-likelihood and expected calibration error, yet good performance on such metrics does not necessarily imply high utility in downstream decisions. We introduce decision-alignment, a criterion that reveals which evaluation metrics meaningfully align with downstream utilities. Applying this framework, we show that many widely used uncertainty metrics are either misaligned with common decision problems or encode pathological prior beliefs about the downstream task. We then propose prior-weighted utility metrics, a special class of proper scoring rules that provides decision-aligned uncertainty evaluation. Across benchmark experiments and real-world case studies, our metrics consistently align with realized decision utility, while conventional metrics do not. Our results surface flaws in the current UQ evaluation protocol and offer a principled extension of existing metrics toward decision-relevant UQ evaluation.

</details>


### [225] [Uncertainty quantification via conformal prediction in data assimilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27001)
*Catherine George,Alireza Javanmardi,Tijana Janjić,Eyke Hüllermeier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究在理想化一维修正浅水模型中，系统比较了三种保形预测（CP）方法（标准CP、归一化CP、保形分位数回归）与经典集合方法在不确定性量化上的表现，并探索了CP不确定性在数据同化循环中的集成应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数值天气预报中，量化不确定性的演化对概率预报和数据同化至关重要，传统集合方法存在局限，需要探索新的机器学习方法来补充或改进现有不确定性量化手段。

Method: 以一维修正浅水模型（模拟对流过程）为理想化测试平台，对比三种CP变体与集合标准差/集合离散度等传统指标，评估指标包括平均经验覆盖、平均区间长度、漏低率、漏高率和平均区间分数损失（AISL），并研究了CP扰动在数据同化循环中的集成方式。

Result: 揭示了每种CP方法在简化的理想化大气模型中的优势与局限性；CP能够为传统集合不确定性量化提供有效补充。

Conclusion: 保形预测作为一种新兴的ML不确定性量化工具，在简化大气模型中具有应用潜力，可作为传统集合方法的补充，但各变体表现存在差异，需根据具体需求选择。

Abstract: Quantifying the evolution of uncertainty is critical to both probabilistic forecasting and data assimilation in numerical weather prediction. In this study, we investigate the applicability of conformal prediction (CP), a recent machine learning (ML) method, to quantify uncertainty in a controlled, idealized setting. We use the one dimensional modified shallow water model, designed to mimic the convective process. CP provides a set of possible outcomes with a chosen confidence level. Here, we compare and evaluate the average empirical coverage, the average interval length, miss low, miss high and average interval score loss (AISL) for three variants of CP, namely a) Standard CP, b) Normalized CP and c) Conformalized Quantile Regression. We further compare these CP-based uncertainty estimates with traditional ensemble-based measures such as standard deviation intervals and ensemble spread. In addition, we investigate the integration of CP-derived uncertainty within the data assimilation cycle through CP perturbations. Our results highlight the strengths and limitations of each approach, providing insight into the effectiveness of CP to complement common ensemble-based uncertainty quantification in simplified atmospheric models.

</details>


### [226] [A Generalization Theory for JEPA-Based World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27014)
*Jingyi Cui,Qi Zhang,Hongwei Wen,Yisen Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次为基于JEPA的世界模型建立了泛化理论，将其预训练形式化为条件谱图学习问题，证明了JEPA目标等价于动作条件共现矩阵的低秩分解，并建立了预训练误差与下游规划遗憾之间的联系，揭示了潜在维度在近似误差和样本误差之间的内在权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEPA作为一种有前景的世界建模范式，在经验上取得了成功，但其理论基础尚不充分，缺乏对其泛化性能的理论理解。

Method: 将JEPA预训练形式化为条件谱图学习问题；证明JEPA目标等价于动作条件共现矩阵的低秩分解；建立预训练误差与下游规划遗憾之间的理论联系；推导有限样本泛化界。

Result: 获得了JEPA世界模型的有限样本泛化界；揭示了潜在维度上近似误差与样本误差之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 为理解潜在预测模型相比输入级预测方法的优势和局限性提供了理论洞见。

Abstract: Joint Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) have recently emerged as a promising paradigm for world modeling by learning predictive dynamics in a latent space rather than generating future observations at the input level. Despite their empirical success, the theoretical understanding of JEPA-based world models remains limited. In this paper, we develop the first generalization theory for JEPA-based world models. We formulate JEPA pretraining as a conditional spectral graph learning problem and show that the JEPA objective is equivalent to a low-rank factorization of an action-conditioned co-occurrence matrix. Building on this characterization, we establish a connection between JEPA pretraining error and downstream planning regret, leading to a finite-sample generalization bound for JEPA-based world models. Our analysis reveals an inherent trade-off between approximation and sample errors with respect to the latent dimension, providing theoretical insights into the advantages and limitations of latent predictive models compared with input-level predictive approaches.

</details>


### [227] [Symplectic Neural Networks for learning Generalized Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27029)
*Harsh Choudhary,Vyacheslav Kungurtsev,Chandan Gupta,Melvin Leok,Georgios Korpas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用隐式辛积分器的伴随系统对称离散化来高效训练Hamiltonian神经网络（HNN），通过预测-校正ODE求解器和不动点迭代降低计算成本，并在不可分混沌系统中展示了能量守恒与系统辨识方面的数值优势，同时通过后向误差分析获得更精确的修正Hamiltonian。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从含噪声的状态变量观测中识别系统Hamiltonian是一项挑战，而使用隐式辛积分器虽然能保真地反映Hamiltonian系统的长期行为（尤其是能量守恒），但其计算强度大且通过ODE求解器反向传播困难，亟需一种高效训练HNN参数的方法。

Method: 利用辛离散化伴随系统与反向传播产生相同灵敏度的性质，实现隐式辛积分器下的HNN高效训练；采用预测-校正ODE求解器和不动点迭代缓解隐式时间步进的计算成本；结合后向误差分析对学习到的Hamiltonian进行后处理以获得更精确的修正Hamiltonian。

Result: 在不可分混沌系统上的实验表明，该方法在系统辨识和能量保持方面具有数值优势，且计算和内存复杂度更高效；后向误差分析得到的修正Hamiltonian比直接学习到的Hamiltonian更接近真实Hamiltonian。

Conclusion: 隐式辛积分器结合伴随灵敏度方法可以有效训练HNN，在保证几何结构保持的同时实现高效计算，后向误差分析进一步提升了Hamiltonian的近似精度。

Abstract: Hamiltonian Neural Networks (HNNs) integrate physical priors into neural models by learning a system's Hamiltonian, improving generalization and sample efficiency. Identifying the system Hamiltonian from noisy observations of state variables is a challenging task. For simulations to faithfully reflect the long-term behavior of Hamiltonian systems, especially energy conservation, it is essential to use symplectic integrators, which preserve the system's geometric structure. This fidelity comes at a cost: implicit symplectic integrators are more computationally intensive and make backpropagation through the ODE solver non-trivial. However, by leveraging the fact that symplectic discretizations of the adjoint system yield the same sensitivities associated by backpropagation, we obtain an efficient method of training the Neural Network parameters. In our work, we explore this alternate method of HNN training under noisy observation of trajectories with our HNN model based on an implicit symplectic integrator. Computationally, a predictor-corrector based ODE solver and fixed point iteration help to mitigate the computational cost of the implicit timestepping, resulting in more efficient generation of gradient updates. We showcase the numerical advantage, in experiments, in system identification and energy preservation on a range of non-separable, chaotic systems and the efficient computation and memory complexity of our method. We also observe that the post-processing of the learned Hamiltonian using backward error analysis yields a modified Hamiltonian that is a more accurate approximation of the true Hamiltonian without the need to use more accurate discretizations of the flow map.

</details>


### [228] [State Representation Matters in Deep Reinforcement Learning: Application to Energy Trading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27032)
*Jesper Klicks,Sander Vržina,Vincent François-Lavet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究抽水蓄能交易中强化学习智能体的状态表征设计，发现将绝对价格特征、近期相对价格特征和短期预测特征三者结合，能显著提升策略在跨市场和跨区域迁移中的鲁棒性，单一特征族效果较差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 储能交易决策不仅依赖当前市场价格，还受未来市场预期和运行约束影响，因此强化学习中状态表征的设计至关重要，但目前缺乏系统研究。

Method: 在HydroDam抽水蓄能套利环境中，使用固定的Double DQN智能体，保持环境、动作空间、奖励函数、网络和训练协议不变，仅改变市场特征。比较了绝对价格/日历特征、相对特征（当前价格与近期市场历史的对比）、预测特征及其组合。使用2007-2011年比利时日前电价训练，在2012-2025年同市场和39个其他ENTSO-E市场区域测试。

Result: 绝对特征仅在测试集达28.8%，跨区域中位数5.7%；相对和预测特征单独使用也低于滚动价格评分基线。组合特征性能大幅提升：绝对+相对在测试集达49.9%，跨区域中位数39.8%；绝对+相对+预测达55.6%和47.5%。

Conclusion: 状态表征在储能交易强化学习中不是次要预处理步骤，而是策略设计的核心部分；鲁棒迁移需要结合价格尺度、近期相对价格背景和短期预测信息，而非依赖单一特征族。

Abstract: Energy trading decisions depend not only on current market prices, but also on expected future market conditions, and operational constraints. This makes the state representation given to a reinforcement learning agent an important design choice. We study this in HydroDam, a pumped-storage arbitrage environment, using a fixed Double DQN agent. The environment, action space, reward function, network, and training protocol are kept fixed; only the market features are changed. We compare absolute price/calendar features, relative features that compare current prices with recent market history, forecast features, and all combinations of these three feature families. Policies are trained and selected using 2007--2011 Belgian day-ahead prices and evaluated on two test settings: a later same-market test set from 2012--2025 and 39 other ENTSO-E market zones. Absolute features only reaches 28.8% on the test set and a median 5.7% across zones. Relative-only and forecast-only states also stay below a rolling price-score heuristic in the cross-zone median. Combining feature families is much stronger: absolute + relative reaches 49.9% on the test set and a 39.8% cross-zone median, while absolute + relative + forecast reaches 55.6% and 47.5%. These results suggest that state representation is not a minor preprocessing choice in storage-trading RL, but a central part of the policy design: robust transfer requires combining price scale, recent relative price context, and short-horizon forecast information, rather than relying on any single feature family.

</details>


### [229] [Finding Stationary Points by Comparisons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27082)
*Helin Wang,Chenyi Zhang,Xiwen Tao,Yexin Zhang,Tongyang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了仅通过比较预言机（给定两点，输出函数值较大者）访问非凸函数时寻找驻点的问题。针对二阶可微且具有Lipschitz梯度和Hessian的函数，提出了一种算法，使用$\widetilde O(n^2/ε^{1.5})$次查询即可找到$ε$-驻点。进一步在量子比较预言机模型下，提出了首个量子算法，查询复杂度为$\widetilde O(n/ε^{1.5})$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在许多实际场景中，函数值难以精确获取，只能通过比较获得相对大小信息。研究在这种受限信息下（比较预言机）如何高效优化非凸函数，具有理论意义和实际价值。此外，探索量子计算能否进一步加速这一过程。

Method: 经典算法：设计了一个子程序，用$\widetilde O(n^2\log(1/δ))$次查询将归一化Hessian矩阵估计到精度$δ$，并基于此构建了寻找$ε$-驻点的算法。量子算法：利用量子比较预言机的叠加查询能力，实现了查询复杂度的平方级加速。

Result: 经典算法查询复杂度为$\widetilde O(n^2/ε^{1.5})$，量子算法查询复杂度为$\widetilde O(n/ε^{1.5})$。

Conclusion: 在比较预言机模型下，非凸优化的查询复杂度与维度$n$和精度$ε$相关；量子计算可在此问题上实现多项式加速。

Abstract: We study the problem of finding stationary points of non-convex functions when access to the objective is provided only through a comparison oracle that, given two points, outputs which has the larger function value. For a twice differentiable $f\colon\mathbb R^n\to\mathbb R$ with Lipschitz gradient and Hessian, we develop an algorithm that visits an $ε$-stationary point using $\widetilde O(n^2/ε^{1.5})$ queries. Our approach uses a subroutine that estimates the normalized Hessian to accuracy $δ$ using $\widetilde O(n^2\log(1/δ))$ queries. We further study this problem with a quantum comparison oracle model where queries can be made in superpositions, and develop the first quantum algorithm that finds an $ε$-stationary point, which takes $\widetilde O(n/ε^{1.5})$ queries.

</details>


### [230] [SOLAR: AI-Powered Speed-of-Light Performance Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26383)
*Qijing Huang,Sana Damani,Zhifan Ye,Athinagoras Skiadopoulos,Siva Kumar Sastry Hari,Jason Clemons,Sahil Modi,Jingquan Wang,Aditya Kane,Edward C Lin,Humphrey Shi,Christos Kozyrakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SOLAR是一个自动从PyTorch/JAX源码推导速度极限(SOL)分析框架，结合LLM和确定性组件，能够计算未融合、融合和缓存感知的理论最小执行时间，实现零违规验证，支持多保真度分析，并在KernelBench、JAX/Flax模型和机器人工作负载上展示了四种应用场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在目标硬件上的理论最小执行时间（速度极限）分析对软硬件和算法优化至关重要，但现有SOL推导过程依赖手动操作、容易出错且与快速模型开发脱节，因此需要自动化框架来填补这一空白。

Method: SOLAR框架包含三个核心组件：1) LLM前端将PyTorch/JAX源码转换为可执行的Affine Loop IR，通过输出比对验证正确性；2) 确定性流程将IR提升为einsum图；3) 分析后端计算未融合、融合和缓存感知的SOL界限。框架提供多保真度分析能力。

Result: SOLAR实现了全面的算子和语言覆盖，产生经过验证的SOL界限且零违规，支持的用例包括多保真度余量分析、优化机会识别、跨平台探索和逆屋顶线硬件配置。

Conclusion: SOLAR通过自动化SOL分析流程，将理论极限分析与实际模型开发连接起来，为机器学习系统优化提供了系统化的分析工具。

Abstract: How fast could a deep-learning model run on target hardware, and how far is today's implementation from that limit? These questions are central to software, hardware, and algorithm optimizations. Speed-of-Light (SOL) analysis answers them by computing a workload's theoretical minimum execution time on a given architecture. Yet deriving SOL bounds remains manual, error-prone, and disconnected from rapid model development. To close this gap, we introduce SOLAR, a framework that automatically derives validated SOL bounds from PyTorch and JAX source code. SOLAR leverages both generative and deterministic components in its flow: an LLM frontend translates any source programs into an executable Affine Loop IR, validated by output comparison; a deterministic flow lifts the IR into an einsum graph; and an analytical backend computes unfused, fused, and cache-aware SOL bounds. SOLAR provides comprehensive operator and language coverage, produces validated bounds with zero observed SOL violations, and offers multi-fidelity analysis that tightens bounds and surfaces optimization insights. We evaluate SOLAR across KernelBench, JAX/Flax models, and robotics workloads. These experiments demonstrate four use cases: headroom analysis at multiple fidelity levels, identifying optimization opportunities, cross-platform exploration, and inverse-roofline hardware provisioning.

</details>


### [231] [Data-Free Reservoir Features for Efficient Long-Horizon Cold-Start Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27095)
*Augustinas Jučas,Yangchen Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CIRCLE方法，使用固定的双向二维储备池特征和流式线性判别分析头进行冷启动无样本类增量学习，在多个标准数据集上超越现有方法，尤其在长任务序列上表现优异且训练速度快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有冷启动无样本类增量学习方法存在计算张力：漂移补偿方法需要重复训练骨干网络，随任务增长更新成本越来越高；冻结骨干方法虽廉价但在冷启动下表现较弱。本文探索第三种选择：使用完全不拟合图像数据的固定特征提取器。

Method: 提出CIRCLE方法，使用固定的双向二维储备池特征（BiRC2D）作为特征提取器，结合流式线性判别分析（SLDA）头。通过集成多个随机储备池实例形成特征集成，并平均独立SLDA头的softmax输出，实现可调节的偏差-方差权衡。特征提取器固定，头支持流式闭式更新，无需回放、任务边界信息或骨干反向传播。

Result: 在CIFAR-100、TinyImageNet、ImageNet-Subset和ImageNet-1k上，CIRCLE在10-20任务分割下表现具有竞争力，在50、100和500任务分割下显著优于强CS-EFCIL基线，且训练速度远快于基于训练骨干的漂移补偿方法。

Conclusion: BiRC2D风格的特征提取器、SLDA头和平衡的特征/预测集成各自对最终性能有贡献，表明固定特征加流式分类头的组合是冷启动增量学习的一种有效且高效的新范式。

Abstract: Cold-start exemplar-free class-incremental learning requires learning a growing set of classes without replay, external pretraining, or a large initial task. Existing cold-start methods typically either train the backbone throughout the stream and compensate for semantic drift, or freeze a backbone after the first task, producing features biased toward the initial classes. These choices also create a computational tension: drift-compensation methods require repeated backbone training and increasingly expensive updates as the task horizon grows, while frozen-backbone methods are cheap but weak under cold start. We study a third option: a feature extractor that is never fit to image data at all. We propose CIRCLE, a class-incremental classifier built from fixed bidirectional two-dimensional reservoir features, adapted from BiRC2D for image classification, and streaming linear discriminant analysis heads. CIRCLE groups multiple random reservoir instantiations into feature ensembles and averages the softmax outputs of independent SLDA heads, yielding a tunable bias-variance tradeoff between richer random features and prediction-level ensembling. Because the feature extractor is fixed and the head admits streaming closed-form updates, CIRCLE performs sample-wise training without replay, task-boundary information, or backbone backpropagation. On CIFAR-100, TinyImageNet, ImageNet-Subset, and ImageNet-1k, CIRCLE is competitive at 10-20 task splits and substantially outperforms strong CS-EFCIL baselines at 50, 100, and 500 task splits, while training much faster than trained-backbone drift-compensation methods. Ablations show that the BiRC2D-style extractor, SLDA head, and balanced feature/prediction ensembling each contribute to the final performance.

</details>


### [232] [Transformer-Based Classification of Bacterial Raman Spectra with LOOCV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27096)
*Jamile Mohammad Jafari,Thomas Bocklitz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了Transformer模型在拉曼光谱分类中的性能，使用嵌套留一复制交叉验证框架，在包含5,417个单细胞光谱的细菌数据集上，Transformer consistently outperformed traditional machine learning methods (PCA/ICA with LDA, SVM, RF), 显示出更好的类别分离和原始数据鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer模型在拉曼光谱分类中的应用日益受到关注，但缺乏系统性的评估和与传统方法的公平比较，尤其是在考虑测量复制的真实场景下。

Method: 采用嵌套留一复制交叉验证框架，系统比较Transformer与传统机器学习流程（PCA/ICA结合LDA、SVM、随机森林），在包含6种细菌、9个独立测量复制、5,417个单细胞光谱的数据集上进行评估。

Result: Transformer在所有独立测试复制中持续达到最高分类性能，显著优于传统方法；学习到的潜在特征空间比PCA和ICA表示具有更好的类别分离；直接应用于未经预处理的原始拉曼光谱时仍保持优越性能。

Conclusion: Transformer模型在鲁棒的拉曼光谱分类中具有巨大潜力，同时强调了复制感知验证对于现实模型评估的重要性。

Abstract: Transformer-based models have recently attracted increasing attention for Raman spectral classification. In this study, a transformer-based approach was systematically evaluated using a nested leave-one-replicate-out cross-validation framework and compared with conventional machine-learning pipelines combining PCA or ICA with LDA, SVM, and Random Forest classifiers. A bacterial Raman dataset comprising 5,417 single-cell spectra from six bacterial species and nine independent measurement replicates was used. The transformer consistently achieved the highest classification performance across independent test replicates and significantly outperformed all conventional approaches. Analysis of the learned latent feature space revealed improved class separation compared with PCA- and ICA-based representations. Furthermore, the transformer maintained superior performance when applied directly to raw Raman spectra without preprocessing, demonstrating robust behavior across measurement replicates. These findings highlight the potential of transformer-based models for robust Raman spectral classification and emphasize the importance of replicate-aware validation for realistic model evaluation.

</details>


### [233] [Heavy-Ball Q-Learning with Residual Weighting Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27112)
*Donghwan Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种修正的重球动量Q-learning方法，基于切换线性系统和联合谱半径分析框架，证明了其收敛性以及在特定条件下比标准Q-learning更快的收敛速度，并将该方法扩展到线性函数逼近场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Q-learning方法收敛速度有限，本文旨在通过引入重球动量技术加速Q-learning，并建立严格的理论收敛保证。

Method: 采用切换线性系统（SLS）表示Q-learning算法，利用联合谱半径（JSR）分析框架，提出修正的重球动量Q-learning方法，并将其推广至线性函数逼近场景。

Result: 证明了修正的重球Q-learning方法的收敛性，给出了其比标准Q-learning收敛更快的理论条件，并在线性函数逼近下获得了类似的收敛与加速结论。

Conclusion: SLS视角为理解重球动量如何加速Q-learning提供了新的分析框架和补充性见解。

Abstract: This paper proposes a corrected heavy-ball Q-learning method for reinforcement learning (RL) and establishes its convergence. It also identifies conditions under which the method is theoretically guaranteed to converge faster than standard Q-learning. The same construction is then extended to Q-learning with linear function approximation, where analogous convergence and acceleration statements are derived. The analysis is based on a switched linear system (SLS) representation of Q-learning algorithms and on the joint spectral radius (JSR) of the associated switching families. This SLS viewpoint is not commonly used in standard analyses of Q-learning, and it provides a complementary framework and new insight into how heavy-ball momentum can accelerate Q-learning.

</details>


### [234] [Cross-Head Attention Uplift Network with Inverse Propensity Score under Unobserved Confounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27114)
*Haoran Zhang,Chuanpu Li,Yuxin Fu,Bin Tong,Guan Wang,Bo Zheng,Feng Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CHAUN（交叉头注意力提升网络）和RA-IPS（鲁棒对抗逆概率加权）方法，分别解决提升建模中组间相似性利用不足和未观测混杂下的偏差问题。CHAUN通过共享嵌入和交叉头注意力增强处理组与对照组的关联建模，RA-IPS通过对抗优化不确定性集内的倾向权重来缓解未观测变量导致的偏差。实验表明CHAUN在QINI指标上最高提升25.6%，RA-IPS在未观测混杂下比标准IPS提升5.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升建模面临两个核心挑战：一是灵活利用组间相似性以增强个体处理效应（ITE）的判别能力；二是在存在未观测混杂因素的情况下进行去偏处理。

Method: 1) CHAUN：采用共享特征嵌入和交叉头注意力机制，动态整合处理组与对照组的特定表示，增强跨组相关性建模。2) RA-IPS：在约束不确定性集内对抗优化倾向权重，以缓解未观测变量导致的偏差。理论上证明了已知真实倾向得分时，即使存在未观测混杂，ITE仍可识别。

Result: 在CRITEO-UPLIFT、LAZADA及生产电商数据集上，CHAUN相比现有最优模型在QINI指标上实现最高25.6%的相对提升；RA-IPS在未观测混杂下比标准IPS提升5.4%的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 提出的CHAUN和RA-IPS方法在真实因果推断任务中有效，能同时提升模型判别能力与对未观测混杂的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Uplift modeling, crucial for estimating individual treatment effects (ITE), faces dual challenges: flexibly leveraging inter-group similarity to enhance discriminative power and debiasing under unobserved confounding scenarios. In this paper, we propose the Cross-Head Attention Uplift Network (CHAUN) and Robust Adversarial Inverse Propensity Score (RA-IPS) method to address these limitations. CHAUN employs shared feature embeddings and cross-head attention mechanisms to dynamically integrate treatment-specific and control-specific representations, enhancing inter-group correlation modeling. Theoretically, we prove that access to the true propensity scores ensures ITE identifiability even with unobserved confounders. For practical scenarios lacking true propensity scores, RA-IPS adversarially optimizes propensity weights within constrained uncertainty sets to mitigate bias from unobserved variables. Experiments on public datasets (CRITEO-UPLIFT, LAZADA) and a production e-commerce dataset demonstrate CHAUN's superiority over state-of-the-art uplift models, achieving relative improvements of up to 25.6% in QINI scores. RA-IPS further enhances robustness, outperforming standard IPS by 5.4% under unobserved confounding. The results validate the effectiveness of our proposed methods in real-world causal inference tasks.

</details>


### [235] [Localizing RL-Induced Tool Use to a Single Crosscoder Feature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26474)
*Andrii Shportko,Shubham Bhokare,Ahmed Zeyad A Alzahrani,Bowen Cheng,Gustavo Mercier,Jessica Hullman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出专用特征交叉编码器（DFC）来分离强化学习（RL）微调引入的特定特征，这些特征主要介导工具调用能力。实验表明，DFC能够以紧凑的特征集编码RL能力，并通过重建提升RL模型的工具正确率31.1个百分点，同时将工具调用能力被动迁移至冻结的基础模型（能力溢出效应+6.8个百分点），从而实现无需重训练的行为控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL微调显著提升了语言模型的工具使用等智能体行为，但其内部表征变化的机制尚不明确，不清楚哪些特征在RL中涌现、哪些被保留，以及这些特征能否被利用以实现无需重训练的行为控制。

Method: 提出专用特征交叉编码器（DFC），将RL微调引入的特定特征与基础模型特征分离。通过在Qwen2.5-3B上进行48组超参数扫描，利用编码-解码重建机制分析RL特征。

Result: DFC重建使RL模型的工具正确率提升31.1±9.7个百分点；将工具调用能力被动迁移至冻结的基础模型，提升6.8±5.0个百分点（能力溢出效应）。

Conclusion: DFC通过分区策略将RL引入的能力集中到最小且可操控的特征集中，实现了对智能体LLM的运行时行为控制，无需重新训练。

Abstract: Fine-tuning through RL reshapes the internal representations of language models to enable agentic behaviors such as tool use, yet the mechanistic basis of these changes remains poorly understood. While RL substantially improves structured tool-call generation, it is unclear which features emerge, which are preserved, and whether identified features can be leveraged for retraining-free behavioral control. In this work, we show that $\textit{Dedicated Feature Crosscoders (DFC)}$ isolate a compact set of RL-specific features that mediate tool-calling capability in $\texttt{Qwen2.5-3B}$. Across a $48$-crosscoder hyperparameter sweep, encode-decode reconstruction improves the RL model's tool correctness by $+31.1 \pm {9.7}$ pp and passively transfers tool-calling ability to the frozen base model by $+6.8 \pm 5.0$ pp which we call a $\textit{capability spillover}$. Our findings show that DFC partitioning concentrates RL-introduced capability into a minimal, steerable feature set that enables runtime behavioral control of agentic LLMs.

</details>


### [236] [Kolmogorov Arnold networks (KAN) for aerodynamic prediction: a comparison with MLPs and GNNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27126)
*Miguel Jaraiz,Fermin Gutierrez,Pablo Yeste,Miguel Sánchez-Domínguez,Eusebio Valero,Gonzalo Rubio,Lucas Lacasa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了KAN在流体力学代理建模中的性能，发现KAN模型性能与MLP相当（略逊），GNN最佳但训练更耗时；KAN复杂度低、训练快，但存在训练不稳定和超参数敏感问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前学界对KAN是否能在经典领域（如符号回归、通用机器学习、NLP、计算机视觉）超越MLP存在争议，作者旨在评估KAN在流体动力学代理建模中的表现，并与MLP和GNN进行细致对比。

Method: 以预测亚音速和跨音速翼型表面压力分布为任务，对比KAN、MLP和GNN三种模型在流体动力学代理建模中的性能，包括预测精度、模型复杂度、训练速度和稳定性等维度。

Result: KAN在预测压力系数方面表现良好，能跨马赫数和攻角插值，但性能略逊于适当训练的MLP；GNN性能最佳但训练时间更长。KAN模型复杂度最低、训练最快，但存在训练不稳定性，且性能高度依赖超参数优化。

Conclusion: 在当前流体动力学代理建模任务中，KAN并未展现出对MLP的明显优势，其性能可比较但略逊于MLP，且存在训练不稳定等局限性。

Abstract: Kolmogorov Arnold networks (KAN) have recently been introduced as a (deep) neural network architecture whose trainable parameters adapt the activation functions, instead of the coefficients of the affine transformations at the core of traditional architectures such as deep multilayer perceptrons (MLPs). This architecture builds on the Kolmogorov-Arnold theorem, which endows it with universal approximation properties. While the advent of KANs has been received with excitement, there is a current debate about the possible KAN supremacy over deep multilayer perceptrons (MLPs) for classic fields such as symbolic regression, generic-purpose machine learning, natural language processing or computer vision. Here we assess the performance of KANs --and its nuanced comparison against MLPs and graph neural networks (GNNs)-- in the realm of fluid dynamics surrogate modelling. To that aim, we consider the task of predicting the surface pressure distribution over subsonic and transonic airfoils, a canonical task in aerodynamics. Our results show that KAN models show good performance in predicting the whole pressure coefficients and is able to interpolate across Mach numbers and angles of attack, however its performance is comparable --marginally inferior-- to a suitably trained MLP, where best performance is achieved by a GNN at the expense or requiring lengthier training. While the optimal KAN model have typically much lower complexity than MLP and GNN --hence resulting in faster training--, we find that KANs suffer from training instabilities, and their performance is highly dependent on a proper hyperparameter optimisation.

</details>


### [237] [Retrieval-Warmed Energy-Based Reasoning: A Five-Arm Ablation Methodology for Diffusion-as-Inference on Structured Reasoning Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26476)
*Libo Sun,Po-Wei Harn,Zewei Zhang,Peixiong He,Xiao Qin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出五臂消融方法（oracle, best-constant, per-query-random, shuffled, aligned），解耦检索预热扩散采样器中三类混杂效应（类先验偏移、随机预热、图对齐值复用），在连通性-2任务上发现图对齐贡献最大（+35pp），而Sudoku任务中失败瓶颈在键编码质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散采样器的检索预热加速了迭代推理，但增益来源（类先验偏移、随机预热、图对齐值复用）混杂不清，缺乏系统诊断方法。

Method: 提出五臂消融方法（oracle, best-constant, per-query-random, shuffled, aligned），从LLM-RAG评估中借鉴诊断分解策略，分别隔离三类效应，并在Erdős–Rényi全配对可达性（连通性-2）和Sudoku两个任务上验证。

Result: 在连通性-2上，aligned vs shuffled-oracle的平衡准确率差异达+35pp，而per-query随机初始化效果低于冷启动，说明图对齐贡献主导；Sudoku中失败瓶颈在键编码质量而非检索采样过程。

Conclusion: 五臂消融方法能准确定位检索预热扩散模型中的瓶颈组件，不同任务中主导效应不同，为结构化推理中的失败模式提供可解释性。

Abstract: Warm-started diffusion samplers accelerate iterative inference, but it is rarely clear which part of the pipeline carries the gain. We study \textbf{retrieval-warmed energy-based reasoning (RW-EBR)} -- an IRED energy-based diffusion model \cite{du2024ired} augmented with a Modern Hopfield trajectory memory -- and contribute a \textbf{five-arm ablation methodology} (oracle, best-constant, per-query-random, shuffled, aligned) that separates three confounded effects: class-prior bias shift, stochastic warm-starting, and graph-aligned value reuse. The diagnostic decomposition is adapted from LLM-RAG evaluation \cite{ru2024ragchecker}. On \textbf{connectivity-2} (Erdős--Rényi all-pairs reachability), the aligned-vs-shuffled-oracle swing reaches \textbf{$+35$\,pp} balanced accuracy on a fixed 1{,}000-graph validation-set diagnostic, with value distribution and retrieval mechanics fixed, only per-graph alignment destroyed, while per-query random initialisation falls below cold -- per-graph alignment, not bias shift or stochasticity, dominates. Yet the \emph{deployable} cold-prediction pipeline misses the acceptance gate at stored-value quality. The same diagnostic logic, stopped at the key-quality screen, applied to \textbf{Sudoku} with a task-specific key encoder produces a clean negative at a \emph{different} component -- key quality, under the current setup. The decomposition names the first blocking component on each task. The setting -- graph reachability refined by an iterative diffusion sampler, with explainability of failure modes as the lens -- places the work within structured and spatio-temporal reasoning.

</details>


### [238] [fTNN: a tensor neural network for fractional PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27140)
*Qingkui Ma,Hehu Xie,Xiaobo Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出fTNN——一种确定性张量神经网络子空间方法，用于求解有界域上的分数阶拉普拉斯问题（分数阶泊松方程和含时分数阶对流扩散方程）。该方法采用几何自适应积分分裂、边界奇异性增强试验函数和时空可分离神经网络架构，在基准测试中相比fPINN和蒙特卡洛方法实现了更高的精度，尤其适用于强边界奇异性和长时间模拟问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分数阶神经网络方法（如fPINN）在处理分数阶拉普拉斯算子时面临积分奇异性和低正则性解带来的精度瓶颈，且蒙特卡洛方法效率低下；亟需一种高精度、确定性且能有效处理边界奇异性的数值框架。

Method: (1) 几何自适应积分分裂：根据空间依赖的近场半径将分数阶拉普拉斯分解为奇异近场、规则中场和解析远场三部分；(2) 确定性求积：近场用Gauss-Jacobi求积处理径向奇异性，中场用Gauss求积，角向用确定性角度求积；(3) 边界奇异性感知的试验函数：显式嵌入边界特征，自动选择主导指数和损失函数；(4) 时空可分离神经网络：将时空残差分解为低维时间积分与空间积分之和，结合交替子空间优化策略进行训练。

Result: 在多个基准测试中，fTNN相比fPINN和蒙特卡洛方法显著提高了精度，尤其对于具有强边界奇异性的问题和长时间模拟场景，展现了优异的数值性能。

Conclusion: fTNN为分数阶拉普拉斯问题提供了一种高效、高精度的确定性神经网络求解框架，有效克服了传统方法在边界奇异性和长时间积分上的局限性。

Abstract: We develop the fTNN, a deterministic tensor neural network subspace method for problems involving the fractional Laplacian on bounded domains, taking the fractional Poisson equation and time-dependent fractional advection-diffusion equation as typical representatives. The work employs a geometry-adapted integration split featuring a spatially dependent near-field radius, which decomposes the fractional Laplacian into three contributions: a singular near field, a regular interior far field, and an analytical exterior far field. Then the singular radial integrals are treated by Gauss-Jacobi quadrature, the regular radial integrals by Gauss quadrature, and the angular variables by deterministic angular quadrature, yielding a fully deterministic integration framework of the fractional Laplacian operator. To accurately resolve low-regularity solutions and the associated loss functional, we construct boundary-singularity-aware trial functions enriched with explicit boundary features, and propose two strategies for automatically selecting the leading exponent and evaluating the loss function from the singularity structure induced by the fractional operator, or jointly by the fractional operator and the source term. For time-dependent fractional PDEs, we design a spatiotemporally separable neural network that factorizes the time-space residual into a sum of low-dimensional temporal and spatial integrals, and we integrate this representation with an alternating neural network subspace optimization strategy for efficient training. Numerical experiments show that the proposed framework attains high accuracy on the tested benchmarks and improves substantially over existing fPINN and Monte Carlo baselines, particularly for problems with strong boundary singularities and long-time simulations.

</details>


### [239] [Stochastic Gradient Optimization with Model-Assisted Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27171)
*Jonne Pohjankukka,Jukka Heikkonen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于模型辅助抽样的梯度估计框架，将小批量梯度下降中的方差问题通过调查抽样理论重新诠释，利用辅助梯度预测模型构建更高效的梯度估计器，在71-86%的实验中取得性能提升，尤其在动量优化器（如AdamW）下能以约一半训练轮次达到更优泛化效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习中的小批量随机梯度下降存在梯度噪声，影响收敛稳定性、速度和泛化性能。现有方法（如方差缩减技术SVRG/SAG、自适应优化器）虽能缓解但可能引入额外计算开销。本文旨在弥合机器学习优化与调查抽样理论之间的鸿沟，利用抽样理论中的模型辅助方法优化梯度估计。

Method: 提出模型辅助抽样框架：将数据集视为固定有限总体，梯度视为基于样本的估计量；引入辅助梯度预测模型构建更高效的梯度估计器，均匀抽样作为无辅助信息时的特例；该框架可无缝集成到现有优化器中，不改变其动力学特性。

Result: 在合成数据和六个基准数据集上，71-86%的实验取得性能提升，尤其适用于中等规模输入空间；与AdamW等动量优化器结合时，所提估计器能在约一半训练轮次内实现更好的泛化性能。

Conclusion: 模型辅助抽样框架有效降低了梯度估计方差，提升了优化效率，且易于与现有优化器集成，在较少训练轮次下即可获得更优泛化，为机器学习优化与调查抽样理论的交叉提供了新视角。

Abstract: This work addresses the problem of variance in stochastic gradient estimation for machine learning optimization. Deep learning relies on mini-batch methods such as stochastic gradient descent, which approximate full gradients but introduce noise, creating trade-offs between convergence stability, speed, and generalization. Existing methods, including variance reduction techniques (e.g., SVRG and SAG) and adaptive optimizers, aim to mitigate gradient noise but may introduce additional computational overhead. We propose a model-assisted sampling framework that interprets mini-batch gradients through survey sampling theory, treating the dataset as a fixed finite population and gradients as sample-based estimates. Our aim is to bridge machine learning optimization and survey sampling theory by combining their perspectives on sample-based estimation and variance reduction. By incorporating auxiliary gradient-prediction models, we construct more efficient gradient estimators, with uniform sampling arising as a special case when no auxiliary information is used. Our approach integrates easily with existing optimizers, improving efficiency without altering their dynamics. Empirical results on synthetic and six benchmark datasets show performance gains in 71-86% of the experiments, particularly for medium-sized input spaces in our benchmarks. Notably, with momentum-based optimizers such as AdamW, the proposed estimator achieves clearly better generalization in roughly half the training epochs compared to baseline estimator.

</details>


### [240] [RecallRisk-BERT: A Multi-Task Framework for Post-Report Medical Device Recall Triage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27174)
*Ali Semih Atalay,Sevgi Yigit-Sert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RecallRisk-BERT，一种基于PubMedBERT与结构化特征嵌入的多任务模型，用于FDA医疗器械召回事件后自动分类召回严重等级（I/II/III）与根本原因类别（9类）。在54,165条记录上，LightGBM单任务严重性预测达0.963准确率；多任务设置下RecallRisk-BERT显著优于单任务PubMedBERT基线，且模型风险排序与真实根本原因严重性模式高度一致（rho=0.983）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗器械召回记录的快速增长给事后召回分类、严重性评估和根本原因解读带来挑战，而现有研究多分别处理召回发生预测或根本原因分析，缺乏对召回严重性与根本原因类别的联合建模。

Method: 使用openFDA中54,165条医疗器械召回记录（2002-2025.10），先评估经典ML与Boosting模型进行单任务预测；再开发RecallRisk-BERT多任务模型，融合PubMedBERT文本表征与产品代码、法规编号、医学专业等结构化类别特征的嵌入表示，同时预测召回严重性（I/II/III）与根本原因类别（9类）。

Result: 单任务严重性预测中，LightGBM文本-表格配置达到最优（准确率0.963，宏F1 0.856，ROC-AUC 0.974）。多任务设置下RecallRisk-BERT显著优于单任务PubMedBERT基线。模型风险排序与真实根本原因严重性模式高度一致（Spearman rho=0.983, p=1.936e-6）。

Conclusion: 文本-表格联合学习可支持可扩展的事后召回分类、监管决策支持及基于模型的根本原因风险分析。

Abstract: Medical device recalls are a critical regulatory mechanism for protecting patient safety. The growing volume of FDA recall records presents challenges in post-report recall triage, severity assessment, and root-cause interpretation. Existing studies mostly address recall occurrence prediction or root-cause analysis separately, while joint modeling of recall severity and root-cause categories has received limited attention. We develop an automated recall triage framework using 54,165 FDA medical device recall records from openFDA, covering the period from 2002 to October 2025. We first evaluate classical machine learning and boosting-based models for recall severity and root-cause category prediction. We then develop RecallRisk-BERT, a multi-task model that combines PubMedBERT-based textual representations of recall narratives with embedding-based representations of structured categorical features, including product code, regulation number, and medical specialty. The model simultaneously predicts recall severity (Class I/II/III) and a consolidated root-cause category (9 classes). Performance was evaluated using accuracy, macro-averaged precision, recall, F1-score, and ROC-AUC. In single-task severity prediction, our LightGBM-based text--tabular configuration achieved the strongest performance, with an accuracy of 0.963, macro-F1 of 0.856, and ROC-AUC of 0.974. In the multi-task setting, RecallRisk-BERT substantially outperformed the single-task PubMedBERT baseline. Model-derived risk rankings were strongly consistent with observed root-cause severity patterns (rho = 0.983, p = 1.936e-6). These findings indicate that text--tabular learning can support scalable post-report recall triage, regulatory decision support, and model-based root-cause risk analysis.

</details>


### [241] [Automating Potential-based Reward Shaping with Vision Language Model Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27180)
*Henrik Müller,Daniel Kudenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出VLM-PBRS框架，利用轻量级视觉语言模型（VLM）的偏好反馈学习势能函数，用于基于势能的奖励塑造（PBRS），在保证最优策略集不变的前提下，提升强化学习在稀疏奖励环境中的样本效率，并防止奖励破解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏奖励环境下智能体缺乏中间反馈，难以探索和归因；传统奖励塑造可能导致奖励破解；PBRS虽保证最优策略不变但依赖专家设计的势能函数。因此需要一种自动化、低成本的势能函数学习方法。

Method: 使用轻量级VLM对图像对进行偏好比较，利用偏好标签训练势能函数模型，将其集成到PBRS框架中，对原始奖励进行势能差分奖励塑造，从而在保持最优策略集的前提下加速学习。

Result: 在Meta-World和Franka Kitchen环境中，VLM-PBRS显著提升了样本效率，并展现出对奖励破解的鲁棒性。实验结果还揭示了VLM偏好标签准确率与样本效率提升之间的正相关关系。

Conclusion: VLM-PBRS是首个将VLM偏好学习用于PBRS势能函数合成的工作，提供了一种原则性且低成本的解决方案，证明了小规模VLM也能有效加速强化学习，为稀疏奖励场景下的奖励塑造提供了新范式。

Abstract: Sparse rewards are inherently challenging for reinforcement learning agents as they lack intermediate feedback to guide exploration and to correctly attribute the sparse success rewards to relevant parts of the trajectory. Naive reward shaping can induce reward hacking, yielding policies that exploit auxiliary signals instead of solving the intended task. Potential-based reward shaping (PBRS) guarantees preservation of the optimal policy set, but requires the definition of a heuristic potential function over the state space. In this work, we introduce the VLM-guided PBRS framework VLM-PBRS that learns the potential function directly from vision language model (VLM) feedback. We query a lightweight VLM to obtain preferences over image pairs and train a model of the potential function using these preferences. As this approach is based on potential-based reward shaping, it preserves the original optimal policies, and removes the need for expert-designed reward shaping terms. Because large VLMs are prohibitively expensive to invoke repeatedly during policy learning, we employ smaller, more computationally efficient VLMs. Although the resulting preference labels are less accurate, empirical evidence shows that the preference labels can still be used to accelerate learning. We validate our method empirically in the Meta-World and Franka Kitchen environments and highlight the connection between VLM preference label accuracy and sample efficiency improvements. Our contributions are threefold: (1) the first application of VLM preference-based learning to synthesize a potential function for PBRS, (2) a principled, low-cost solution that leverages small VLMs, and (3) extensive empirical demonstration of improved sample efficiency and robustness to reward hacking.

</details>


### [242] [Explaining Temporal Graph Neural Networks via Feature-induced Information Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27201)
*Ping Xiong,Thomas Schnake,Klaus-Robert Müller,Shinichi Nakajima*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于归一化相关测度（NRM）框架的事件时序图神经网络（ETGNN）完整信息流归因方法，通过模块化解构覆盖事件诱导变量路径，在合成与真实数据集上优于现有解释方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ETGNN解释方法仅关注事件相关嵌入到输出的部分信息流，忽略了通过事件诱导变量（event-induced variables）的关键路径，而这些变量在捕获长程时序依赖中起核心作用。

Method: 基于NRM框架，扩展以量化事件嵌入和事件诱导变量的完整信息流，确保跨层潜在变量可比性，支持事件间高阶交互分析；引入模块化解构程序应对ETGNN架构复杂性，系统构建相关性结构。

Result: 在流行病追踪、社会动力学两个合成数据集以及政治事件网络真实数据集上，定性与定量实验均一致优于现有解释方法，生成更可解释的解释。

Conclusion: 所提方法通过覆盖完整信息流路径，有效克服了现有ETGNN解释方法的局限性，在解释质量和可解释性上均取得显著提升。

Abstract: Event-based Temporal Graph Neural Networks (ETGNNs) have demonstrated strong performance across a wide range of applications, including social network analysis, epidemic tracing, recommender systems, and political event forecasting. However, their increasing complexity poses significant challenges for explainability. Existing explanation methods focus only on a subset of the information flow within ETGNNs, typically tracing contributions from the event-related embeddings to the output. Consequently, they overlook the important pathways through event-induced variables, which mediate interactions between nodes and thereby play a central role in capturing long-range temporal dependencies. To overcome this limitation, we propose a novel attribution method that analyzes the \emph{entire} information flow through all event-associated variables. Our method is built upon the recent Normalized Relevance Measure (NRM) framework, which enables explicit quantification of information flow originating from event embeddings as well as information flow passing through event-induced variables. It also ensures comparability of latent variables across layers, and supports higher-order analysis of interactions between events. To handle the architectural complexity of ETGNNs, we extend the NRM framework with a modular decomposition procedure that facilitates the systematic construction of relevance structure for complex neural architectures. We evaluate our approach on two synthetic datasets for epidemic tracing and social dynamics, as well as a real-world dataset of political event networks. Our qualitative and quantitative experiments show that our method consistently outperforms existing explanation approaches while producing more human-interpretable explanations.

</details>


### [243] [Reinforcement Learning without Ground-Truth Solutions can Improve LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27369)
*Yingyu Lin,Qiyue Gao,Nikki Lijing Kuang,Xunpeng Huang,Kun Zhou,Tongtong Liang,Zhewei Yao,Yi-An Ma,Yuxiong He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RiVER框架，通过排序诱导的校准奖励塑造方法，使LLM能够在没有真实解的情况下从基于分数的优化任务中学习，同时提升在精确解基准上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的RLVR方法依赖真实答案分配奖励，限制了其在真实解未知的任务中的应用。作者希望探索在没有真实解的情况下，仅使用执行得分的连续反馈来训练LLM。

Method: 提出RiVER框架，针对连续奖励中出现的"规模主导"（分数幅度未校准导致策略更新失真）和"频率主导"（重复采样次优解掩盖稀有强解）两大挑战，设计校准奖励塑造方法：通过实例间比较进行归一化，并强调排名靠前的求解器，同时为其他有效解保留有界反馈。

Result: 在12个AtCoder启发式竞赛任务上训练，在ALE-Bench上Qwen3-8B提升8.9%、GLM-Z1-9B-0414提升9.4%的排名；在LiveCodeBench和USACO上分别平均提升2.4%和3.5%。

Conclusion: 基于分数的优化任务结合适当的奖励校准，可以在没有真实解的情况下作为通用编码能力的有效训练环境，而直接使用原始执行分数的基线方法虽能提升ALE评分但无法迁移到精确解基准。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) for training LLMs typically rely on ground-truth answers to assign rewards, limiting their applicability to tasks where the ground-truth solution is unknown. We introduce a \textbf{R}anking-\textbf{i}nduced \textbf{VER}ifiable framework (RiVER) that trains LLMs on score-based optimization tasks without ground-truth solutions, using deterministic execution feedback as continuous-valued supervision. When applying group-relative RL to such continuous rewards, we identify two key challenges: \emph{scale dominance}, where uncalibrated score magnitudes across test instances distort policy updates, and \emph{frequency dominance}, where repeatedly sampled suboptimal solutions can outweigh rare but stronger candidates. RiVER addresses these challenges with calibrated reward shaping that uses instance-wise comparisons and emphasizes top-ranked solvers while retaining bounded feedback for other valid solutions. We train on 12 AtCoder Heuristic Contest tasks and evaluate on Algorithm Engineering Benchmark (ALE-Bench), LiveCodeBench, and USACO. RiVER advances Qwen3-8B and GLM-Z1-9B-0414 by 8.9\% and 9.4\% in ALE rating rank. More importantly, despite training exclusively on score-based tasks without any ground-truth solutions, RiVER also improves the backbones across exact-solution benchmarks such as LiveCodeBench and USACO by an absolute average improvement of 2.4\% and 3.5\%. By contrast, baselines trained with raw execution scores improve ALE rating but fail to transfer to exact-solution benchmarks. These results suggest that score-based optimization tasks, combined with proper reward calibration, can serve as effective training environments for general coding ability without ground-truth solutions.

</details>
